- b) In the case of other property, when they have acquired it with knowledge that in this way its confiscation was being hindered or when a diligent person would have had reason to suspect, in the circumstances of the case, that in that way its confiscation was being hindered.
  - 2. It shall be presumed, unless proven otherwise, that the third party has known or has had reason to suspect that it was property deriving from illicit activity or that it was transferred to avoid its confiscation, when the property or effects had been transferred to him or her free of charge or for a price lower than the actual market price.

###### Article 127 quinquies.

1. Judges and courts may also order the confiscation of property, effects and proceeds deriving from the prior criminal activity of the convicted person, when the following requirements are cumulatively met:

- a) That the subject is or has been convicted of any of the offences referred to in

###### Article 127 bis.1 of the Criminal Code.

b) That the offence has been committed in the context of a prior continued criminal activity.

c) That there are well-founded indications that a relevant part of the convicted person's assets derives from prior criminal activity.

Relevant indications are:

1.º The disproportion between the value of the property and effects in question and the lawful income of the convicted person.

2.º The concealment of ownership or of any power of disposal over the property or effects through the use of interposed natural or legal persons or entities without legal personality, or tax havens or territories with zero taxation that conceal or hinder the determination of the true ownership of the property.

3.º The transfer of the property or effects by means of transactions that hinder or prevent their location or destination and that lack valid legal or economic justification.

The provisions of the preceding paragraph shall only apply when there are well-founded indications that the subject has obtained, from his or her criminal activity, a benefit exceeding 6,000 euros.

2. For the purposes of the preceding paragraph, it shall be understood that the offence has been committed in the context of a continued criminal activity whenever:

- a) The subject is convicted or has been convicted in the same proceedings for three or more offences from which the obtaining of a direct or indirect economic benefit has been derived, or for a continued offence that includes at least three criminal infractions from which a direct or indirect economic benefit has been derived.
- b) Or in the period of six years prior to the moment when the proceedings were initiated in which he or she has been convicted of any of the offences referred to in Article 127 bis of the Criminal Code, he or she had been convicted of two or more offences from which the obtaining of an economic benefit had been derived, or of a continued offence that includes at least two criminal infractions from which the obtaining of an economic benefit has been derived.

###### Article 127 sexies.

For the purposes of the provisions of the preceding article, the following presumptions shall apply:

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

1.º It shall be presumed that all assets acquired by the convicted person within the period of time beginning six years before the date of opening of the criminal proceedings, derive from their criminal activity.

For these purposes, it is understood that the assets have been acquired on the earliest date on which it is established that the subject has had them at their disposal.

2.º It shall be presumed that all expenses incurred by the convicted person during the period of time referred to in the first paragraph of the preceding number, were paid with funds deriving from their criminal activity.

3.º It shall be presumed that all assets referred to in number 1 were acquired free of encumbrances.

The judge or court may decide that the foregoing presumptions shall not be applied in relation to certain assets, effects or gains, when, in the specific circumstances of the case, they prove to be incorrect or disproportionate.

###### Article 127 septies.

If the execution of the confiscation could not be carried out, in whole or in part, due to the nature or situation of the assets, effects or gains in question, or for any other circumstance, the judge or court may, by means of an order, decide the confiscation of other assets, even of lawful origin, belonging to those criminally responsible for the act for a value equivalent to that of the unexecuted part of the confiscation initially decided.

The same procedure shall be followed when the confiscation of specific assets, effects or gains is decided, but their value is lower than that which they had at the time of their acquisition.

###### Article 127 octies.

1. In order to guarantee the effectiveness of confiscation, the assets, means, instruments and gains may be seized or attached and placed in custody by the judicial authority from the moment of the first proceedings.

2. It shall be for the judge or court to decide, in accordance with the provisions of the Code of Criminal Procedure, on the early disposal or provisional use of the assets and effects seized.

3. The assets, instruments and gains confiscated by final decision, unless they must be allocated to the payment of compensation to victims, shall be awarded to the State, which shall give them the destination that is provided legally or by regulation.

###### Article 128.

When the said effects and instruments are of lawful trade and their value is not proportionate to the nature or seriousness of the criminal offence, or when civil liabilities have been fully satisfied, the Judge or Court may not decree the confiscation, or decree it partially.

###### Article 129.

1. In the case of offences committed within, with the collaboration of, through or by means of companies, organisations, groups or any other kind of entities or associations of persons that, for lacking legal personality, are not covered by article 31 bis, the judge or court may impose with reasons on such companies, organisations, groups, entities or associations one or more ancillary consequences to the penalty corresponding to the perpetrator of the offence, with the content provided for in letters c) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33. It may also decide the definitive prohibition to carry out any activity, even if lawful.

2. The ancillary consequences referred to in the preceding paragraph may only be applied to the companies, organisations, groups or entities or associations mentioned therein when this Code expressly provides for it, or when it concerns one of the offences for which it allows criminal liability to be required of legal persons.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

3. The temporary closure of premises or establishments, the suspension of corporate activities and judicial intervention may also be decided by the Examining Judge as a precautionary measure during the investigation of the case for the purposes established in this article and with the limits indicated in article 33.7.

###### Article 129 bis.

If it concerns persons convicted of the commission of a serious offence against life, the physical integrity of persons, liberty, sexual freedom or indemnity, terrorism, or any other serious offence that entails a serious risk to the life, health or physical integrity of persons, when from the circumstances of the act, background, assessment of their personality, or other available information it can be assessed that there exists a relevant danger of criminal recidivism, the judge or court may decide the taking of biological samples from their person and the carrying out of analyses to obtain DNA identifiers and their registration in the police database. Only the analyses necessary to obtain the identifiers that provide, exclusively, genetic information revealing the identity of the person and their sex may be carried out. If the affected person opposes the collection of the samples, their forced execution may be imposed by resorting to the minimum coercive measures indispensable for their execution, which must in all cases be proportionate to the circumstances of the case and respectful of their dignity.

#### TITLE VII

On the extinction of criminal liability and its effects

##### CHAPTER I

On the causes that extinguish criminal liability

###### Article 130.

1. Criminal liability is extinguished:

1.º By the death of the offender.

2.º By the fulfilment of the sentence.

3.º By the definitive remission of the penalty, in accordance with the provisions of paragraphs 1 and 2 of article 87.

4.º By pardon.

5.º By the forgiveness of the offended person, when it concerns minor offences prosecutable at the instance of the aggrieved person or the law so provides. The forgiveness must be granted expressly before a judgment has been rendered, for which purpose the sentencing judicial authority must hear the person offended by the offence before rendering it.

In offences committed against minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection that affect eminently personal legal interests, the forgiveness of the offended person does not extinguish criminal liability. 6.º By the prescription of the offence.

7.º By the prescription of the penalty or of the security measure.

2. The transformation, merger, absorption or spin-off of a legal person does not extinguish its criminal liability, which shall be transferred to the entity or entities into which it is transformed, merged or absorbed and shall extend to the entity or entities resulting from the spin-off. The Judge or Court may moderate the transfer of the penalty to the legal person according to the proportion that the legal person originally responsible for the offence bears with it.

The concealed or merely apparent dissolution of the legal person does not extinguish criminal liability. It shall be considered in all cases that there exists concealed or merely apparent dissolution of the legal person when its economic activity is continued and the substantial identity of clients, suppliers and employees, or of the most relevant part of all of them, is maintained.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Artículo 131.

1. Offences shall prescribe:

After twenty years, when the maximum penalty assigned to the offence is imprisonment of fifteen or more years.

After fifteen, when the maximum penalty assigned by law is disqualification for more than ten years, or imprisonment for more than ten and less than fifteen years.

After ten, when the maximum penalty assigned by law is imprisonment or disqualification for more than five years and not exceeding ten.

After five, all other offences, except minor offences and the offences of insults and slander, which prescribe after one year.

2. When the penalty assigned by law is compound, the rule contained in this article requiring the longest time for prescription shall apply.

3. Crimes against humanity and genocide and crimes against persons and property protected in case of armed conflict, except those punished in article 614, shall not prescribe in any case.

Neither shall terrorist offences prescribe, if they have caused the death of a person.

4. In cases of concurrence of offences or connected offences, the prescription period shall be that which corresponds to the most serious offence.

###### Artículo 132.

1. The periods provided for in the preceding article shall be computed from the day on which the punishable offence was committed. In cases of continued offence, permanent offence, as well as in offences that require habituality, such periods shall be computed, respectively, from the day on which the last offence was committed, from when the unlawful situation was eliminated or from when the conduct ceased.

In offences of non-consensual abortion, injuries, against freedom, of torture and against moral integrity, against privacy, the right to one's own image and the inviolability of the home, and against family relations, excluding the offences contemplated in the following paragraph, when the victim is a person under eighteen years of age, the periods shall be computed from the day on which the victim reached the age of majority, and if the victim died before reaching it, from the date of death.

In offences of attempted homicide, injuries under articles 149 and 150, in the offence of habitual mistreatment provided for in article 173.2, in offences against sexual freedom and in offences of trafficking in human beings, when the victim is a person under eighteen years of age, the periods shall be computed from when the victim reaches the age of thirty-five, and if the victim died before reaching that age, from the date of death.

2. Prescription shall be interrupted, rendering the elapsed time without effect, when the proceedings are directed against the person allegedly responsible for the offence, beginning to run again from when the proceedings are paralysed or terminated without conviction in accordance with the following rules:

1.ª Proceedings shall be understood to be directed against a specific person from the moment when, upon initiating the case or subsequently, a reasoned judicial decision is issued attributing to that person alleged participation in an act that may constitute an offence.

2.ª Notwithstanding the foregoing, the filing of a criminal complaint or the denunciation made before a judicial body, in which a specific person is attributed alleged participation in an act that may constitute an offence, shall suspend the computation of prescription for a maximum period of six months, to be counted from the same date of filing of the criminal complaint or making of the denunciation.

If within such period there is issued against the person subject to the criminal complaint or denunciation, or against any other person involved in the facts, any of the judicial decisions mentioned in rule 1.ª, the interruption of prescription shall be understood to be retroactively produced, for all purposes, on the date of filing of the criminal complaint or denunciation.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

On the contrary, the computation of the prescription period shall continue from the date of filing of the criminal complaint or denunciation if, within the six-month period, a final judicial decision is issued dismissing the criminal complaint or denunciation or whereby it is decided not to direct the proceedings against the person subject to the criminal complaint or denunciation. The continuation of the computation shall also occur if, within such period, the investigating judge does not adopt any of the decisions provided for in this article.

3. For the purposes of this article, the person against whom the proceedings are directed must be sufficiently determined in the judicial decision, either by direct identification or by means of data that allow such identification to be subsequently specified within the organisation or group of persons to whom the act is attributed.

4. In proceedings whose investigation has been assumed by the European Public Prosecutor's Office, prescription shall be interrupted:

- a) when the investigation is directed against a specific person, sufficiently identified, in the terms of the preceding paragraph, and this is reflected in a reasoned Decree.
- b) when a criminal complaint or denunciation is filed before the European Public Prosecutor's Office in which a specific person is attributed alleged participation in an act that may constitute an offence, rule 2.ª of paragraph 2 of this article being applicable.

###### Artículo 133.

1. Penalties imposed by final judgment shall prescribe:

After 30 years, those of imprisonment for more than 20 years.

After 25 years, those of imprisonment of 15 or more years without exceeding 20.

After 20, those of disqualification for more than 10 years and those of imprisonment for more than 10 and less than 15.

After 15, those of disqualification for more than six years and not exceeding 10, and those of imprisonment for more than five years and not exceeding 10.

After 10, the remaining serious penalties.

After five, less serious penalties.

After one year, minor penalties.

2. Penalties imposed for crimes against humanity and genocide and for crimes against persons and property protected in case of armed conflict, except those punished in article 614, shall not prescribe in any case.

Neither shall penalties imposed for terrorist offences prescribe, if these have caused the death of a person.

###### Artículo 134.

1. The time for prescription of the penalty shall be computed from the date of the final judgment, or from the breach of the sentence, if it had begun to be served.

2. The prescription period for the penalty shall be suspended:

- a) During the period of suspension of execution of the penalty.
- b) During the service of other penalties, when the provisions of

###### artículo 75.

###### Artículo 135.

1. Security measures shall prescribe after ten years, if they are custodial measures exceeding three years, and after five years if they are custodial measures equal to or less than three years or have other content.

2. The time for prescription shall be computed from the day on which the decision imposing the measure became final or, in case of successive service, from when it should have begun to be served.

3. If the service of a security measure is subsequent to that of a penalty, the period shall be computed from the extinction of the latter.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

##### CAPÍTULO II

De la cancelación de antecedentes delictivos

###### Artículo 136.

1. Those convicted who have discharged their criminal liability have the right to obtain from the Ministry of Justice, ex officio or at the request of a party, the cancellation of their criminal records, when the following periods have elapsed without having reoffended:

- a) Six months for minor penalties.
- b) Two years for penalties not exceeding twelve months and those imposed for negligent offenses.
- c) Three years for the remaining less serious penalties of less than three years.
- d) Five years for the remaining less serious penalties equal to or greater than three years.
- e) Ten years for serious penalties.
  - 2. The periods referred to in the preceding paragraph shall be counted from the day following that on which the penalty was discharged, but if this occurred through conditional remission, the period, once final remission has been obtained, shall be calculated by backdating it to the day following that on which the penalty would have been served if this benefit had not been enjoyed. In this case, the initial date for calculating the duration of the penalty shall be the day following the granting of the suspension.
  - 3. Penalties imposed on legal persons and the accessory consequences of

###### article 129

shall be cancelled within the corresponding period, in accordance with the rule provided in paragraph 1 of this article, unless dissolution or definitive prohibition of activities has been agreed. In these cases, the entries shall be cancelled after fifty years computed from the day following the finality of the judgment.

4. Entries of criminal records in the various sections of the Central Registry of Convicts and Rebels shall not be public. During their validity, certifications shall only be issued with the limitations and guarantees provided in their specific regulations and in the cases established by law. In any case, those requested by judges or courts shall be issued, whether or not they refer to cancelled entries, expressly stating this latter circumstance.

5. In cases where, despite the requirements established in this article for cancellation being met, it has not occurred, the judge or court, once such circumstances are proven, shall not take into account said records.

###### Artículo 137.

The entries of security measures imposed in accordance with the provisions of this Code or other criminal laws shall be cancelled once the respective measure has been served or prescribed; meanwhile, they shall only appear in certifications issued by the Registry to Judges or Courts or administrative authorities, in the cases established by Law.

## BOOK II

Offenses and their penalties

#### TITLE I

On homicide and its forms

###### Artículo 138.

1. Whoever kills another shall be punished, as guilty of homicide, with a prison sentence of ten to fifteen years.

2. The acts shall be punished with the penalty raised by one degree in the following cases:

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

a) when any of the circumstances of paragraph 1 of

###### article 140 occurs in its commission, or

b) when the acts are additionally constitutive of an offense of attack under article

###### 550. Artículo 139.

1. Whoever kills another when any of the following circumstances are present shall be punished with a prison sentence of fifteen to twenty-five years, as guilty of murder: 1.ª With treachery.

2.ª For price, reward or promise.

3.ª With cruelty, deliberately and inhumanly increasing the pain of the victim. 4.ª To facilitate the commission of another offense or to prevent its discovery.

2. When more than one of the circumstances provided in the preceding paragraph are present in a murder, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half.

###### Artículo 140.

1. Murder shall be punished with reviewable permanent imprisonment when any of the following circumstances are present:

1.ª That the victim is under sixteen years of age, or is an especially vulnerable person by reason of age, illness or disability.

2.ª That the act followed an offense against sexual freedom that the perpetrator had committed against the victim.

3.ª That the offense was committed by someone belonging to a criminal group or organization.

2. A person guilty of murder who has been convicted for the death of more than two persons shall be imposed a reviewable permanent imprisonment sentence. In this case, the provisions of letter b) of paragraph 1 of article 78 bis and letter b) of paragraph 2 of the same article shall apply.

###### Artículo 140 bis.

1. Persons convicted for the commission of one or more offenses included in this title may also be imposed a supervised release measure.

2. If the victim and the perpetrator of the offenses provided for in the three preceding articles had a son or daughter in common, the judicial authority shall impose, with respect to the latter, the penalty of deprivation of parental authority.

The same penalty shall be imposed when the victim was a son or daughter of the perpetrator, with respect to other sons and daughters, if they exist.

###### Artículo 141.

Provocation, conspiracy and proposition to commit the offenses provided for in the three preceding articles shall be punished with the penalty lowered by one or two degrees from that indicated in the preceding articles, as the case may be.

###### Artículo 142.

1. Whoever causes the death of another through gross negligence shall be punished, as guilty of negligent homicide, with a prison sentence of one to four years.

If negligent homicide was committed using a motor vehicle or moped, the penalty of deprivation of the right to drive motor vehicles and mopeds for one to six years shall also be imposed. For the purposes of this paragraph, driving in which the concurrence of any of the circumstances provided in article 379 determined the occurrence of the act shall in any case be deemed gross negligence.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

If negligent homicide was committed using a firearm, the penalty of deprivation of the right to carry or possess weapons for a period of one to six years shall also be imposed.

If the homicide was committed through professional negligence, the penalty of special disqualification for the exercise of the profession, trade or position for a period of three to six years shall also be imposed.

2. Whoever causes the death of another through less serious negligence shall be punished with a fine of three months to eighteen months.

If the homicide was committed using a motor vehicle or moped, the penalty of deprivation of the right to drive motor vehicles and mopeds for three to eighteen months shall also be imposed. In any case, negligence not classified as gross in which the commission of any of the serious infractions of traffic rules, motor vehicle circulation and road safety was determinant for the occurrence of the act shall be deemed less serious negligence. The assessment of whether or not the determination exists must be appreciated in a reasoned resolution.

If the homicide was committed using a firearm, the penalty of deprivation of the right to carry or possess weapons for a period of three to eighteen months may also be imposed.

Except in cases where it occurs using a motor vehicle or moped, the offense provided in this paragraph shall only be prosecutable by complaint of the aggrieved person or their legal representative.

###### Article 142 bis.

In the cases provided for in number 1 of the preceding article, the Judge or Court may impose, with reasoning, the penalty increased by one degree, to the extent it deems appropriate, if the act were of notorious gravity, in view of the singular nature and relevance of the risk created and the normative duty of care infringed, and had caused the death of two or more persons or the death of one and injuries constituting an offense under article 152.1.2.º or 3.º in the others, and by two degrees if the number of deceased were very high.

###### Article 143.

1. Whoever induces another to commit suicide shall be punished with a prison sentence of four to eight years.

2. A prison sentence of two to five years shall be imposed on whoever cooperates with acts necessary for the suicide of a person.

3. A prison sentence of six to ten years shall be imposed if the cooperation reached the point of executing the death.

4. Whoever causes or actively cooperates with necessary and direct acts leading to the death of a person suffering from severe, chronic and disabling affliction or a serious and incurable illness, with constant and unbearable physical or psychological suffering, at the express, serious and unequivocal request of that person, shall be punished with the penalty reduced by one or two degrees from those indicated in paragraphs 2 and 3.

5. Notwithstanding the provisions of the preceding paragraph, whoever causes or actively cooperates in the death of another person in compliance with the provisions of the organic law regulating euthanasia shall not incur criminal liability.

###### Article 143 bis.

The distribution or public dissemination through the Internet, telephone or any other information or communication technology of content specifically intended to promote, encourage or incite the suicide of minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to four years.

The judicial authorities shall order the adoption of the necessary measures for the removal of the content referred to in the preceding paragraph, for the interruption of services that predominantly offer said content or for the blocking of both when they are located abroad.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

#### TITLE II

On abortion

###### Article 144.

Whoever causes the abortion of a woman without her consent shall be punished with a prison sentence of four to eight years and special disqualification from exercising any health profession, or from providing services of any kind in clinics, establishments or gynecological offices, public or private, for a period of three to ten years. The same penalties shall be imposed on whoever performs the abortion having obtained the woman's consent through violence, threat or deception.

###### Article 145.

1. Whoever causes the abortion of a woman, with her consent, outside the cases permitted by law shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to three years and special disqualification from exercising any health profession, or from providing services of any kind in clinics, establishments or gynecological offices, public or private, for a period of one to six years. The judge may impose the penalty in its upper half when the acts described in this paragraph are performed outside an accredited public or private center or establishment.

2. The woman who causes her own abortion or consents to another person causing it, outside the cases permitted by law, shall be punished with a fine of six to twenty-four months.

3. In all cases, the judge or court shall impose the penalties respectively provided for in this article in their upper half when the conduct is carried out from the twenty-second week of gestation onwards.

###### Article 145 bis.

1. Whoever, within the cases contemplated by law, performs an abortion shall be punished with a fine of six to twelve months and special disqualification from providing services of any kind in clinics, establishments or gynecological offices, public or private, for a period of six months to two years:

- a) Without the mandatory prior opinions;
- b) Outside an accredited public or private center or establishment. In this case, the judge may impose the penalty in its upper half.
  - 2. In all cases, the judge or court shall impose the penalties provided for in this article in their upper half when the abortion has been performed from the twenty-second week of gestation onwards.
  - 3. The pregnant woman shall not be penalized under this provision.

###### Article 146.

Whoever causes an abortion through gross negligence shall be punished with a prison sentence of three to five months or a fine of six to 10 months.

When the abortion is committed through professional negligence, the penalty of special disqualification from exercising the profession, trade or position for a period of one to three years shall also be imposed.

The pregnant woman shall not be penalized under this provision.

#### TITLE III

On injuries

###### Article 147.

1. Whoever, by any means or procedure, causes another an injury that impairs their bodily integrity or physical or mental health, shall be punished, as perpetrator of the

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

offense of injuries with a prison sentence of three months to three years or a fine of six to twelve months, provided that the injury objectively requires, in addition to initial medical care, medical or surgical treatment for its healing. Simple monitoring or medical follow-up of the course of the injury shall not be considered medical treatment.

2. Whoever, by any means or procedure, causes another an injury not included in the preceding paragraph, shall be punished with a fine of one to three months.

3. Whoever strikes or physically mistreats another without causing injury, shall be punished with a fine of one to two months.

4. The offenses provided for in the two preceding paragraphs shall only be prosecutable upon complaint by the aggrieved person or their legal representative.

###### Article 148.

The injuries provided for in paragraph 1 of the preceding article may be punished with a prison sentence of two to five years, considering the result caused or risk produced: 1.º If in the assault weapons, instruments, objects, means, methods or forms specifically dangerous to the life or physical or psychological health of the injured person have been used. 2.º If there has been cruelty or treachery.

3.º If the victim is under fourteen years of age or a person with a disability in need of special protection.

4.º If the victim is or has been the spouse, or a woman who is or has been linked to the perpetrator by an analogous relationship of affection, even without cohabitation.

5.º If the victim is a particularly vulnerable person who lives with the perpetrator.

###### Article 149.

1. Whoever causes another, by any means or procedure, the loss or uselessness of a principal organ or limb, or of a sense, impotence, sterility, a severe deformity, or a severe somatic or psychological illness, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six to 12 years.

2. Whoever causes another genital mutilation in any of its manifestations shall be punished with a prison sentence of six to 12 years. If the victim is a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection, the penalty of special disqualification from exercising parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, custody or foster care for a period of four to 10 years shall be applicable, if the judge deems it appropriate to the interest of the minor or person with a disability in need of special protection.

###### Article 150.

Whoever causes another the loss or uselessness of a non-principal organ or limb, or deformity, shall be punished with a prison sentence of three to six years.

###### Artículo 151.

Incitement, conspiracy and proposal to commit the crimes provided for in the preceding articles of this Title, shall be punished with a penalty one or two degrees lower than that of the corresponding crime.

###### Artículo 152.

1. Whoever by gross negligence causes any of the injuries provided for in the preceding articles shall be punished, with regard to the risk created and the result produced: 1.° With the penalty of imprisonment from three to six months or a fine from six to eighteen months, if it concerns the injuries of paragraph 1 of artículo 147.

2.° With the penalty of imprisonment from one to three years, if it concerns the injuries of artículo 149.

3.° With the penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years, if it concerns the injuries of

###### artículo 150.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

If the acts had been committed using a motor vehicle or a moped, the penalty of deprivation of the right to drive motor vehicles and mopeds from one to four years shall also be imposed. For the purposes of this paragraph, driving in which the concurrence of any of the circumstances provided for in artículo 379 determined the production of the act shall in any case be deemed gross negligence.

If the injuries had been caused using a firearm, the penalty of deprivation of the right to carry or possess weapons for a period of one to four years shall also be imposed. If the injuries had been committed by professional negligence, the penalty of special disqualification from the exercise of the profession, trade or office for a period of six months to four years shall also be imposed.

2. Whoever by less serious negligence causes any of the injuries referred to in artículo 147.1, shall be punished with a fine from one to two months, and if the injuries referred to in artículos 149 and 150 are caused, shall be punished with a fine from three months to twelve months.

If the acts had been committed using a motor vehicle or a moped, the penalty of deprivation of the right to drive motor vehicles and mopeds from three to eighteen months shall also be imposed. For the purposes of this paragraph, that not qualified as gross in which the commission of any of the serious violations of traffic, vehicle circulation and road safety regulations has been determinant for the production of the act shall in any case be deemed less serious negligence. The assessment of the existence or not of the determination must be appreciated in a reasoned resolution. If the injuries had been caused using a firearm, the penalty of deprivation of the right to carry or possess weapons for a period of three months to one year may also be imposed.

The crime provided for in this paragraph shall only be prosecutable by complaint of the aggrieved person or their legal representative.

###### Artículo 152 bis.

In the cases provided for in number 1 of the preceding article, the Judge or Court may impose with reasoning the penalty one degree higher, to the extent deemed appropriate, if the act were of notorious gravity, with regard to the singular nature and relevance of the risk created and the normative duty of care infringed, and had caused injuries constituting a crime under artículo 152.1.2.º or 3.º to a plurality of persons, and by two degrees if the number of injured persons were very high.

###### Artículo 153.

1. Whoever by any means or procedure causes to another psychological harm or a less serious injury of those provided for in paragraph 2 of artículo 147, or strikes or physically mistreats another without causing injury, when the offended party is or has been a spouse, or a woman who is or has been linked to him by an analogous relationship of affection even without cohabitation, or an especially vulnerable person who cohabits with the perpetrator, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year or community service from thirty-one to eighty days and, in any case, deprivation of the right to possess and carry weapons from one year and one day to three years, as well as, when the judge or court deems it appropriate to the interest of the minor or person with disability in need of special protection, disqualification from the exercise of parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, custody or foster care for up to five years.

2. If the victim of the crime provided for in the preceding paragraph were any of the persons referred to in artículo 173.2, except for the persons contemplated in the preceding paragraph of this article, the perpetrator shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from three months to one year or community service from thirty-one to eighty days and, in any case, deprivation of the right to possess and carry weapons from one year and one day to three years, as well as, when the Judge or Court deems it appropriate to the interest of the minor or person with disability in need of special protection, disqualification from the exercise of parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, custody or foster care from six months to three years.

3. The penalties provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 shall be imposed in their upper half when the crime is perpetrated in the presence of minors, or using weapons, or takes place in the

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

common domicile or in the domicile of the victim, or is carried out in violation of a penalty contemplated in artículo 48 of this Code or a precautionary or security measure of the same nature.

4. Notwithstanding the provisions of the preceding paragraphs, the Judge or Court, reasoning it in the judgment, with regard to the personal circumstances of the perpetrator and those concurrent in the commission of the act, may impose the penalty one degree lower.

###### Artículo 154.

Those who fight among themselves, assaulting each other in a tumultuous manner, and using means or instruments that endanger the life or integrity of persons, shall be punished for their participation in the fight with the penalty of imprisonment from three months to one year or a fine from six to 24 months.

###### Artículo 155.

In crimes of injury, if valid, free, spontaneous and expressly given consent of the offended party has intervened, the penalty one or two degrees lower shall be imposed. Consent given by a minor or a person with disability in need of special protection shall not be valid.

###### Artículo 156.

Notwithstanding the provisions of the preceding article, valid, free, conscious and expressly given consent exempts from criminal liability in cases of organ transplant performed in accordance with the provisions of the law, sterilizations and transsexual surgery performed by a physician, unless the consent has been obtained through fraud, or by means of payment or reward, or the grantor is a minor or absolutely lacks the capacity to give it, in which case that given by them or by their legal representatives shall not be valid.

###### Article 156 bis.

1. Those who in any manner promote, favor, facilitate, advertise or execute the trafficking of human organs shall be punished with a prison sentence of six to twelve years in the case of an organ from a living person and imprisonment of three to six years in the case of an organ from a deceased person.

For these purposes, trafficking of human organs shall mean:

- a) The illicit extraction or obtaining of organs from others. Such extraction or obtaining shall be illicit if it occurs under any of the following circumstances:

1.ª that it has been carried out without the free, informed and express consent of the living donor in the form and with the requirements legally provided for;

2.ª that it has been carried out without the necessary authorization required by law in the case of the deceased donor,

3.ª that, in exchange for the extraction or obtaining, for one's own benefit or that of another, a gift or remuneration of any kind is requested or received by the donor or a third party, directly or through an intermediary, or an offer or promise is accepted. The reimbursement of expenses or loss of income derived from the donation shall not be understood as a gift or remuneration.

b) The preparation, preservation, storage, transport, transfer, receipt, importation or exportation of illicitly extracted organs.

c) The use of illicitly extracted organs for the purpose of transplantation or for other purposes.

2. In the same manner shall be punished those who, for their own benefit or that of another:

- a) request or receive, directly or through an intermediary, a gift or remuneration of any kind, or accept an offer or promise for proposing or recruiting a donor or recipient of organs;
- b) offer or deliver, directly or through an intermediary, a gift or remuneration of any kind to medical personnel, public officials or private individuals in connection with CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION the exercise of their profession or position in clinics, establishments or offices, public or private, in order to carry out or facilitate the illicit extraction or obtaining or the implantation of illicitly extracted organs.
  - 3. If the organ recipient consents to the performance of the transplant knowing its illicit origin, he or she shall be punished with the same penalties provided in paragraph 1, which may be reduced by one or two degrees taking into account the circumstances of the act and of the offender.
  - 4. Penalties one degree higher than those provided in paragraph 1 shall be imposed when:
- a) the life or physical or mental integrity of the victim of the crime has been placed in serious danger.
- b) the victim is a minor or especially vulnerable by reason of age, disability, illness or situation.

If both circumstances occur, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half.

5. The medical personnel, public official or private individual who, in connection with the exercise of their profession or position, carries out in public or private centers the conduct described in paragraphs 1 and 2, or requests or receives the gift or remuneration referred to in letter b) of the latter paragraph, or accepts the offer or promise to receive it, shall incur the penalty indicated therein one degree higher and, in addition, special disqualification from public employment or position, profession or trade, from exercising any health profession or from providing services of any kind in clinics, establishments or offices, public or private, for the duration of the sentence. If, in addition, any of the circumstances provided in paragraph 4 occur, the penalties shall be imposed in their upper half.

For the purposes of this article, the term medical personnel includes physicians, nursing staff and any other person who performs a health or social-health activity.

6. A penalty one degree higher than that provided in paragraph 1 and special disqualification from profession, trade, industry or commerce for the duration of the sentence shall be imposed when the offender belongs to a criminal organization or group dedicated to carrying out such activities. If any of the circumstances provided in paragraph 4 occur, the penalties shall be imposed in the upper half. If the circumstance provided in paragraph 5 occurs, the penalties indicated therein shall be imposed in their upper half.

When dealing with the leaders, administrators or managers of such organizations or groups, the penalty shall be applied in its upper half, which may be raised to the immediately higher degree. In any case, the penalty shall be raised to the immediately higher degree if any of the circumstances provided in paragraph 4 or the circumstance provided in paragraph 5 occurs.

7. When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal entity is responsible for the crimes included in this article, it shall be subject to a fine of three to five times the benefit obtained.

Taking into account the rules established in article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set forth in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33.

8. Incitement, conspiracy and proposal to commit the crimes provided for in this article shall be punished with a penalty one to two degrees lower than that corresponding, respectively, to the acts provided for in the preceding paragraphs.

9. In any case, the penalties provided for in this article shall be imposed without prejudice to those that may correspond, where applicable, for the crime of article 177 bis of this Code and other crimes actually committed.

10. Convictions by foreign judges or courts for crimes of the same nature as those provided for in this article shall produce the effects of recidivism, unless the criminal record has been cancelled or may be cancelled in accordance with Spanish law.

###### Article 156 ter.

The public distribution or dissemination through the Internet, telephone or any other information or communication technology of content specifically intended to promote, encourage or incite self-harm of minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

Judicial authorities shall order the adoption of the necessary measures for the removal of the content referred to in the preceding paragraph, for the interruption of services that predominantly offer such content or for the blocking of both when they are located abroad.

###### Article 156 quater.

Persons convicted of the commission of one or more crimes included in this Title, when the victim is one of the persons referred to in paragraph 2 of

###### article 173, may also be subject to a measure of supervised release.

###### Article 156 quinquies.

Persons convicted of the commission of any of the crimes provided for in articles 147.1, 148, 149, 150 and 153 in which the victim is a minor may be subject, in addition to the applicable penalties, to the penalty of special disqualification from any profession, trade or other activities, whether or not remunerated, that entails regular and direct contact with minors, for a period of three to five years beyond the duration of the custodial sentence imposed in the judgment or for a period of two to five years when a prison sentence has not been imposed, in both cases taking into account proportionally the seriousness of the crime, the number of crimes committed and the circumstances present in the convicted person.

#### TITLE IV

Of injuries to the fetus

###### Artículo 157.

Whoever, by any means or procedure, causes in a fetus an injury or illness that seriously harms its normal development, or causes in it a serious physical or mental defect, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to four years and special disqualification from exercising any health profession, or from providing services of any kind in clinics, establishments or gynecological offices, public or private, for a period of two to eight years.

###### Artículo 158.

Whoever, through serious negligence, commits the acts described in the preceding article, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to five months or a fine of six to 10 months. When the acts described in the preceding article are committed through professional negligence, the penalty of special disqualification for the exercise of the profession, occupation or position for a period of six months to two years shall also be imposed.

The pregnant woman shall not be punished under this provision.

#### TÍTULO V

Offenses relating to genetic manipulation

###### Artículo 159.

1. Those who, for a purpose other than the elimination or reduction of serious defects or illnesses, manipulate human genes in a manner that alters the genotype, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to six years and special disqualification for public employment or office, profession or occupation from seven to ten years.

2. If the alteration of the genotype is carried out through serious negligence, the penalty shall be a fine of six to fifteen months and special disqualification for public employment or office, profession or occupation from one to three years.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Artículo 160.

1. The use of genetic engineering to produce biological or exterminating weapons against the human species shall be punished with imprisonment from three to seven years and special disqualification for public employment or office, profession or occupation for a period of seven to 10 years.

2. Those who fertilize human eggs for any purpose other than human procreation shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years and special disqualification for public employment or office, profession or occupation from six to 10 years.

3. The creation of identical human beings by cloning or other procedures aimed at the selection of race shall be punished with the same penalty.

###### Artículo 161.

1. Whoever performs assisted reproduction on a woman, without her consent, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to six years, and special disqualification for public employment or office, profession or occupation for a period of one to four years.

2. To proceed for this offense, a complaint from the aggrieved person or their legal representative shall be required. When the latter is a minor, a person with a disability in need of special protection or a helpless person, the Public Prosecutor's Office may also file a complaint.

###### Artículo 162.

In the offenses contemplated in this title, the judicial authority may impose one or more of the consequences provided for in Article 129 of this Code when the guilty party belongs to a company, organization or association, even of a temporary nature, that is dedicated to carrying out such activities.

#### TÍTULO VI

Offenses against freedom

##### CAPÍTULO I

Illegal detentions and kidnappings

###### Artículo 163.

1. A private individual who confines or detains another, depriving him of his freedom, shall be punished with imprisonment from four to six years.

2. If the guilty party releases the confined or detained person within the first three days of detention, without having achieved the object that he had proposed, the penalty lower in degree shall be imposed.

3. Imprisonment from five to eight years shall be imposed if the confinement or detention has lasted more than fifteen days.

4. A private individual who, outside the cases permitted by law, apprehends a person to present him immediately to the authority, shall be punished with a fine of three to six months.

###### Artículo 164.

The kidnapping of a person demanding some condition for releasing him, shall be punished with imprisonment from six to ten years. If in the kidnapping the circumstance of Article 163.3 has occurred, the penalty higher in degree shall be imposed, and the lower in degree if the conditions of Article 163.2 are met.

###### Artículo 165.

The penalties of the preceding articles shall be imposed in their upper half, in the respective cases, if the illegal detention or kidnapping has been executed with simulation of

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

authority or public function, or the victim is a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection or a public official in the exercise of his functions.

###### Artículo 166.

1. The perpetrator of illegal detention or kidnapping who does not give an account of the whereabouts of the detained person shall be punished with imprisonment from ten to fifteen years, in the case of illegal detention, and from fifteen to twenty years in the case of kidnapping.

2. The act shall be punished with imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years, in the case of illegal detention, and from twenty to twenty-five years of imprisonment, in the case of kidnapping, when any of the following circumstances concur:

- a) That the victim is a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection.
- b) That the perpetrator has carried out the illegal detention or kidnapping with the intention of attacking the freedom or sexual integrity of the victim, or has subsequently acted with that purpose.

###### Artículo 167.

1. The authority or public official who, outside the cases permitted by law, and without intervening cause for an offense, commits any of the acts described in this Chapter shall be punished with the penalties respectively provided for therein, in their upper half, being able to reach up to the higher in degree.

2. With the same penalties shall be punished:

- a) The public official or authority who, with or without cause for an offense, orders, carries out or prolongs the deprivation of liberty of anyone and who does not acknowledge said deprivation of liberty or, in any other way, conceals the situation or whereabouts of that person depriving him of his constitutional or legal rights.
- b) The private individual who has carried out the acts with the authorization, support or acquiescence of the State or its authorities.
  - 3. In all cases in which the acts referred to in this article have been committed by an authority or public official, the penalty of absolute disqualification for a period of eight to twelve years shall also be imposed on them.

###### Artículo 168.

The incitement, conspiracy and proposal to commit the offenses provided for in this Chapter shall be punished with the penalty one or two degrees lower than that assigned to the offense in question.

##### CAPÍTULO II

Threats

###### Artículo 169.

Whoever threatens another with causing to him, his family or other persons with whom he is intimately linked a harm that constitutes offenses of homicide, injuries, abortion, against freedom, torture and against moral integrity, sexual freedom, privacy, honor, property and the socioeconomic order, shall be punished:

1.º With imprisonment from one to five years, if the threat has been made demanding a sum or imposing any other condition, even if not unlawful, and the guilty party has achieved his purpose. If not achieved, imprisonment from six months to three years shall be imposed.

The penalties indicated in the preceding paragraph shall be imposed in their upper half if the threats are made in writing, by telephone or by any means of communication or reproduction, or in the name of real or supposed entities or groups.

2.º With imprisonment from six months to two years, when the threat has not been conditional.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Article 170.

1. If threats of harm that would constitute a crime are directed at intimidating the inhabitants of a town, ethnic, cultural or religious group, or social or professional collective, or any other group of persons, and have the necessary gravity to achieve this, the penalties one degree higher than those provided for in the previous article shall be imposed respectively.

2. Those who, with the same purpose and gravity, publicly call for the commission of violent actions by terrorist organizations or groups, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to two years.

###### Article 171.

1. Threats of harm that does not constitute a crime shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to one year or a fine of six to 24 months, considering the gravity and circumstances of the act, when the threat is conditional and the condition does not consist of conduct that is due. If the guilty party has achieved their purpose, the penalty in its upper half shall be imposed.

2. If someone demands from another a sum or reward under the threat of revealing or disseminating facts relating to their private life or family relations that are not publicly known and may affect their reputation, credit or interest, they shall be punished with imprisonment of two to four years, if they have obtained delivery of all or part of what was demanded, and with imprisonment of four months to two years, if they have not obtained it.

3. If the act described in the preceding paragraph consists of the threat to reveal or report the commission of any crime, the public prosecutor may, to facilitate the punishment of the threat, refrain from prosecuting for the crime whose revelation has been threatened, unless it is punishable with imprisonment exceeding two years. In the latter case, the judge or court may reduce the sanction by one or two degrees.

4. Whoever lightly threatens a person who is or has been their spouse, or a woman who is or has been linked to them by a similar affective relationship even without cohabitation, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to one year or community service of thirty-one to eighty days and, in any case, deprivation of the right to possess and carry weapons from one year and one day to three years, as well as, when the Judge or Court deems it appropriate to the interest of the minor or person with disability in need of special protection, special disqualification for the exercise of parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, care or foster care for up to five years.

The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever lightly threatens a particularly vulnerable person living with the perpetrator.

5. Whoever lightly threatens with weapons or other dangerous instruments any of the persons referred to in article 173.2, except those contemplated in the preceding paragraph of this article, shall be punished with imprisonment of three months to one year or community service of thirty-one to eighty days and, in any case, deprivation of the right to possess and carry weapons from one to three years, as well as, when the Judge or Court deems it appropriate to the interest of the minor or person with disability in need of special protection, special disqualification for the exercise of parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, care or foster care for a period of six months to three years. The penalties provided for in paragraphs 4 and 5 shall be imposed in their upper half when the crime is perpetrated in the presence of minors, or takes place in the common domicile or in the domicile of the victim, or is committed in breach of a penalty contemplated in

###### article 48

of this Code or a precautionary or security measure of the same nature.

6. Notwithstanding the provisions of paragraphs 4 and 5, the Judge or Court, stating reasons in the judgment, in consideration of the personal circumstances of the perpetrator and those concurrent in the commission of the act, may impose the penalty one degree lower.

7. Outside the aforementioned cases, whoever lightly threatens another shall be punished with a fine of one to three months. This act shall only be prosecutable upon complaint by the aggrieved person or their legal representative.

When the offended party is one of the persons referred to in paragraph 2 of

###### article 173, the penalty shall be permanent location for five to thirty days, always in

a different domicile and away from that of the victim, or community service of

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

five to thirty days, or a fine of one to four months, the latter only in cases where the circumstances expressed in paragraph 2 of article 84 are present. In these cases, the complaint referred to in the preceding paragraph shall not be required.

##### CHAPTER III

On coercion

###### Article 172.

1. Whoever, without being lawfully authorized, prevents another by violence from doing what the law does not prohibit, or compels them to do what they do not want, whether just or unjust, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to three years or with a fine of 12 to 24 months, depending on the gravity of the coercion or the means employed.

When the coercion exercised has as its object to prevent the exercise of a fundamental right, the penalties in their upper half shall be imposed, unless the act has a greater penalty established in another provision of this Code.

The penalties in their upper half shall also be imposed when the coercion exercised has as its object to prevent the lawful enjoyment of housing.

2. Whoever lightly coerces a person who is or has been their spouse, or a woman who is or has been linked to them by a similar affective relationship, even without cohabitation, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to one year or community service of thirty-one to eighty days and, in any case, deprivation of the right to possess and carry weapons from one year and one day to three years, as well as, when the Judge or Court deems it appropriate to the interest of the minor or person with disability in need of special protection, special disqualification for the exercise of parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, care or foster care for up to five years.

The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever lightly coerces a particularly vulnerable person living with the perpetrator.

The penalty in its upper half shall be imposed when the crime is perpetrated in the presence of minors, or takes place in the common domicile or in the domicile of the victim, or is committed in breach of a penalty contemplated in article 48 of this Code or a precautionary or security measure of the same nature.

Notwithstanding the provisions of the preceding paragraphs, the Judge or Court, stating reasons in the judgment, in consideration of the personal circumstances of the perpetrator and those concurrent in the commission of the act, may impose the penalty one degree lower.

3. Outside the aforementioned cases, whoever causes another a coercion of a light nature shall be punished with a fine of one to three months. This act shall only be prosecutable upon complaint by the aggrieved person or their legal representative.

When the offended party is one of the persons referred to in paragraph 2 of

###### article 173, the penalty shall be permanent location for five to thirty days, always in

a different domicile and away from that of the victim, or community service of five to thirty days, or a fine of one to four months, the latter only in cases where the circumstances expressed in paragraph 2 of article 84 are present. In these cases, the complaint referred to in the preceding paragraph shall not be required.

###### Article 172 bis.

1. Whoever compels another person to contract marriage through serious intimidation or violence shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years and six months or with a fine of twelve to twenty-four months, according to the seriousness of the coercion or the means employed.

2. The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever, for the purpose of committing the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph, uses violence, serious intimidation or deceit to force another to leave Spanish territory or not to return to it.

3. The penalties shall be imposed in their upper half when the victim is a minor.

4. In convictions for the crime of forced marriage, in addition to the ruling corresponding to civil liability, there shall be made, where appropriate, those that

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

are appropriate regarding the declaration of nullity or dissolution of the marriage thus contracted and regarding filiation and the determination of maintenance.

###### Article 172 ter.

1. Whoever harasses a person by carrying out, in a persistent and repeated manner, and without being legitimately authorized, any of the following conducts and, in this way, alters the normal development of their daily life, shall be punished with a prison sentence of three months to two years or a fine of six to twenty-four months:

1.ª Watches them, pursues them or seeks their physical proximity.

2.ª Establishes or attempts to establish contact with them through any means of communication, or through third parties.

3.ª Through the improper use of their personal data, acquires products or goods, or contracts services, or causes third parties to contact them. 4.ª Commits acts against their freedom or against their property, or against the freedom or property of another person close to them. When the victim is in a situation of special vulnerability by reason of their age, illness, disability or any other circumstance, a prison sentence of six months to two years shall be imposed.

2. When the offended party is one of the persons referred to in paragraph 2 of

###### article 173, a prison sentence of one to two years, or community service of

sixty to one hundred twenty days, shall be imposed. In this case, the complaint referred to in paragraph 4 of this article shall not be necessary.

3. The penalties provided for in this article shall be imposed without prejudice to those that may correspond to the crimes in which the acts of harassment have materialized.

4. The acts described in this article shall only be prosecuted upon complaint by the aggrieved person or their legal representative.

5. Whoever, without the consent of its owner, uses the image of a person to make announcements or open false profiles on social networks, contact pages or any means of public dissemination, causing them a situation of harassment, harassment or humiliation, shall be punished with a prison sentence of three months to one year or a fine of six to twelve months. If the victim of the crime is a minor or a person with a disability, the upper half of the sentence shall be applied.

###### Article 172 quater.

1. Whoever, in order to obstruct the exercise of the right to voluntary termination of pregnancy, harasses a woman through annoying, offensive, intimidating or coercive acts that undermine her freedom, shall be punished with a prison sentence of three months to one year or community service of thirty-one to eighty days.

2. The same penalties shall be imposed on whoever, in the manner described in the preceding paragraph, harasses health workers in their professional practice or public function and the medical or management staff of centers authorized to terminate pregnancy with the objective of obstructing the exercise of their profession or position.

3. Taking into account the seriousness, the personal circumstances of the perpetrator and those present in the commission of the act, the court may also impose a prohibition from attending certain places for a period of six months to three years.

4. The penalties provided for in this article shall be imposed without prejudice to those that may correspond to the crimes in which the acts of harassment have materialized.

5. In the prosecution of the acts described in this article, the complaint of the aggrieved person or their legal representation shall not be necessary.

#### TITLE VII

Of torture and other crimes against moral integrity

###### Article 173.

1. Whoever inflicts degrading treatment on another person, seriously undermining their moral integrity, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

The same penalty shall be imposed on those who, having knowledge of the whereabouts of a person's corpse, repeatedly conceal such information from the family members or relatives of that person.

The same penalty shall be imposed on those who, within the scope of any employment or official relationship and taking advantage of their position of superiority, repeatedly carry out hostile or humiliating acts against another person that, without constituting degrading treatment, constitute serious harassment of the victim.

The same penalty shall also be imposed on whoever repeatedly carries out hostile or humiliating acts that, without constituting degrading treatment, are intended to prevent the legitimate enjoyment of housing.

When, in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis, a legal person is responsible for the offenses included in the preceding paragraphs, it shall be punished with a fine of six months to two years. In accordance with the rules established in article 66 bis, Judges and Courts may also impose the penalties set forth in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33.

2. Whoever habitually exercises physical or psychological violence against a person who is or has been their spouse or against a person who is or has been linked to them by an analogous relationship of affection even without cohabitation, or against descendants, ascendants or siblings by nature, adoption or affinity, their own or those of the spouse or partner, or against minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection who live with them or who are subject to the authority, guardianship, curatorship, foster care or de facto custody of the spouse or partner, or against a person protected by any other relationship by which they are integrated into their family unit, as well as against persons who, due to their special vulnerability, are subject to custody or care in public or private centers, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years, deprivation of the right to own and carry weapons for three to five years and, where appropriate, when the judge or court deems it appropriate to the interest of the minor or person with disability in need of special protection, special disqualification from the exercise of parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, custody or foster care for a period of one to five years, without prejudice to the penalties that may correspond to the offenses in which the acts of physical or psychological violence have materialized.

The penalties shall be imposed in their upper half when one or more of the acts of violence are perpetrated in the presence of minors, or using weapons, or take place in the common domicile or in the victim's domicile, or are carried out in breach of a penalty contemplated in article 48 or a precautionary or security measure or prohibition of the same nature.

In the cases referred to in this paragraph, a supervised release measure may also be imposed.

3. To assess the habitual nature referred to in the preceding paragraph, consideration shall be given to the number of acts of violence that are proven, as well as their temporal proximity, regardless of whether such violence has been exercised against the same or different victims included in this article, and whether the violent acts have been or have not been subject to prosecution in previous proceedings.

4. Whoever causes injury or unjust vexation of a minor nature, when the offended party is one of the persons referred to in paragraph 2 of article 173, shall be punished with permanent location for five to thirty days, always in a different domicile and away from that of the victim, or community service for five to thirty days, or a fine of one to four months, the latter only in cases where the circumstances expressed in paragraph 2 of article 84 apply.

The same penalties shall be imposed on those who address another person with expressions, behaviors or propositions of a sexual nature that create for the victim an objectively humiliating, hostile or intimidating situation, without constituting other more serious offenses.

The offenses defined in the two preceding paragraphs may only be prosecuted upon complaint by the aggrieved person or their legal representative.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Article 174.

1. An authority or public official who, abusing their position, and with the purpose of obtaining a confession or information from any person or of punishing them for any act they have committed or are suspected of having committed, or for any reason based on any type of discrimination, subjects them to conditions or procedures that, by their nature, duration or other circumstances, cause them physical or mental suffering, the suppression or diminution of their faculties of knowledge, discernment or decision or that, in any other way, violate their moral integrity, commits torture. The person guilty of torture shall be punished with imprisonment from two to six years if the offense is serious, and with imprisonment from one to three years if it is not. In addition to the penalties indicated, the penalty of absolute disqualification from eight to 12 years shall be imposed in all cases.

2. The same penalties shall be incurred, respectively, by the authority or official of penitentiary institutions or centers for the protection or correction of minors who commits, with respect to detainees, inmates or prisoners, the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph.

###### Article 175.

An authority or public official who, abusing their position and outside the cases included in the preceding article, violates the moral integrity of a person shall be punished with imprisonment from two to four years if the offense is serious, and with imprisonment from six months to two years if it is not. In all cases, the perpetrator shall be subject, in addition to the penalties indicated, to special disqualification from public employment or office for two to four years.

###### Article 176.

The penalties respectively established in the preceding articles shall be imposed on the authority or official who, failing in the duties of their office, allows other persons to execute the acts provided for therein.

###### Article 177.

If in the offenses described in the preceding articles, in addition to the violation of moral integrity, injury or damage is caused to the life, physical integrity, health, sexual freedom or property of the victim or of a third party, the acts shall be punished separately with the penalty corresponding to the offenses committed, except when the former is already specifically punished by law.

#### TITLE VII BIS

On trafficking in human beings

###### Article 177 bis.

1. Whoever, whether in Spanish territory, from Spain, in transit or with destination to it, using violence, intimidation or deception, or by abusing a situation of superiority or of need or vulnerability of the national or foreign victim, or through the delivery or receipt of payments or benefits to obtain the consent of the person who had control over the victim, recruits, transports, transfers, harbors, or receives them, including the exchange or transfer of control over such persons, for any of the following purposes, shall be punished with a penalty of five to eight years of imprisonment as guilty of trafficking in human beings:

- a) The imposition of forced labor or services, slavery or practices similar to slavery, servitude or begging.
- b) Sexual exploitation, including pornography.
- c) Exploitation to carry out criminal activities.
- d) The removal of their bodily organs.
- e) The celebration of forced marriages.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

A situation of need or vulnerability exists when the person in question has no other alternative, real or acceptable, than to submit to the abuse.

When the victim of trafficking in human beings is a minor, the penalty of special disqualification for any profession, occupation or activities, whether paid or not, that involves regular and direct contact with minors, for a period of between six and twenty years in addition to the duration of the custodial sentence imposed, shall in any case be imposed.

2. Even when none of the means set forth in the preceding paragraph is used, any of the actions indicated in the preceding paragraph shall be considered trafficking in human beings when carried out with respect to minors for the purpose of exploitation.

3. The consent of a victim of trafficking in human beings shall be irrelevant when any of the means indicated in the first paragraph of this article has been used.

4. The penalty one degree higher than that provided for in the first paragraph of this article shall be imposed when:

- a) the life or physical or mental integrity of the persons subject to the offense has been endangered;
- b) the victim is especially vulnerable by reason of illness, pregnancy, disability or personal situation, or is a minor.
- c) the victim is a person whose situation of vulnerability has been caused or aggravated by displacement resulting from an armed conflict or a humanitarian catastrophe.

If more than one circumstance concurs, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half.

5. The penalty one degree higher than that provided for in paragraph 1 of this article and absolute disqualification for six to twelve years shall be imposed on those who commit the acts by taking advantage of their status as an authority, agent thereof or public official. If any of the circumstances provided for in paragraph 4 of this article also concurs, the penalties shall be imposed in their upper half.

6. The penalty one degree higher than that provided for in paragraph 1 of this article and special disqualification for profession, occupation, industry or trade for the term of the sentence shall be imposed when the guilty party belongs to an organization or association of more than two persons, even of a transitory nature, that is engaged in carrying out such activities. If any of the circumstances provided for in paragraph 4 of this article concurs, the penalties shall be imposed in the upper half. If the circumstance provided for in paragraph 5 of this article concurs, the penalties indicated therein shall be imposed in their upper half.

When it concerns the leaders, administrators or managers of such organizations or associations, the penalty shall be applied to them in its upper half, which may be raised to the immediately higher degree. In any case, the penalty shall be raised to the immediately higher degree if any of the circumstances provided for in paragraph 4 or the circumstance provided for in paragraph 5 of this article concurs.

7. When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the offenses included in this article, it shall be imposed a fine of three to five times the benefit obtained. Having regard to the rules established in article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set forth in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33.

8. Incitement, conspiracy and solicitation to commit the offense of trafficking in human beings shall be punished with a penalty one or two degrees lower than that of the corresponding offense.

9. In any case, the penalties provided for in this article shall be imposed without prejudice to those that correspond, as the case may be, for the offense of article 318 bis of this Code and other offenses actually committed, including those constituting the corresponding exploitation.

10. Convictions by foreign judges or courts for offenses of the same nature as those provided for in this article shall produce the effects of recidivism, unless the criminal record has been expunged or may be expunged in accordance with Spanish law.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

11.
