Without prejudice to the application of the general rules of this Code, the victim of trafficking in human beings shall be exempt from penalty for the criminal offenses committed in the situation of exploitation suffered, provided that their participation in them has been a direct consequence of the situation of violence, intimidation, deception or abuse to which they have been subjected and that there is adequate proportionality between said situation and the criminal act committed.

#### TITLE VIII

Offenses against sexual freedom

##### CHAPTER I

On sexual assaults

###### Article 178.

1. Whoever commits any act that violates the sexual freedom of another person without their consent shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to four years, as responsible for sexual assault. Consent shall only be understood to exist when it has been freely manifested through acts that, in attention to the circumstances of the case, clearly express the will of the person.

2. Sexual acts that are committed using violence, intimidation or abuse of a situation of superiority or vulnerability of the victim, as well as those that are executed on persons who are deprived of consciousness or whose mental condition is abused and those that are committed when the victim has their will annulled for any reason, shall in any case be considered sexual assault.

3. If the assault has been committed using violence or intimidation or on a victim who has their will annulled for any reason, its perpetrator shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to five years.

4. The sentencing body, reasoning it in the judgment, and provided that violence or intimidation is not involved or that the victim had their will annulled for any reason or the circumstances of article 180 do not concur, may impose the prison sentence in its lower half or a fine of eighteen to twenty-four months, in consideration of the lesser severity of the act and the personal circumstances of the guilty party.

###### Article 179.

1. When the sexual assault consists of carnal access by vaginal, anal or oral route, or introduction of bodily members or objects by either of the first two routes, the perpetrator shall be punished as guilty of rape with a prison sentence of four to twelve years.

2. If the assault referred to in the preceding paragraph is committed using violence or intimidation or when the victim has their will annulled for any reason, a prison sentence of six to twelve years shall be imposed.

###### Artículo 180.

1. The aforementioned conducts shall be punished, respectively, with the penalties of imprisonment from two to eight years for the aggressions of Article 178.1, imprisonment from five to ten years for the aggressions of Article 178.3, imprisonment from seven to fifteen years for the aggressions of Article 179.1 and imprisonment from twelve to fifteen years for those of Article 179.2, when any of the following circumstances concur:

1.ª When the acts are committed through the joint action of two or more persons. 2.ª When the sexual aggression is preceded or accompanied by violence of extreme gravity or by acts of a particularly degrading or humiliating nature.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

3.ª When the acts are committed against a person who is in a situation of special vulnerability by reason of age, illness, disability or any other circumstance, except as provided in Article 181.

4.ª When the victim is or has been the wife or woman who is or has been linked by an analogous relationship of affection, even without cohabitation.

5.ª When, for the execution of the crime, the person responsible had taken advantage of a situation or relationship of cohabitation or kinship or a relationship of superiority with respect to the victim.

6.ª When the person responsible makes use of weapons or other equally dangerous means, capable of producing death or any of the injuries provided for in Articles 149 and 150 of this Code, without prejudice to the provisions of Article 194 bis.

7.ª When for the commission of these acts the person responsible has annulled the will of the victim by supplying pharmaceuticals, drugs or any other natural or chemical substance suitable for such purpose.

When in the description of the typical modalities provided for in Articles 178 or 179 any of the foregoing circumstances had been taken into consideration, the conflict shall be resolved in accordance with the rule of Article 8.4 of this Code.

2. If two or more of the foregoing circumstances concur, the penalties respectively provided for in paragraph 1 of this article shall be imposed in their upper half.

3. In all cases provided for in this chapter, when the guilty party had taken advantage of his or her condition as an authority, agent thereof or public official, the penalty of absolute disqualification from six to twelve years shall also be imposed.

##### CAPÍTULO II

Of sexual aggressions against minors under sixteen years of age

###### Artículo 181.

1. Whoever performs acts of a sexual nature with a minor under sixteen years of age shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from two to six years.

For these purposes, acts of a sexual nature are considered to include those performed by the minor with a third party or upon himself or herself at the instigation of the perpetrator.

2. If in the conducts of the preceding paragraph any of the modalities described in Article 178.2 and 3 concurs, a penalty of imprisonment from five to ten years shall be imposed.

3. The sentencing body, reasoning it in the sentence, in view of the lesser seriousness of the act and assessing all the concurrent circumstances, including the personal circumstances of the guilty party, may impose the penalty of imprisonment lower in degree, except when violence or intimidation is involved or it is performed upon a victim whose will has been annulled for any reason, or the circumstances mentioned in paragraph 5 of this article concur.

4. When the sexual act consists of carnal access by vaginal, anal or oral route, or in introduction of body members or objects through either of the first two routes, the person responsible shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from eight to twelve years in the cases of paragraph 1, and with the penalty of imprisonment from twelve to fifteen years in the cases of paragraph 2.

5. The conducts provided for in the preceding paragraphs shall be punished with the corresponding penalty of imprisonment in its upper half when any of the following circumstances concur:

- a) When the acts are committed through the joint action of two or more persons.
- b) When the sexual aggression is preceded or accompanied by violence of extreme gravity or by acts of a particularly degrading or humiliating nature.
- c) When the acts are committed against a person who is in a situation of special vulnerability by reason of age, illness, disability or any other circumstance, and, in any case, when the person is under four years of age.
- d) When the victim is or has been the partner of the perpetrator, even without cohabitation.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

e) When, for the execution of the crime, the person responsible had taken advantage of a situation or relationship of cohabitation or kinship or a relationship of superiority with respect to the victim.

f) When the person responsible makes use of weapons or other equally dangerous means, capable of producing death or any of the injuries provided for in Articles 149 and 150 of this Code, without prejudice to the provisions of Article 194 bis.

g) When for the commission of these acts the person responsible has annulled the will of the victim by supplying pharmaceuticals, drugs or any other natural or chemical substance suitable for such purpose.

h) When the infraction has been committed within an organization or a criminal group dedicated to carrying out such activities.

In the event that in the description of the typical modalities provided for in paragraphs 1 to 3 of this article any of the foregoing circumstances had been taken into consideration, the conflict shall be resolved in accordance with the rule of Article 8.4 of this Code.

6. If two or more of the foregoing circumstances concur, the penalties of the preceding paragraph shall be imposed in their upper half.

7. In all cases provided for in this article, when the guilty party had taken advantage of his or her condition as an authority, agent thereof or public official, the penalty of absolute disqualification from six to twelve years shall also be imposed.

###### Artículo 182.

1. Whoever, for sexual purposes, causes a minor under sixteen years of age to witness acts of a sexual nature, even if the perpetrator does not participate in them, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years.

2. If the acts of a sexual nature that are made to be witnessed by the minor under sixteen years of age constitute a crime against sexual freedom, the penalty shall be imprisonment from one to three years.

###### Artículo 183.

1. Whoever through the internet, the telephone or any other information and communication technology contacts a minor under sixteen years of age and proposes to arrange a meeting with him or her in order to commit any of the crimes described in Articles 181 and 189, provided that such proposal is accompanied by material acts aimed at the approach, shall be punished with the penalty of one to three years of imprisonment or a fine of twelve to twenty-four months, without prejudice to the penalties corresponding to the crimes committed, if any. The penalties shall be imposed in their upper half when the approach is obtained by means of coercion, intimidation or deception.

2. Whoever, through the internet, the telephone or any other information and communication technology contacts a minor under sixteen years of age and performs acts aimed at luring him or her to provide pornographic material or to show him or her pornographic images in which a minor is represented or appears, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years.

###### Artículo 183 bis.

Except in cases where any of the circumstances provided for in the second paragraph of Article 178 concur, the free consent of the minor under sixteen years of age shall exclude criminal liability for the crimes provided for in this chapter when the perpetrator is a person close to the minor in age and degree of physical and psychological development or maturity.

##### CAPÍTULO III

Of sexual harassment

###### Artículo 184.

1. Whoever requests favors of a sexual nature, for himself or for a third party, within the scope of an employment, teaching, service provision or similar relationship, continued or habitual, and with such behavior causes the victim an objective and seriously

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

intimidating, hostile or humiliating situation, shall be punished, as the perpetrator of sexual harassment, with a prison sentence of six to twelve months or a fine of ten to fifteen months and special disqualification for the exercise of the profession, trade or activity for twelve to fifteen months.

2. If the perpetrator of sexual harassment had committed the act by taking advantage of a situation of employment, teaching or hierarchical superiority, or over a person subject to his or her custody or care, or with the express or tacit announcement of causing the victim harm related to the legitimate expectations that the victim may have within the scope of the indicated relationship, the penalty shall be imprisonment from one to two years and special disqualification for the exercise of the profession, trade or activity for eighteen to twenty-four months.

3. Likewise, if the perpetrator of sexual harassment had committed it in centers for the protection or reform of minors, detention centers for foreign persons, or any other center of detention, custody or reception, including temporary stay, the penalty shall be imprisonment from one to two years and special disqualification for the exercise of the profession, trade or activity for eighteen to twenty-four months, without prejudice to what is established in the article

4. When the victim is in a situation of special vulnerability due to age, illness or disability, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half.

5. When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis, a legal person is responsible for this offense, a fine of six months to two years shall be imposed. Taking into account the rules established in article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set forth in letters b) to g) of section 7 of article 33.

##### CAPÍTULO IV

Of the offenses of exhibitionism and sexual provocation

###### Artículo 185.

Whoever performs or causes another person to perform acts of obscene exhibition before minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to one year or a fine of 12 to 24 months.

###### Artículo 186.

Whoever, by any direct means, sells, disseminates or exhibits pornographic material among minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to one year or a fine of 12 to 24 months.

##### CAPÍTULO V

Of offenses relating to prostitution and sexual exploitation and corruption of minors.

###### Artículo 187.

1. Whoever, using violence, intimidation or deception, or abusing a situation of superiority or necessity or vulnerability of the victim, induces an adult person to engage in or remain in prostitution, shall be punished with prison sentences of two to five years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

A prison sentence of two to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months shall be imposed on whoever profits by exploiting the prostitution of another person, even with their consent. In any case, it shall be understood that there is exploitation when any of the following circumstances is present:

- a) That the victim is in a situation of personal or economic vulnerability.
- b) That onerous, disproportionate or abusive conditions are imposed on them for its exercise.
  - 2. The penalties provided in the preceding sections shall be imposed in their upper half, in their respective cases, when any of the following circumstances is present:

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

a) When the perpetrator had taken advantage of their status as an authority, agent thereof or public official. In this case, the penalty of absolute disqualification from six to twelve years shall also apply.

b) When the perpetrator belongs to a criminal organization or group dedicated to carrying out such activities.

c) When the perpetrator had endangered, intentionally or through gross negligence, the life or health of the victim.

3. The penalties indicated shall be imposed in their respective cases without prejudice to those corresponding to sexual assaults or abuses committed against the prostituted person.

###### Artículo 188.

1. Whoever induces, promotes, favors or facilitates the prostitution of a minor or a person with disability in need of special protection, or profits therefrom, or otherwise exploits a minor or a person with disability for these purposes, shall be punished with prison sentences of two to five years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

If the victim is under sixteen years of age, a prison sentence of four to eight years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months shall be imposed.

2. If the acts described in the preceding section are committed with violence or intimidation, in addition to the fines provided, a prison sentence of five to ten years shall be imposed if the victim is under sixteen years of age, and a prison sentence of four to six years in other cases.

3. Penalties higher in degree than those provided in the preceding sections shall be imposed, in their respective cases, when any of the following circumstances is present:

- a) When the victim is in a situation of special vulnerability due to age, illness, disability or any other circumstance.
- b) When, for the execution of the offense, the perpetrator had taken advantage of a situation of cohabitation or a relationship of superiority or kinship, by being an ascendant, or sibling, by nature or adoption, or relatives by marriage, with the victim.
- c) When, for the execution of the offense, the perpetrator had taken advantage of their status as an authority, agent thereof or public official. In this case, a penalty of absolute disqualification from six to twelve years shall also be imposed.
- d) When the perpetrator had endangered, intentionally or through gross negligence, the life or health of the victim.
- e) When the acts had been committed by the joint action of two or more persons.
- f) When the perpetrator belongs to an organization or association, even of a temporary nature, dedicated to carrying out such activities.
  - 4. Whoever solicits, accepts or obtains, in exchange for remuneration or promise, a sexual relationship with a minor or a person with disability in need of special protection, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to four years. If the minor has not reached sixteen years of age, a prison sentence of two to six years shall be imposed.
  - 5. The penalties indicated shall be imposed in their respective cases without prejudice to those corresponding to offenses against sexual freedom or indemnity committed against minors and persons with disabilities in need of special protection.

###### Article 189.

1. The following shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to five years:

- a) Anyone who recruits or uses minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection for purposes of or in exhibitionist or pornographic performances, whether public or private, or to produce any kind of pornographic material, whatever its medium, or finances any of these activities or profits from them.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

b) Anyone who produces, sells, distributes, exhibits, offers or facilitates the production, sale, dissemination or exhibition by any means of child pornography or pornography in whose production persons with disabilities in need of special protection have been used, or possesses it for these purposes, even if the material originated abroad or is of unknown origin.

For the purposes of this Title, child pornography or pornography in whose production persons with disabilities in need of special protection have been used is considered to be:

- a) Any material that visually depicts a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection participating in sexually explicit conduct, whether real or simulated.
- b) Any depiction of the sexual organs of a minor or person with a disability in need of special protection for primarily sexual purposes.
- c) Any material that visually depicts a person appearing to be a minor participating in sexually explicit conduct, whether real or simulated, or any depiction of the sexual organs of a person appearing to be a minor, for primarily sexual purposes, unless the person appearing to be a minor was actually eighteen years of age or older at the time the images were obtained.
- d) Realistic images of a minor participating in sexually explicit conduct or realistic images of the sexual organs of a minor, for primarily sexual purposes.
  - 2. Those who carry out the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this article shall be punished with a prison sentence of five to nine years when any of the following circumstances is present:
- a) When minors under sixteen years of age are used.
- b) When the acts are of a particularly degrading or humiliating nature, physical or sexual violence is used to obtain the pornographic material, or scenes of physical or sexual violence are depicted.
- c) When minors in a situation of special vulnerability due to illness, disability or any other circumstance are used.
- d) When the offender has intentionally or through gross negligence endangered the life or health of the victim.
- e) When the pornographic material is of notorious importance.
- f) When the offender belongs to an organization or association, even of a temporary nature, that is dedicated to carrying out such activities.
- g) When the person responsible is an ascendant, tutor, curator, guardian, teacher or any other person in charge, de facto, even if provisionally, or de jure, of the minor or person with a disability in need of special protection, or is any person who lives with him or her or another person who has acted by abusing his or her recognized position of trust or authority.
- h) When the aggravating circumstance of recidivism is present.
  - 3. If the acts referred to in letter a) of the first paragraph of paragraph 1 had been committed with violence or intimidation, the penalty immediately higher in degree than those provided for in the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed.
  - 4. Anyone who knowingly attends exhibitionist or pornographic performances in which minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection participate shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to two years.
  - 5. Anyone who for his or her own use acquires or possesses child pornography or pornography in whose production persons with disabilities in need of special protection have been used shall be punished with a prison sentence of three months to one year or a fine of six months to two years.

The same penalty shall be imposed on anyone who knowingly accesses child pornography or pornography in whose production persons with disabilities in need of special protection have been used, by means of information and communication technologies.

6. Anyone who has under his or her authority, tutorship, guardianship or foster care a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection and who, with knowledge of his or her state of prostitution or corruption, does not do what is possible to prevent his or her continuation in such

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

state, or does not go to the competent authority for the same purpose if he or she lacks the means for the custody of the minor or person with a disability in need of special protection, shall be punished with a prison sentence of three to six months or a fine of six to twelve months.

7. The Public Prosecutor's Office shall promote the pertinent actions with the purpose of depriving of parental authority, tutorship, guardianship or family foster care, as the case may be, the person who engages in any of the conducts described in the preceding paragraph.

8. Judges and courts shall order the adoption of the necessary measures for the removal of web pages or internet applications that contain or disseminate child pornography or in whose production persons with disabilities in need of special protection have been used or, as the case may be, to block access to them for internet users located in Spanish territory.

These measures may be ordered as interim measures at the request of the Public Prosecutor's Office.

###### Article 189 bis.

The distribution or public dissemination through the Internet, telephone or any other information or communication technology of content specifically intended to promote, encourage or incite the commission of the offenses provided for in this chapter and in chapters II and IV of this title shall be punished with a fine of six to twelve months or a prison sentence of one to three years.

Judicial authorities shall order the adoption of the necessary measures for the removal of the content referred to in the preceding paragraph, for the interruption of services that predominantly offer such content or for the blocking of both when they are located abroad.

###### Article 189 ter.

When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is liable for the offenses included in this Chapter, the following penalties shall be imposed:

- a) Fine of three to five times the profit obtained, if the offense committed by the natural person carries a prison sentence of more than five years.
- b) Fine of two to four times the profit obtained, if the offense committed by the natural person carries a prison sentence of more than two years not included in the preceding subparagraph.
- c) Fine of two to three times the profit obtained, in all other cases.
- d) Dissolution of the legal person, in accordance with the provisions of article 33.7 b) of this Code, and the other penalties provided therein that are compatible with dissolution may be ordered, taking into account the rules contained in article 66 bis.

##### CHAPTER VI

Common provisions to the preceding chapters

###### Article 190.

The conviction of a foreign Judge or Court, imposed for offenses included in this Title, shall be equivalent to judgments of Spanish Judges or Courts for purposes of applying the aggravating circumstance of recidivism.

###### Article 191.

1. To proceed for the crimes of sexual assault and sexual harassment, a complaint by the aggrieved person, their legal representative, or a charge by the Public Prosecutor's Office, which shall act weighing the legitimate interests present, shall be required. When the victim is a minor, a person with a disability in need of special protection, or a helpless person, a complaint by the Public Prosecutor's Office shall suffice.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

2. In these crimes, the pardon of the offended party or the legal representative does not extinguish the criminal action or the liability of that kind.

###### Article 192.

1. Those convicted to imprisonment for one or more offenses included in this Title shall also be imposed the measure of supervised release, which shall be executed after the custodial sentence. The duration of said measure shall be from five to ten years, if any of the crimes were serious, and from one to five years if it concerns one or more less serious crimes. In the latter case, when it concerns a single crime committed by a first-time offender, the court may impose or not impose the measure of supervised release in consideration of the lower dangerousness of the perpetrator.

2. Ascendants, tutors, curators, guardians, teachers, or any other person in charge de facto or de jure of the minor or person with a disability in need of special protection, who participate as perpetrators or accomplices in the commission of the crimes included in this Title, shall be punished with the penalty that corresponds to them, in its upper half.

This rule shall not apply when the circumstance contained therein is specifically contemplated in the criminal type in question.

3. The judicial authority shall impose on the persons responsible for committing any of the crimes of Chapters I or V when the victim is a minor and in any case of any of the crimes of Chapter II, in addition to the penalties provided in such Chapters, the penalty of deprivation of parental authority or special disqualification for the exercise of the rights of parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, custody or foster care, for a period of four to ten years. On the persons responsible for the remaining crimes of this Title, there may be imposed with reasoning, in addition to the penalties indicated for such crimes, the penalty of deprivation of parental authority or the penalty of special disqualification for the exercise of the rights of parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, custody or foster care, for a period of six months to six years, as well as the penalty of disqualification for public employment or office or exercise of profession or trade, paid or unpaid, for a period of six months to six years. Likewise, the judicial authority shall impose on the persons responsible for the crimes included in this Title, without prejudice to the penalties that correspond in accordance with the preceding articles, a penalty of special disqualification for any profession, trade or activities, whether paid or not, that involves regular and direct contact with minors, for a period exceeding between five and twenty years the duration of the custodial sentence imposed in the judgment if the crime were serious, and between two and twenty years if it were less serious. In both cases, consideration shall be given proportionally to the seriousness of the crime, the number of crimes committed and the circumstances present in the convicted person.

###### Article 193.

In convictions for crimes against sexual freedom, in addition to the ruling corresponding to civil liability, those that are appropriate regarding filiation and determination of child support shall be made, where applicable.

###### Article 194.

In the cases typified in Chapters IV and V of this title, when establishments or premises, open or not to the public, are used in carrying out the acts, their permanent closure shall be decreed in the conviction. The closure may also be adopted as a precautionary measure.

###### Article 194 bis.

The penalties provided for the crimes of this title shall be imposed without prejudice to those that may correspond for the acts of physical or psychological violence that are committed.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

#### TITLE IX

Of the omission of the duty to rescue

###### Article 195.

1. Whoever does not rescue a person who is helpless and in manifest and grave danger, when they could do so without risk to themselves or third parties, shall be punished with a fine of three to twelve months.

2. The same penalties shall be incurred by whoever, prevented from providing rescue, does not urgently request help from others.

3. If the victim were so by accident fortuitously caused by the person who omitted the assistance, the penalty shall be imprisonment from six months to 18 months, and if the accident were due to recklessness, imprisonment from six months to four years.

###### Article 196.

The professional who, being obliged to do so, denies healthcare or abandons healthcare services, when serious risk to the health of persons derives from the denial or abandonment, shall be punished with the penalties of the preceding article in its upper half and with special disqualification for public employment or office, profession or trade, for a period of six months to three years.

#### TITLE X

Crimes against privacy, the right to one's own image and the inviolability of the home

##### CHAPTER I

Of the discovery and disclosure of secrets

###### Article 197.

1. Whoever, in order to discover the secrets or violate the privacy of another, without their consent, seizes their papers, letters, email messages or any other personal documents or effects, intercepts their telecommunications or uses technical devices for listening, transmission, recording or reproduction of sound or image, or any other communication signal, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

2. The same penalties shall be imposed on whoever, without being authorized, seizes, uses or modifies, to the detriment of a third party, reserved data of a personal or family nature of another that are recorded in files or computer, electronic or telematic media, or in any other type of public or private archive or register. Equal penalties shall be imposed on whoever, without being authorized, accesses them by any means and on whoever alters or uses them to the detriment of the data subject or a third party.

3. The penalty of imprisonment from two to five years shall be imposed if the data or facts discovered or the images captured referred to in the preceding paragraphs are disseminated, revealed or transferred to third parties.

Whoever, with knowledge of their illicit origin and without having taken part in their discovery, carries out the conduct described in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

4. The acts described in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this article shall be punished with imprisonment from three to five years when:

- a) They are committed by the persons in charge of or responsible for the files, computer, electronic or telematic media, archives or registers; or
- b) they are carried out through the unauthorized use of the victim's personal data.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

If the reserved data have been disseminated, transferred or revealed to third parties, the penalties shall be imposed in their upper half.

5. Likewise, when the acts described in the preceding paragraphs affect personal data that reveal ideology, religion, beliefs, health, racial origin or sexual life, or the victim is a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection, the penalties provided for shall be imposed in their upper half.

6. If the acts are carried out for profit, the penalties respectively provided for in paragraphs 1 to 4 of this article shall be imposed in their upper half. If they also affect data mentioned in the preceding paragraph, the penalty to be imposed shall be imprisonment from four to seven years.

7. Whoever, without authorization from the affected person, disseminates, reveals or transfers to third parties images or audiovisual recordings of that person that they had obtained with their consent in a home or any other place out of the sight of third parties, when the disclosure seriously undermines the personal privacy of that person, shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to one year or a fine of six to twelve months.

A fine of one to three months shall be imposed on whoever, having received the images or audiovisual recordings referred to in the preceding paragraph, disseminates, reveals or transfers them to third parties without the consent of the affected person.

In the cases of the preceding paragraphs, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half when the acts have been committed by the spouse or by a person who is or has been linked to them by an analogous affective relationship, even without cohabitation, the victim is a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection, or the acts have been committed with a profit-making purpose.

###### Article 197 bis.

1. Whoever by any means or procedure, violating the security measures established to prevent it, and without being duly authorized, accesses or facilitates another person's access to all or part of an information system or remains in it against the will of the person who has the legitimate right to exclude them, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years.

2. Whoever through the use of devices or technical instruments, and without being duly authorized, intercepts non-public transmissions of computer data that occur from, to or within an information system, including electromagnetic emissions from the same, shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to two years or a fine of three to twelve months.

###### Article 197 ter.

Whoever, without being duly authorized, produces, acquires for their use, imports or, in any way, provides to third parties, with the intention of facilitating the commission of any of the offenses referred to in paragraphs 1 and 2 of article 197 or article 197 bis, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years or a fine of three to eighteen months:

- a) a computer program, conceived or adapted mainly to commit such offenses; or
- b) a computer password, an access code or similar data that allow access to all or part of an information system.

###### Article 197 quater.

If the acts described in this Chapter have been committed within a criminal organization or group, the penalties superior in degree shall be applied respectively.

###### Article 197 quinquies.

When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the offenses included in articles 197, 197 bis and 197 ter, a fine of six months to two years shall be imposed. Taking into account the rules established in

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33.

###### Article 198.

The authority or public official who, outside the cases permitted by law, without legal cause for an offense, and taking advantage of their position, carries out any of the conducts described in the preceding article, shall be punished with the penalties respectively provided for therein, in their upper half and, in addition, with absolute disqualification for a period of six to twelve years.

###### Article 199.

1. Whoever reveals another person's secrets, of which they have knowledge by reason of their profession or employment relationships, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of six to twelve months.

2. The professional who, in breach of their obligation of secrecy or confidentiality, discloses another person's secrets, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to four years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification for that profession for a period of two to six years.

###### Article 200.

The provisions of this chapter shall apply to whoever discovers, reveals or transfers reserved data of legal persons, without the consent of their representatives, except as provided in other provisions of this Code.

###### Article 201.

1. To proceed for the offenses provided for in this Chapter, a complaint from the aggrieved person or their legal representative shall be necessary.

2. The complaint required in the preceding paragraph shall not be necessary to proceed for the acts described in article 198 of this Code, nor when the commission of the offense affects general interests, a plurality of persons or if the victim is a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection.

3. The pardon of the offended person or their legal representative, as applicable, extinguishes the criminal action without prejudice to the provisions of article 130.1.5.º, second paragraph.

##### CHAPTER II

Breaking and entering of dwellings, domicile of legal persons and establishments open to the public

###### Artículo 202.

1. An individual who, without residing therein, enters another person's dwelling or remains therein against the will of its occupant, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years.

2. If the act is carried out with violence or intimidation, the penalty shall be imprisonment from one to four years and a fine of six to twelve months.

###### Artículo 203.

1. Anyone who enters against the will of its holder into the premises of a public or private legal person, professional office, or in a commercial establishment or premises open to the public outside opening hours, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine of six to ten months.

2. Anyone who remains against the will of its holder, outside opening hours, in the premises of a public or private legal person, professional office, or in a commercial establishment or premises open to the public, shall be punished with a fine of one to three months.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

3. Anyone who, with violence or intimidation, enters or remains against the will of its holder in the premises of a public or private legal person, professional office, or in a commercial establishment or premises open to the public, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years.

###### Artículo 204.

The authority or public official who, outside the cases permitted by law and without legal cause for an offense, commits any of the acts described in the two preceding articles, shall be punished with the penalty provided respectively therein, in its upper half, and absolute disqualification from six to twelve years.

#### TÍTULO XI

Offenses against honor

##### CAPÍTULO I

On calumny

###### Artículo 205.

Calumny is the imputation of an offense made with knowledge of its falsity or reckless disregard for the truth.

###### Artículo 206.

Calumnies shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years or a fine of twelve to 24 months, if disseminated with publicity and, otherwise, with a fine of six to 12 months.

###### Artículo 207.

The accused of the offense of calumny shall be exempt from all penalty by proving the criminal act that he or she had imputed.

##### CAPÍTULO II

On insult

###### Artículo 208.

An insult is an action or expression that injures the dignity of another person, damaging their reputation or offending their self-esteem.

Only those insults which, by their nature, effects and circumstances, are considered by public perception to be serious shall constitute an offense, without prejudice to the provisions of section 4 of article 173.

Insults consisting of the imputation of facts shall not be considered serious, except when they have been carried out with knowledge of their falsity or reckless disregard for the truth.

###### Artículo 209.

Serious insults made with publicity shall be punished with a fine of six to fourteen months and, otherwise, with a fine of three to seven months.

###### Artículo 210.

The accused of insult shall be exempt from liability by proving the truth of the imputations when these are directed against public officials regarding facts concerning the exercise of their duties or relating to the commission of administrative infractions.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

##### CAPÍTULO III

General provisions

###### Artículo 211.

Calumny and insult shall be deemed to have been made with publicity when they are disseminated by means of print, broadcasting or by any other means of similar effect.

###### Artículo 212.

In the cases referred to in the preceding article, the natural or legal person owning the media outlet through which the calumny or insult has been disseminated shall be jointly and severally liable in civil terms.

###### Artículo 213.

If the calumny or insult were committed for payment, reward or promise, the Courts shall impose, in addition to the penalties established for the offenses in question, the special disqualification provided for in articles 42 or 45 of this Code, for a period of six months to two years.

###### Artículo 214.

If the accused of calumny or insult acknowledges before the judicial authority the falsity or lack of certainty of the imputations and retracts them, the Judge or Court shall impose the immediately lower penalty in degree and may refrain from imposing the disqualification penalty established by the preceding article.

The Judge or Court before whom the acknowledgment is made shall order that testimony of the retraction be delivered to the offended party and, if the latter requests it, shall order its publication in the same medium in which the calumny or insult was made, in a space identical or similar to that in which its dissemination occurred and within the period established by the sentencing Judge or Court.

###### Artículo 215.

1. No one shall be punished for calumny or insult except by virtue of a complaint by the person offended by the offense or their legal representative. Proceedings shall be initiated ex officio when the offense is directed against a public official, authority or agent thereof regarding facts concerning the exercise of their duties.

2. No one may bring an action for calumny or insult made in judicial proceedings without prior permission of the Judge or Court having or having had jurisdiction over them.

3. The pardon of the offended person extinguishes the criminal action, without prejudice to the provisions of article 130.1.5.º, second paragraph of this Code.

###### Artículo 216.

In the offenses of calumny or insult, reparation of the damage is considered to also include the publication or dissemination of the conviction, at the expense of the person convicted of such offenses, at the time and in the manner that the Judge or Court considers most appropriate for such purpose, having heard both parties.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

#### TÍTULO XII

Offenses against family relations

##### CAPÍTULO I

On illegal marriages

###### Artículo 217.

Anyone who contracts a second or subsequent marriage, knowing that the previous one legally subsists, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to one year.

###### Artículo 218.

1. Anyone who, in order to harm the other contracting party, enters into an invalid marriage shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years.

2. The offender shall be exempt from penalty if the marriage is subsequently validated.

###### Artículo 219.

1. Anyone who authorizes a marriage in which there is a ground for nullity known or reported in the file shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years and special disqualification for public employment or office from two to six years.

2. If the ground for nullity were dispensable, the penalty shall be suspension from public employment or office from six months to two years.

##### CAPÍTULO II

On simulation of birth and alteration of paternity, status or condition of a minor

###### Artículo 220.

1. The simulation of a birth shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years.

2. The same penalty shall be imposed on anyone who conceals or delivers to third parties a person under eighteen years of age in order to alter or modify their filiation.

3. The substitution of one child for another shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years.

4. Ascendants, by nature or by adoption, who commit the acts described in the three preceding sections may also be punished with the penalty of special disqualification for the exercise of parental authority that they hold over the supposed, concealed, delivered or substituted child or descendant, and, where applicable, over the other children or descendants for a period of four to ten years.

5. Substitutions of one child for another that occur in health or social-health centers due to gross negligence of those responsible for their identification and custody shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to one year.

###### Artículo 221.

1. Those who, through economic compensation, deliver to another person a child, descendant or any minor even if there is no relationship of filiation or kinship, evading the legal procedures of guardianship, foster care or adoption, with the purpose of establishing a relationship analogous to that of filiation, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years and special disqualification from exercising parental authority, guardianship, curatorship or custody for a period of four to ten years.

2. The person who receives them and the intermediary shall be punished with the same penalty, even if the delivery of the minor was carried out in a foreign country.

3. If the acts were committed using nurseries, schools or other premises or establishments where children are housed, the guilty parties shall be subject to the penalty of

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

special disqualification from exercising the aforementioned activities for a period of two to six years and the temporary or permanent closure of the establishments may be ordered. In the case of temporary closure, the period may not exceed five years.

###### Artículo 222.

The educator, physician, authority or public official who, in the exercise of their profession or position, carries out the conducts described in the two previous articles, shall incur the penalty indicated therein and, in addition, special disqualification from employment or public office, profession or trade, for two to six years.

For the purposes of this article, the term physician includes doctors, midwives, nursing staff and any other person who performs a health or social-health activity.

##### CAPÍTULO III

Of crimes against family rights and duties

###### Sección 1.ª Of the breach of custody duties and the inducement

of minors to abandon their domicile

###### Artículo 223.

Whoever, having custody of a minor or a person with disability in need of special protection, does not present them to their parents or guardians without justification for it, when required by them, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years, without prejudice to the facts constituting another more serious crime.

###### Artículo 224.

Whoever induces a minor or a person with disability in need of special protection to abandon the family domicile, or place where they reside with the consent of their parents, guardians or custodians, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years.

The parent who induces their minor child to violate the custody regime established by the judicial or administrative authority shall incur the same penalty.

###### Artículo 225.

When the person responsible for the crimes provided for in the two previous articles returns the minor or the person with disability in need of special protection to their domicile or residence, or deposits them in a known and safe place, without having subjected them to vexations, abuse or any criminal act, nor having put their life, health, physical integrity or sexual freedom at risk, the act shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to one year or a fine of six to 24 months, provided that the whereabouts of the minor or the person with disability in need of special protection has been communicated to their parents, guardians or custodians, or the absence has not exceeded 24 hours.

###### Sección 2.ª Of the abduction of minors

###### Artículo 225 bis.

1. The parent who without justified cause abducts their minor child shall be punished with imprisonment from two to four years and special disqualification from exercising parental authority for a period of four to ten years.

2. For the purposes of this article, abduction is considered:

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

1.º The transfer of a minor from their habitual place of residence without the consent of the other parent or of the persons or institutions to whom their care or custody was entrusted.

2.º The retention of a minor in serious breach of the duty established by judicial or administrative resolution.

3. When the minor is transferred outside of Spain or any condition is required for their return, the penalty indicated in paragraph 1 shall be imposed in its upper half.

4. When the abductor has communicated the place of stay to the other parent or to whomever legally corresponds their care within twenty-four hours following the abduction with the commitment of immediate return that is effectively carried out, or the absence has not exceeded said period of twenty-four hours, they shall be exempt from penalty.

If the return is made, without the communication referred to in the preceding paragraph, within fifteen days following the abduction, the penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years shall be imposed.

These periods shall be computed from the date of the report of the abduction.

5. The penalties indicated in this article shall be imposed equally on the ascendants of the minor and on the relatives of the parent up to the second degree of consanguinity or affinity who incur in the conducts previously described.

###### Sección 3.ª Of the abandonment of family, minors or persons with disability

in need of special protection.

###### Artículo 226.

1. Whoever fails to fulfill the legal duties of assistance inherent to parental authority, guardianship, custody or foster care or to provide the necessary assistance legally established for the sustenance of their descendants, ascendants or spouse, who are in need, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to six months or a fine of six to 12 months.

2. The Judge or Court may impose, with reasons, on the offender the penalty of special disqualification from exercising parental authority, guardianship, custody or foster care for a period of four to ten years.

###### Artículo 227.

1. Whoever fails to pay for two consecutive months or four non-consecutive months any type of economic benefit in favor of their spouse or their children, established in a judicially approved agreement or judicial resolution in cases of legal separation, divorce, declaration of nullity of marriage, filiation proceedings, or alimony proceedings in favor of their children, shall be punished with imprisonment from three months to one year or a fine of six to 24 months.

2. Whoever fails to pay any other economic benefit established jointly or solely in the cases provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with the same penalty.

3. The reparation of the damage arising from the crime shall always entail the payment of the amounts owed.

###### Artículo 228.

The crimes provided for in the two previous articles shall only be prosecuted upon complaint by the aggrieved person or their legal representative. When the latter is a minor, a person with disability in need of special protection or a helpless person, the Public Prosecutor may also file a complaint.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Artículo 229.

1. The abandonment of a minor or a person with disability in need of special protection by the person in charge of their custody shall be punished with imprisonment from one to two years.

2. If the abandonment is carried out by parents, guardians or legal custodians, the penalty of imprisonment from eighteen months to three years shall be imposed.

3. The penalty of imprisonment from two to four years shall be imposed when, due to the circumstances of the abandonment, the life, health, physical integrity or sexual freedom of the minor or the person with disability in need of special protection has been put in concrete danger, without prejudice to punishing the act accordingly if it constitutes another more serious crime.

###### Artículo 230.

The temporary abandonment of a minor or of a person with a disability in need of special protection shall be punished, in the respective cases, with penalties one degree lower than those provided in the preceding article.

###### Artículo 231.

1. Whoever, having in his charge the upbringing or education of a minor or of a person with a disability in need of special protection, delivers him to a third party or to a public establishment without the consent of the person who entrusted him to him, or of the authority, in default thereof, shall be punished with a fine of six to twelve months.

2. If the delivery has placed in concrete danger the life, health, physical integrity or sexual freedom of the minor or of the person with a disability in need of special protection, the penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years shall be imposed.

###### Artículo 232.

1. Those who use or lend minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection for the practice of begging, even if it is concealed, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to one year.

2. If for the purposes of the preceding paragraph trafficking in minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection is carried out, violence or intimidation is used against them, or substances harmful to their health are supplied to them, the penalty of imprisonment from one to four years shall be imposed.

###### Artículo 233.

1. The Judge or Court, if it deems it appropriate in view of the circumstances of the minor, may impose on those responsible for the offenses provided in Articles 229 to 232 the penalty of special disqualification for the exercise of parental authority or of the rights of guardianship, tutorship, curatorship or foster care for a period of four to ten years.

2. If the guilty party held the guardianship of the minor by virtue of his status as a public official, he shall also be subject to the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of two to six years.

3. In any case, the Public Prosecutor shall request from the competent authority the appropriate measures for the proper custody and protection of the minor.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

#### TITLE XIII

Offenses against property and against the socio-economic order

##### CHAPTER I

Of thefts

###### Artículo 234.

1. Whoever, with intent to profit, takes movable property belonging to others without the consent of its owner shall be punished, as guilty of theft, with imprisonment from six to eighteen months if the amount of what was stolen exceeds 400 euros.

2. A fine of one to three months shall be imposed if the amount of what was stolen does not exceed 400 euros, unless any of the circumstances of Article 235 are present. However, in the event that the guilty party has been convicted by final judgment at least for three offenses included in this Title, even if they are of a minor nature, provided they are of the same nature and the cumulative amount of the offenses exceeds 400 €, the penalty of paragraph 1 of this article shall be imposed.

Canceled records or those that should be canceled shall not be taken into account.

3. The penalties established in the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed in their upper half when, in the commission of the act, the alarm or security devices installed on the stolen items have been neutralized, eliminated or rendered useless by any means.

###### Artículo 235.

1. Theft shall be punished with imprisonment from one to three years:

1.º When things of artistic, historical, cultural or scientific value are stolen. 2.º When they are items of basic necessity and a supply shortage situation is caused.

3.º When they are conduits, cabling, equipment or components of electric supply, hydrocarbon or telecommunications service infrastructures, or other things intended for the provision of services of general interest, and serious damage is caused to them.

4.º When they are agricultural or livestock products, or the instruments or means used to obtain them, provided that the offense is committed on agricultural or livestock farms and serious damage is caused to them.

5.º When it is particularly serious, taking into account the value of the stolen items, or particularly significant damage is caused.

6.º When it places the victim or his family in a serious economic situation or has been carried out by abusing their personal circumstances or their situation of helplessness, or by taking advantage of the occurrence of an accident or the existence of a risk or general danger to the community that has weakened the defense of the injured party or facilitated the unpunished commission of the offense.

7.º When, upon committing the offense, the guilty party has been convicted by final judgment at least for three offenses included in this Title, provided they are of the same nature. Canceled records or those that should be canceled shall not be taken into account.

8.º When minors under sixteen years of age are used to commit the offense. 9.º When the guilty party or parties participate in the acts as members of a criminal organization or group engaged in the commission of offenses included in this Title, provided they are of the same nature.

2. The penalty indicated in the preceding paragraph shall be imposed in its upper half when two or more of the circumstances provided therein are present.

###### Artículo 236.

1. Whoever, being the owner of a movable thing or acting with his consent, takes it from the person who lawfully possesses it, to the detriment of that person or of a third party, shall be punished with a fine of three to twelve months.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

2. If the value of the stolen item does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

##### CHAPTER II

Of robberies

###### Artículo 237.

Those who, with intent to profit, seize movable property belonging to others by using force on things to access or leave the place where they are located or violence or intimidation on persons, whether when committing the offense, to protect the escape, or on those who come to the aid of the victim or who pursue them, are guilty of the offense of robbery.

###### Artículo 238.

Those who execute the act when any of the following circumstances are present are guilty of the offense of robbery with force on things:

1.º Scaling.

2.º Breaking through a wall, ceiling or floor, or breaking a door or window.

3.º Breaking cabinets, chests or other types of closed or sealed furniture or objects, or forcing their locks or discovering their combinations to extract their contents, whether at the place of the robbery or outside it.

4.º Use of false keys.

5.º Disabling of specific alarm or guard systems.

###### Artículo 239.

The following shall be considered false keys:

- 1. Lock picks or other similar instruments.
- 2. Legitimate keys lost by the owner or obtained by means constituting a criminal offense.
- 3. Any others that are not those intended by the owner to open the lock violated by the offender.

For the purposes of this article, cards, whether magnetic or perforated, remote opening controls or instruments, and any other technological instrument of similar efficacy are considered keys.

###### Artículo 240.

1. The person guilty of robbery with force on things shall be punished with imprisonment from one to three years.

2. Imprisonment from two to five years shall be imposed when any of the circumstances provided in Article 235 are present.

###### Artículo 241.

1. Robbery committed in an inhabited house, building or premises open to the public, or in any of their outbuildings, shall be punished with a prison sentence of two to five years.

If the acts were committed in an establishment open to the public, or in any of its outbuildings, outside opening hours, a prison sentence of one to five years shall be imposed.

2. An inhabited house is considered to be any dwelling that constitutes the residence of one or more persons, even if they are accidentally absent from it when the robbery takes place.

3. Outbuildings of an inhabited house or of a building or premises open to the public are considered to be its courtyards, garages and other departments or enclosed sites adjacent to the building and in interior communication with it, and with which they form a physical unit.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

4. A prison sentence of two to six years shall be imposed when the acts referred to in the preceding paragraphs are particularly serious, taking into account the manner of commission of the offense or the damage caused and, in any case, when any of the circumstances set forth in Article 235 occur.

###### Artículo 242.

1. The perpetrator of robbery with violence or intimidation against persons shall be punished with a prison sentence of two to five years, without prejudice to the sentence that may correspond to the acts of physical violence committed.

2. When the robbery is committed in an inhabited house, building or premises open to the public or in any of their outbuildings, a prison sentence of three years and six months to five years shall be imposed.

3. The sentences indicated in the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed in their upper half when the offender makes use of weapons or other equally dangerous means, whether in committing the offense or to protect the escape, and when attacking those who come to the aid of the victim or those who pursue him.

4. In view of the lesser degree of violence or intimidation exercised and also considering the remaining circumstances of the act, the sentence one degree lower than that provided in the preceding paragraphs may be imposed.

##### CAPÍTULO III

Of extortion

###### Artículo 243.

Whoever, with intent to gain, forces another, with violence or intimidation, to perform or omit a legal act or transaction to the detriment of his property or that of a third party, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to five years, without prejudice to those that may be imposed for the acts of physical violence committed.

##### CAPÍTULO IV

Of robbery and unauthorized use of vehicles

###### Artículo 244.

1. Whoever takes or uses without due authorization another person's motor vehicle or moped, without intent to appropriate it, shall be punished with a sentence of community service from thirty-one to ninety days or a fine of two to twelve months, if it is returned, directly or indirectly, within a period not exceeding forty-eight hours, provided that, in no case, the sentence imposed may be equal to or greater than that which would correspond if the vehicle were permanently appropriated.

2. If the act is carried out using force against things, the sentence shall be applied in its upper half.

3. If restitution is not made within the specified period, the act shall be punished as theft or robbery in their respective cases.

4. If the act is committed with violence or intimidation against persons, the sentences of Article 242 shall be imposed in any case.

##### CAPÍTULO V

Of usurpation

###### Artículo 245.

1. Whoever, with violence or intimidation against persons, occupies an immovable thing or usurps an immovable real right belonging to another, shall be imposed, in addition to the sentences incurred for the violence exercised, a prison sentence of one to two years, which shall be set taking into account the benefit obtained and the damage caused.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

2. Whoever occupies, without due authorization, another person's immovable property, dwelling or building that does not constitute a residence, or remains in them against the will of its owner, shall be punished with a fine of three to six months.

###### Artículo 246.

1. Whoever alters boundaries or limits of towns or estates or any kind of markers or boundary stones intended to fix the limits of properties or demarcations of adjacent lands, whether in public or private ownership, shall be punished with a fine of three to eighteen months.

2. If the benefit obtained does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

###### Artículo 247.

1. Whoever, without being authorized, diverts water for public or private use from its course, or from its natural or artificial reservoir, shall be punished with a fine of three to six months.

2. If the benefit obtained does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

##### CAPÍTULO VI

Of frauds

###### Sección 1.ª Of swindles

###### Artículo 248.

Those who, with intent to gain, use sufficient deceit to produce error in another, inducing him to perform an act of disposition to his own detriment or that of another, commit swindle. Those guilty of swindle shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years. For the determination of the sentence, account shall be taken of the amount defrauded, the economic loss caused to the injured party, the relations between the latter and the defrauder, the means employed by the latter, and any other circumstances that may serve to assess the seriousness of the offense.

If the amount defrauded does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

###### Artículo 249.

1. Also considered guilty of swindle and shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years:

- a) Those who, with intent to gain, by obstructing or improperly interfering with the operation of an information system or by improperly introducing, altering, deleting, transmitting or suppressing computer data or by using any other computer manipulation or similar device, achieve an unconsented transfer of any asset to the detriment of another.
- b) Those who, by fraudulently using credit or debit cards, travelers' checks or any other payment instrument, material or immaterial, other than cash or the data contained in any of them, carry out operations of any kind to the detriment of their holder or of a third party.
  - 2. With the same sentence provided in the preceding paragraph shall be punished:
- a) Those who manufacture, import, obtain, possess, transport, trade or otherwise provide to third parties devices, instruments or data or computer programs, or any other means specifically designed or adapted for the commission of the swindles provided for in this article.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

b) Those who, for their fraudulent use, steal, appropriate or unlawfully acquire credit or debit cards, travelers' checks or any other payment instrument, material or immaterial, other than cash.

3. The sentence in its lower half shall be imposed on those who, for their fraudulent use and knowing that they were unlawfully obtained, possess, acquire, transfer, distribute or make available to third parties credit or debit cards, travelers' checks or any other payment instruments, material or immaterial, other than cash.

###### Article 250.

1. The crime of fraud shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from one to six years and a fine of six to twelve months, when:

1.º It concerns things of basic necessity, housing or other goods of recognized social utility.

2.º It is perpetrated by abusing another's signature, or by removing, concealing or rendering useless, in whole or in part, any process, file, protocol or public or official document of any kind.

3.º It concerns property that forms part of the artistic, historical, cultural or scientific heritage.

4.º It is of particular gravity, considering the extent of the harm and the economic situation in which it leaves the victim or their family.

5.º The value of the fraud exceeds 50,000 euros, or affects a large number of persons.

6.º It is committed by abusing the personal relationships existing between victim and defrauder, or the latter takes advantage of their business or professional credibility.

7.º Procedural fraud is committed. Those who, in judicial proceedings of any kind, manipulate the evidence on which they intend to base their pleadings or employ other analogous procedural fraud, causing error in the judge or court and leading them to issue a resolution that harms the economic interests of the other party or a third party, incur the same.

8.º At the time of committing the offense, the offender had been finally convicted of at least three crimes included in this Chapter. Canceled records or those that should be canceled shall not be taken into account.

2. If the circumstances included in numerals 4.º, 5.º, 6.º or 7.º concur with that of numeral 1.º of the preceding paragraph, the penalties of imprisonment from four to eight years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months shall be imposed. The same penalty shall be imposed when the value of the fraud exceeds 250,000 euros.

###### Article 251.

The following shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from one to four years:

1.º Whoever, falsely attributing to themselves power of disposition over a movable or immovable thing which they lack, either by never having had it or by having already exercised it, alienates, encumbers or leases it to another, to the detriment of the latter or a third party. 2.º Whoever disposes of a movable or immovable thing concealing the existence of any encumbrance on it, or whoever, having alienated it as free, encumbers or alienates it again before the final transfer to the acquirer, to the detriment of the latter or a third party.

3.º Whoever executes a simulated contract to the detriment of another.

###### Article 251 bis.

When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the crimes included in this Section, the following penalties shall be imposed on it:

- a) A fine of three to five times the amount defrauded, if the crime committed by the natural person carries a penalty of imprisonment of more than five years.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

b) A fine of two to four times the amount defrauded, in other cases. Taking into account the rules established in article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33.

###### Section 2.ª On unfaithful administration

###### Article 252.

1. Those who, having powers to administer another's property, derived from law, entrusted by authority or assumed through a legal transaction, infringe them by exceeding their exercise and, in that manner, cause harm to the property administered, shall be punished with the penalties of article 248 or, as applicable, those of article 250.

2. If the amount of the patrimonial harm does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

###### Section 2.ª bis On misappropriation

###### Article 253.

1. Those who, to the detriment of another, appropriate for themselves or for a third party, money, effects, securities or any other movable thing, which they had received on deposit, commission, or custody, or which had been entrusted to them by virtue of any other title that creates the obligation to deliver or return them, or deny having received them, shall be punished with the penalties of article 248 or, as applicable, of article 250, unless they were already punished with a more severe penalty in another provision of this Code.

2. If the amount of what is misappropriated does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

###### Article 254.

1. Whoever, outside the cases of the preceding article, appropriates another's movable thing, shall be punished with a fine of three to six months. If it concerns things of artistic, historical, cultural or scientific value, the penalty shall be imprisonment from six months to two years.

2. If the amount of what is misappropriated does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to two months shall be imposed.

###### Section 3.ª On fraud of electricity and similar utilities

###### Article 255.

1. Whoever commits fraud using electricity, gas, water, telecommunications or other element, energy or utility belonging to another, by any of the following means, shall be punished with a fine of three to twelve months:

1.º Using mechanisms installed to commit the fraud.

2.º Maliciously altering the indications or metering devices.

3.º Employing any other clandestine means.

2. If the amount of what is defrauded does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

###### Article 256.

1. Whoever uses any telecommunications terminal equipment, without the consent of its holder, and causing economic harm to the latter, shall be punished with a fine of three to twelve months.

2. If the amount of the harm caused does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

##### CHAPTER VII

Frustration of enforcement

###### Article 257.

1. The following shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from one to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months:

1.º Whoever absconds with their property to the detriment of their creditors.

2.º Whoever with the same purpose performs any act of patrimonial disposition or generating obligations that delays, hinders or prevents the effectiveness of an attachment or enforcement or collection procedure, judicial, extrajudicial or administrative, initiated or foreseeable to be initiated.

2. Whoever performs acts of disposition, contracts obligations that diminish their patrimony or conceals by any means elements of their patrimony on which enforcement could be carried out, with the purpose of evading payment of civil liabilities arising from a crime they have committed or for which they should be liable, shall be punished with the same penalty.

3. The provisions of this article shall apply whatever the nature or origin of the obligation or debt whose satisfaction or payment is attempted to be evaded, including the economic rights of workers, and regardless of whether the creditor is an individual or any legal person, public or private.

Notwithstanding the foregoing, in the event that the debt or obligation that is attempted to be evaded is of public law and the creditor is a public legal person, or it concerns pecuniary obligations arising from the commission of a crime against the Public Treasury or Social Security, the penalty to be imposed shall be imprisonment from one to six years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

4. The penalties provided for in this article shall be imposed in their upper half in the cases provided for in numerals 5.º or 6.º of paragraph 1 of article 250.

5. This crime shall be prosecuted even when insolvency proceedings are initiated after its commission.

###### Article 258.

1. A penalty of imprisonment from three months to one year or a fine of six to eighteen months shall be imposed on whoever, in a judicial or administrative enforcement procedure, presents to the authority or official in charge of the enforcement an incomplete or false list of assets or property, and thereby delays, hinders or prevents the satisfaction of the creditor. The list of assets or property shall be considered incomplete when the debtor under enforcement uses or enjoys assets owned by third parties and does not provide sufficient justification of the right that supports such enjoyment and the conditions to which it is subject.

2. The same penalty shall be imposed when the debtor, having been required to do so, fails to provide the list of assets or property referred to in the preceding paragraph.

3. The offenses referred to in this article shall not be prosecutable if the perpetrator, before the authority or official had discovered the false or incomplete nature of the submitted statement, appeared before them and presented a truthful and complete statement of assets or property.

###### Article 258 bis.

A penalty of imprisonment from three to six months or a fine of six to twenty-four months shall be imposed, unless already punished with a more severe penalty in another provision of this Code, on those who make use of assets seized by public authority that have been placed in deposit without being authorized to do so.

###### Article 258 ter.

When in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis a legal person is liable for the offenses included in this Chapter, the following penalties shall be imposed:

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

a) Fine of two to five years, if the offense committed by the natural person carries a penalty of imprisonment of more than five years.

b) Fine of one to three years, if the offense committed by the natural person carries a penalty of imprisonment of more than two years not included in the preceding subparagraph.

c) Fine of six months to two years, in all other cases.

Taking into account the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b to g of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

##### CHAPTER VII BIS

On punishable insolvencies

###### Article 259.

1. A penalty of imprisonment from one to four years and a fine of eight to twenty-four months shall be imposed on whoever, being in a situation of current or imminent insolvency, carries out any of the following conducts:

1.ª Conceals, damages or destroys the assets or property elements that are included, or that would have been included, in the insolvency estate at the time of its opening.

2.ª Performs acts of disposition through the delivery or transfer of money or other property assets, or through the assumption of debts, that are not proportionate to the debtor's financial situation or income, and that lack economic or business justification.

3.ª Carries out sales transactions or service provisions for a price lower than their acquisition or production cost, and that in the circumstances of the case lack economic justification.
