4.ª Simulates third-party credits or proceeds to recognize fictitious credits. 5.ª Participates in speculative businesses, when this lacks economic justification and proves, in the circumstances of the case and in view of the economic activity carried out, contrary to the duty of diligence in the management of economic affairs.

6.ª Fails to comply with the legal duty to keep accounts, keeps double accounts, or commits irregularities in their keeping that are relevant to understanding their financial or property situation. The destruction or alteration of accounting books shall also be punishable when this significantly hinders or prevents the understanding of their financial or property situation.

7.ª Conceals, destroys or alters the documentation that the entrepreneur is obliged to preserve before the expiration of the period to which this legal duty extends, when this hinders or makes impossible the examination or assessment of the debtor's actual economic situation.

8.ª Prepares the annual accounts or accounting books in a manner contrary to the regulations governing commercial accounting, in such a way that the examination or assessment of the debtor's actual economic situation is hindered or made impossible, or fails to comply with the duty to prepare the balance sheet or inventory within the deadline.

9.ª Carries out any other active or omissive conduct that constitutes a serious breach of the duty of diligence in the management of economic affairs and to which a decrease in the debtor's assets is attributable or by means of which the debtor's actual economic situation or business activity is concealed.

2. The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever, through any of the conducts referred to in the preceding paragraph, causes their situation of insolvency.

3. When the acts have been committed through negligence, a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years or a fine of twelve to twenty-four months shall be imposed.

4. This offense shall only be prosecutable when the debtor has ceased to regularly fulfill their enforceable obligations or their insolvency has been declared.

5. This offense and the individual offenses related to it, committed by the debtor or person who has acted on their behalf, may be prosecuted without waiting for the conclusion of the

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

insolvency proceedings and without prejudice to their continuation. The amount of civil liability arising from such offenses shall be incorporated, where applicable, into the estate.

6. In no case shall the classification of the insolvency in the insolvency proceedings bind the criminal jurisdiction.

###### Article 259 bis.

The acts referred to in the preceding article shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from two to six years and a fine of eight to twenty-four months, when any of the following circumstances occur:

1.ª When patrimonial harm is or may be caused to a generality of persons or may place them in a serious economic situation.

2.ª When economic harm exceeding 600,000 euros is caused to any of the creditors.

3.ª When at least half of the amount of the insolvency credits has as holders the Public Treasury, whether state, regional, local or foral, and Social Security.

###### Article 260.

1. A penalty of six months to three years of imprisonment or a fine of eight to twenty-four months shall be imposed on the debtor who, being in a situation of current or imminent insolvency, favors one of the creditors by performing an act of patrimonial disposition or generating obligations intended to pay a non-enforceable credit or to provide them with a guarantee to which they were not entitled, when it involves an operation that lacks economic or business justification.

2. A penalty of one to four years of imprisonment and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months shall be imposed on the debtor who, once the insolvency application has been admitted for processing, without being authorized to do so either judicially or by the insolvency administrators, and outside the cases permitted by law, performs any act of patrimonial disposition or generating obligations, intended to pay one or more creditors, whether privileged or not, to the detriment of the rest.

###### Article 261.

Whoever in insolvency proceedings knowingly presents false data relating to the accounting status, with the purpose of improperly obtaining the declaration thereof, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from one to two years and a fine of six to 12 months.

###### Article 261 bis.

When, in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis, a legal person is responsible for the offenses included in this Chapter, the following penalties shall be imposed:

- a) Fine of two to five years, if the offense committed by the natural person has a prescribed prison sentence of more than five years.
- b) Fine of one to three years, if the offense committed by the natural person has a prescribed prison sentence of more than two years not included in the preceding subparagraph.
- c) Fine of six months to two years, in the remaining cases.

Taking into account the rules established in article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of subsection 7 of article 33.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

##### CHAPTER VIII

On price manipulation in public tenders and auctions

###### Article 262.

1. Those who solicit gifts or promises not to take part in a public tender or auction; those who attempt to deter bidders from it by means of threats, gifts, promises or any other artifice; those who collude with each other in order to alter the award price, or those who fraudulently breach or abandon the auction having obtained the award, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to three years and a fine of 12 to 24 months, as well as special disqualification from bidding in judicial auctions for between three and five years. If it concerns a tender or auction called by the Administrations or public entities, the penalty of special disqualification shall also be imposed on the agent and the person or company represented by him, which shall include, in any case, the right to contract with the public Administrations for a period of three to five years.

2. The judge or court may impose one or more of the consequences provided for in article 129 if the guilty party belongs to any company, organization or association, even of a temporary nature, that is engaged in carrying out such activities.

3. Current and former directors, de facto or de jure administrators, managers and other members of staff of any company, incorporated or in formation, who in that capacity have committed any of the acts provided for in this article, shall be exempt from criminal liability when they put an end to their participation in them and cooperate with the competent authorities in a full, continuous and diligent manner, providing information and evidence that they lacked, which are useful for the investigation, detection and punishment of the other persons involved, provided that the following conditions are met:

- a) They actively cooperate in this regard with the competition authority handling the case,
- b) these companies or natural persons have submitted an application for exemption from payment of the fine in accordance with the provisions of the Competition Defence Act,
- c) such application has been submitted at a time prior to that at which the current or former directors, de facto or de jure administrators, managers and other members of staff of the company, incorporated or in formation, have been informed that they are being investigated in connection with these acts,
- d) it involves active collaboration also with the judicial authority or the Public Prosecutor's Office, providing useful and concrete evidence to secure proof of the offense and identify other perpetrators.

##### CHAPTER IX

On damage

###### Article 263.

1. Whoever causes damage to another's property not covered by other titles of this Code shall be punished with a fine of six to twenty-four months, taking into account the economic condition of the victim and the amount of the damage.

If the amount of the damage caused does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

2. Whoever causes the damage referred to in the preceding subsection shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to three years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months if any of the following circumstances occur:

1.º That they are carried out to prevent the free exercise of authority or as a consequence of actions executed in the exercise of their functions, whether the offense is committed against public officials or against private individuals who, as witnesses or in any other way, have contributed or may contribute to the execution or application of the Laws or general provisions.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

2.º That infection or contagion of livestock is caused by any means.

3.º That poisonous or corrosive substances are used.

4.º That they affect property in the public or communal domain or use.

5.º That they ruin the injured party or place them in a serious economic situation.

6.º Damage of particular gravity has been caused or general interests have been affected.

###### Article 264.

1. Whoever, by any means, without authorization and in a serious manner, erases, damages, deteriorates, alters, deletes or renders inaccessible computer data, computer programs or electronic documents belonging to others, when the result produced is serious, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years.

2. A prison sentence of two to five years and a fine from one to ten times the damage caused shall be imposed when any of the following circumstances are present in the conduct described:

1.ª It has been committed within the framework of a criminal organization.

2.ª It has caused damage of particular gravity or affected a large number of computer systems.

3.ª The act has seriously prejudiced the functioning of essential public services or the provision of basic necessities.

4.ª The acts have affected the computer system of a critical infrastructure or have created a situation of serious danger to the security of the State, the European Union or a Member State of the European Union. For these purposes, critical infrastructure shall be considered an element, system or part thereof that is essential for the maintenance of vital functions of society, health, safety, protection and economic and social welfare of the population whose disruption or destruction would have a significant impact by not being able to maintain its functions.

5.ª The offense has been committed using any of the means referred to in article 264 ter.

If the acts have been of extreme gravity, the next higher penalty in degree may be imposed.

3. The penalties provided for in the preceding subsections shall be imposed, in their respective cases, in their upper half, when the acts have been committed through the illicit use of another person's personal data to facilitate access to the computer system or to gain the trust of a third party.

###### Article 264 bis.

1. Anyone who, without authorization and in a serious manner, obstructs or interrupts the operation of another person's computer system shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years:

- a) by performing any of the conducts referred to in the preceding article;
- b) by introducing or transmitting data; or
- c) by destroying, damaging, rendering unusable, deleting or replacing a computer system, telematic system or electronic information storage system.

If the acts have significantly harmed the normal activity of a company, business or a Public Administration, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half, and may reach the next degree.

2. A prison sentence of three to eight years and a fine of three to ten times the damage caused shall be imposed when any of the circumstances of paragraph 2 of the preceding article are present in the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph.

3. The penalties provided for in the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed, in their respective cases, in their upper half, when the acts have been committed through the unlawful use of another person's personal data to facilitate access to the computer system or to gain the trust of a third party.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Article 264 ter.

Anyone who, without being duly authorized, produces, acquires for use, imports or, in any manner, provides to third parties, with the intention of facilitating the commission of any of the offenses referred to in the two preceding articles, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to two years or a fine of three to eighteen months:

- a) a computer program, conceived or adapted primarily to commit any of the offenses referred to in the two preceding articles; or
- b) a computer password, an access code or similar data that allow access to all or part of an information system.

###### Article 264 quater.

When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the offenses included in the three preceding articles, the following penalties shall be imposed:

- a) A fine of two to five years or five to twelve times the value of the damage caused, if a higher amount results, when dealing with offenses punishable by a prison sentence of more than three years.
- b) A fine of one to three years or three to eight times the value of the damage caused, if a higher amount results, in all other cases.

Taking into account the rules established in article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set forth in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33.

###### Article 265.

Anyone who destroys, seriously damages, or renders unfit for service, even temporarily, military works, establishments or installations, warships, military aircraft, military transport or transmission means, war materiel, supplies or other means or resources assigned to the service of the Armed Forces or the Security Forces and Corps, shall be punished with a prison sentence of two to four years if the damage caused exceeds one thousand euros.

###### Article 266.

1. Anyone who commits the damage provided for in paragraph 1 of article 263 by means of fire, or by causing explosions, or by using any other means of similar destructive power or that generates a significant risk of explosion or of causing other damage of special gravity, or by endangering the life or physical integrity of persons, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to three years.

2. Anyone who commits the damage provided for in paragraph 2 of article 263, under any of the circumstances mentioned in the preceding paragraph, shall be punished with a prison sentence of three to five years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

3. Anyone who commits the damage provided for in articles 265, 323 and 560, under any of the circumstances mentioned in paragraph 1 of this article, shall be punished with a prison sentence of four to eight years.

4. In any of the cases provided for in the preceding paragraphs, when the damage is committed with the causing of explosions or the use of other means of similar destructive power and, in addition, the life or physical integrity of persons is endangered, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half.

In case of fire, the provisions of article 351 shall apply.

###### Article 267.

Damage caused by gross negligence in an amount exceeding 80,000 euros shall be punished with a fine of three to nine months, taking into account the magnitude thereof.

The offenses referred to in this article shall only be prosecutable upon complaint by the aggrieved person or their legal representative. The Public Prosecutor may also

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

file a complaint when the latter is a minor, a person with a disability in need of special protection or a helpless person.

In these cases, forgiveness by the offended person extinguishes the criminal action.

##### CHAPTER X

Common provisions to the preceding chapters

###### Article 268.

1. Spouses who are not legally or de facto separated or in judicial proceedings for separation, divorce or annulment of their marriage, and ascendants, descendants and siblings by birth or adoption, as well as relatives by affinity in the first degree if living together, are exempt from criminal liability and subject only to civil liability for property offenses caused against each other, provided that there is no violence or intimidation, or abuse of the victim's vulnerability, whether by reason of age or because the victim is a person with a disability.

2. This provision is not applicable to strangers who participate in the offense.

###### Article 269.

Incitement, conspiracy and proposal to commit the offenses of theft, extortion, fraud or misappropriation shall be punished with a penalty one or two degrees lower than that of the corresponding offense.

##### CHAPTER XI

Offenses relating to intellectual and industrial property, the market and consumers

###### Section 1. Offenses relating to intellectual property

###### Article 270.

1. Anyone who, with the intention of obtaining a direct or indirect economic benefit and to the detriment of a third party, reproduces, plagiarizes, distributes, publicly communicates or in any other way economically exploits, in whole or in part, a literary, artistic or scientific work or performance, or its transformation, interpretation or artistic execution fixed on any type of medium or communicated through any means, without the authorization of the holders of the corresponding intellectual property rights or their assignees, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

2. The same penalty shall be imposed on anyone who, in providing information society services, with the intention of obtaining a direct or indirect economic benefit, and to the detriment of a third party, facilitates in an active and non-neutral manner and without limiting themselves to merely technical processing, access to or location on the internet of works or performances subject to intellectual property without the authorization of the holders of the corresponding rights or their assignees, in particular by offering ordered and classified lists of links to the works and content referred to above, even if such links were initially provided by the recipients of their services.

3. In these cases, the judge or court shall order the removal of the works or performances that are the subject of the infringement. When, through an internet access portal or information society service, content subject to the intellectual property referred to in the preceding paragraphs is disseminated exclusively or predominantly, the interruption of the provision thereof shall be ordered, and the judge may adopt any precautionary measure aimed at protecting intellectual property rights. Exceptionally, when there is repetition of the conduct and when it proves to be a proportionate, efficient and effective measure, the blocking of the corresponding access may be ordered.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

4. In the cases referred to in paragraph 1, itinerant or merely occasional distribution or commercialization shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years.

However, taking into account the characteristics of the offender and the reduced amount of the economic benefit obtained or that could have been obtained, provided that none of the circumstances of Article 271 are present, the Judge may impose a fine of one to six months or community service from thirty-one to sixty days.

5. The following shall be punished with the penalties provided in the preceding paragraphs, in their respective cases:

- a) Those who intentionally export or store copies of the works, productions or performances referred to in the first two paragraphs of this article, including digital copies thereof, without the aforementioned authorization, when they are intended to be reproduced, distributed or publicly communicated.
- b) Those who intentionally import these products without such authorization, when they are intended to be reproduced, distributed or publicly communicated, whether they have a lawful or unlawful origin in their country of origin; however, the importation of the aforementioned products from a Member State of the European Union shall not be punishable when they have been acquired directly from the holder of the rights in that State, or with their consent.
- c) Those who favor or facilitate the performance of the conduct referred to in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this article by removing or modifying, without authorization from the holders of intellectual property rights or their assignees, the effective technological measures incorporated by them for the purpose of preventing or restricting their performance.
- d) Those who, with the intention of obtaining a direct or indirect economic benefit, with the purpose of facilitating third parties' access to a copy of a literary, artistic or scientific work, or its transformation, interpretation or artistic execution, fixed on any type of medium or communicated through any means, and without authorization from the holders of intellectual property rights or their assignees, circumvent or facilitate the circumvention of effective technological measures designed to prevent it.
  - 6. Anyone who manufactures, imports, puts into circulation or possesses for commercial purposes any means mainly conceived, produced, adapted or made to facilitate the unauthorized suppression or neutralization of any technical device that has been used to protect computer programs or any of the other works, interpretations or performances under the terms provided in the first two paragraphs of this article shall also be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years.

###### Article 271.

The penalty of imprisonment from two to six years, a fine of eighteen to thirty-six months and special disqualification from exercising the profession related to the offense committed, for a period of two to five years, shall be imposed when the offense of the preceding article is committed with any of the following circumstances:

- a) That the benefit obtained or that could have been obtained has special economic significance.
- b) That the facts are particularly serious, taking into account the value of the unlawfully produced objects, the number of works, or their transformation, execution or interpretation, unlawfully reproduced, distributed, communicated to the public or made available, or the special importance of the harm caused.
- c) That the offender belongs to an organization or association, even of a transitory nature, whose purpose is to carry out activities that infringe intellectual property rights.
- d) That minors under 18 years of age are used to commit these offenses.

###### Article 272.

1. The extent of civil liability arising from the offenses typified in the two preceding articles shall be governed by the provisions of the Intellectual Property Law relating to the cessation of unlawful activity and compensation for damages.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

2. In the case of a conviction, the Judge or Court may order its publication, at the expense of the offender, in an official gazette.

###### Section 2. Offenses relating to industrial property

###### Article 273.

1. Anyone who, for industrial or commercial purposes, without the consent of the holder of a patent or utility model and with knowledge of its registration, manufactures, imports, possesses, uses, offers or introduces into commerce objects protected by such rights shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine of 12 to 24 months.

2. The same penalties shall be imposed on anyone who, in the same manner, and for the aforementioned purposes, uses or offers the use of a process that is the subject of a patent, or possesses, offers, introduces into commerce, or uses the product directly obtained by the patented process.

3. Anyone who performs any of the acts typified in the first paragraph of this article under the same circumstances in relation to objects protected in favor of a third party by an industrial or artistic model or design or topography of a semiconductor product shall be punished with the same penalties.

###### Article 274.

1. Whoever, for industrial or commercial purposes, without the consent of the holder of an industrial property right registered in accordance with trademark legislation and with knowledge of the registration, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months,

a) manufactures, produces or imports products that incorporate a distinctive sign identical or confusable with that one, or

b) offers, distributes, or wholesales products that incorporate a distinctive sign identical or confusable with that one, or stores them for that purpose, when they are the same or similar products, services or activities for which the industrial property right is registered.

2. Whoever, for industrial or commercial purposes, without the consent of the holder of an industrial property right registered in accordance with trademark legislation and with knowledge of the registration, offers, distributes or retails, or provides services or carries out activities, that incorporate a distinctive sign identical or confusable with that one, when they are the same or similar products, services or activities for which the industrial property right is registered, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years.

The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever reproduces or imitates a distinctive sign identical or confusable with that one for its use in the commission of the conducts sanctioned in this article.

3. The itinerant or occasional sale of the products referred to in the preceding paragraphs shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years.

However, taking into account the characteristics of the guilty party and the reduced amount of the economic benefit obtained or that could have been obtained, provided that none of the circumstances of Article 276 are present, the Judge may impose a fine of one to six months or community service from thirty-one to sixty days.

4. Whoever, for agricultural or commercial purposes, without the consent of the holder of a plant variety right and with knowledge of its registration, produces or reproduces, conditions with a view to production or reproduction, offers for sale, sells or otherwise commercializes, exports or imports, or possesses for any of the aforementioned purposes, plant reproductive or multiplication material of a plant variety protected in accordance with national or European Union legislation on the protection of plant variety rights, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to three years.

Whoever performs any of the acts described in the preceding paragraph using, under the denomination of a protected plant variety, plant reproductive or multiplication material that does not belong to such variety, shall be punished with the same penalty.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Article 275.

The same penalties provided for in the preceding article shall be imposed on whoever intentionally and without being authorized to do so, uses in economic transactions a designation of origin or a geographical indication representative of a specific quality legally protected to distinguish the products covered by them, with knowledge of this protection.

###### Article 276.

Imprisonment from two to six years, a fine of eighteen to thirty-six months and special disqualification from the exercise of the profession related to the offense committed, for a period of two to five years, shall be imposed when any of the following circumstances is present:

- a) That the benefit obtained or that could have been obtained has special economic significance.
- b) That the acts are of special gravity, taking into account the value of the objects illegally produced, distributed, commercialized or offered, or the special importance of the damages caused.
- c) That the guilty party belongs to an organization or association, even of a transitory nature, whose purpose is to carry out activities infringing industrial property rights.
- d) That minors under 18 years of age are used to commit these offenses.

###### Article 277.

Whoever has intentionally disclosed the invention that is the subject of a secret patent application, in contravention of the provisions of patent legislation, provided that this is to the detriment of national defense, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine of six to twenty-four months.

###### Sección 3.ª Offenses relating to the market and consumers

###### Article 278.

1. Whoever, in order to discover a trade secret, seizes by any means data, written or electronic documents, computer media or other objects that refer to it, or uses any of the means or instruments indicated in paragraph 1 of Article 197, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

2. Imprisonment from three to five years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months shall be imposed if the discovered secrets are disseminated, disclosed or transferred to third parties.

3. The provisions of this article shall be understood without prejudice to the penalties that may correspond for the seizure or destruction of computer media.

###### Article 279.

The dissemination, disclosure or transfer of a trade secret carried out by whoever has a legal or contractual obligation to maintain confidentiality, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

If the secret is used for one's own benefit, the penalties shall be imposed in their lower half.

###### Article 280.

Whoever, with knowledge of its illicit origin, and without having taken part in its discovery, performs any of the conducts described in the two preceding articles, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Article 281.

1. Whoever withdraws from the market raw materials or basic necessities with the intention of creating a shortage in a sector thereof, forcing a price alteration, or seriously harming consumers, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

2. The penalty shall be imposed in its upper degree if the act is performed in situations of serious need or catastrophic situations.

###### Article 282.

Manufacturers or traders who, in their offers or advertising of products or services, make false allegations or manifest uncertain characteristics about them, in such a way that they may cause serious and manifest harm to consumers, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to one year or a fine of 12 to 24 months, without prejudice to the penalty that may be applicable for the commission of other offenses.

###### Artículo 282 bis.

Those who, as de facto or de jure administrators of a company issuing securities traded on securities markets, falsify the economic and financial information contained in the prospectuses for the issuance of any financial instruments or the information that the company must publish and disseminate in accordance with securities market legislation regarding its resources, activities and present and future business, with the purpose of attracting investors or depositors, placing any type of financial asset, or obtaining financing by any means, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to four years, without prejudice to the provisions of Article 308 of this Code.

In the event that the investment, deposit, placement of the asset or financing is actually obtained, with prejudice to the investor, depositor, acquirer of the financial assets or creditor, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half. If the damage caused were of notorious severity, the penalty to be imposed shall be one to six years of imprisonment and a fine of six to twelve months.

###### Artículo 283.

The penalties of imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine of six to eighteen months shall be imposed on those who, to the detriment of the consumer, invoice amounts higher than those for products or services whose cost or price is measured by automatic devices, by means of the alteration or manipulation thereof.

###### Artículo 284.

1. The penalty of imprisonment from six months to six years, a fine of two to five years, or from one to three times the benefit obtained or favored, or the damages avoided, if the resulting amount were higher, and special disqualification from intervening in the financial market as an actor, agent or intermediary or informer for a period of two to five years, shall be imposed on those who:

1.º Using violence, threat, deceit or any other artifice, alter the prices that should result from the free competition of products, goods, financial instruments, spot contracts on commodities related to them, reference indices, services or any other movable or immovable things that are the object of contracting, without prejudice to the penalty that may correspond to them for other crimes committed.

2.º By themselves, directly or indirectly or through a media outlet, by means of the internet or through the use of information and communication technologies, or by any other means, disseminate news or rumors or transmit false or misleading signals about persons or companies, knowingly offering economic data wholly or partially false in order to alter or preserve the listing price of a financial instrument or a spot contract on related commodities or to manipulate the

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

calculation of a reference index, when they obtain, for themselves or for a third party, a benefit, provided that any of the following circumstances occur:

- a) that said benefit was greater than two hundred and fifty thousand euros or damage of an identical amount was caused;
- b) that the amount of the funds used was greater than two million euros;
- c) that a serious impact on the integrity of the market was caused.

3.º Carry out transactions, transmit false or misleading signals, or give operation orders capable of providing false or misleading indications about the supply, demand or price of a financial instrument, a spot contract on related commodities or reference indices, or secure, using the same information, by themselves or in concert with others, a dominant position in the market for said instruments or contracts with the purpose of fixing their prices at abnormal or artificial levels, provided that any of the following circumstances occur:

- a) that as a consequence of their conduct they obtain, for themselves or for a third party, a benefit greater than two hundred and fifty thousand euros or cause damage of an identical amount;
- b) that the amount of the funds used was greater than two million euros;
- c) that a serious impact on the integrity of the market was caused.
  - 2. The penalty shall be imposed in its upper half if any of the following circumstances occur:

1.ª That the subject habitually engages in the aforementioned abusive practices.
2.ª That the benefit obtained, the loss avoided or the damage caused is of notorious importance.

3. If the person responsible for the act were a worker or employee of an investment services firm, credit institution, supervisory or regulatory authority, or governing body of regulated markets or trading venues, the penalties shall be imposed in their upper half.

###### Artículo 285.

1. Whoever directly or indirectly or through an intermediary carries out acts of acquisition, transmission or transfer of a financial instrument, or cancellation or modification of an order relating to a financial instrument, using inside information to which he or she had reserved access under the terms of paragraph 4, or recommends to a third party the use of said inside information for any of those acts, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to six years, a fine of two to five years, or from one to three times the benefit obtained or favored or the damages avoided if the resulting amount were higher, and special disqualification from the exercise of the profession or activity from two to five years, provided that any of the following circumstances occur:

- a) that, as a consequence of their conduct they obtain, for themselves or for a third party, a benefit greater than five hundred thousand euros or cause damage of an identical amount;
- b) that the value of the financial instruments used was greater than two million euros;
- c) that a serious impact on the integrity of the market was caused.
  - 2. The penalty shall be imposed in its upper half if any of the following circumstances occur:

1.ª That the subject habitually engages in the aforementioned practices of operations with inside information.

2.ª That the benefit obtained, the loss avoided or the damage caused is of notorious importance.

3. The penalties provided for in this article shall be imposed, in their respective cases, in their upper half if the person responsible for the act were a worker or employee of an

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

investment services firm, credit institution, supervisory or regulatory authority, or governing bodies of regulated markets or trading venues.

4. For the purposes of this article, it is understood that a person has reserved access to inside information when he or she is a member of the administrative, management or supervisory bodies of the issuer or of the participant in the emission rights market, participates in the capital of the issuer or of the participant in the emission rights market, knows it on the occasion of the exercise of his or her professional or business activity, or in the performance of his or her functions, and obtains it through a criminal activity.

5. The same penalties provided for in this article shall be imposed when the person responsible for the act, without having reserved access to inside information, obtains it in any manner other than those provided for in the preceding paragraph and uses it knowing that it is inside information.

###### Article 285 bis.

Outside the cases provided for in the preceding article, whoever possesses inside information and discloses it outside the normal exercise of their work, profession or functions, endangering the integrity of the market or the confidence of investors, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to four years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification for the exercise of the profession or activity from one to three years.

For the purposes of the provisions of this article, the disclosure of inside information in a market sounding shall be included when it has been carried out without observing the requirements provided for in European regulations on markets and financial instruments.

###### Article 285 ter.

The provisions of the three preceding articles shall extend to the financial instruments, contracts, conduct, operations and orders provided for in European and Spanish regulations on markets and financial instruments.

###### Article 285 quater.

The incitement, conspiracy and proposal to commit the offenses provided for in articles 284 to 285 bis shall be punished, respectively, with the penalty lower by one or two degrees.

###### Article 286.

1. Whoever, without the consent of the service provider and for commercial purposes, facilitates intelligible access to a sound or television broadcasting service, to interactive services provided remotely by electronic means, or supplies conditional access thereto, considered as an independent service, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine of six to 24 months, by means of:

1.º The manufacture, import, distribution, making available by electronic means, sale, rental, or possession of any equipment or computer program, not authorized in another Member State of the European Union, designed or adapted to make such access possible.

2.º The installation, maintenance or replacement of the equipment or computer programs mentioned in paragraph 1.º

2. Whoever, with intent to profit, alters or duplicates the identification number of telecommunications equipment, or markets equipment that has undergone fraudulent alteration, shall be punished with the same penalty.

3. Whoever, without intent to profit, facilitates to third parties the access described in paragraph 1, or by means of a public communication, commercial or not, provides information to a plurality of persons about the manner of achieving unauthorized access to a service or the use of a device or program, of those expressed in that same paragraph 1, inciting to achieve them, shall be imposed the fine penalty provided for therein.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

4. Whoever uses the equipment or programs that allow unauthorized access to conditional access services or telecommunications equipment shall be imposed the penalty provided for in article 255 of this Code regardless of the amount of the fraud.

###### Section 4.ª Crimes of corruption in business

###### Article 286 bis.

1. The director, administrator, employee or collaborator of a commercial company or a company who, directly or through an intermediary, receives, requests or accepts an unjustified benefit or advantage of any nature, or an offer or promise to obtain it, for themselves or for a third party, as consideration for unduly favoring another in the acquisition or sale of goods, or in the contracting of services or in commercial relations, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to four years, special disqualification for the exercise of industry or trade for a period of one to six years and a fine of the equivalent to three times the value of the benefit or advantage.

2. Whoever, directly or through an intermediary, promises, offers or grants to directors, administrators, employees or collaborators of a commercial company or a company, an unjustified benefit or advantage, of any nature, for them or for third parties, as consideration for unduly favoring them or a third party over others in the acquisition or sale of goods, contracting of services or in commercial relations, shall be punished with the same penalties.

3. The judges and courts, taking into account the amount of the benefit or the value of the advantage, and the significance of the functions of the guilty party, may impose the penalty lower in degree and reduce the fine at their prudent discretion.

4. The provisions of this article shall be applicable, in their respective cases, to the directors, administrators, employees or collaborators of a sports entity, whatever its legal form, as well as to athletes, referees or judges, with respect to those conducts that have the purpose of predetermining or deliberately and fraudulently altering the result of a test, match or sports competition of special economic or sporting relevance.

For these purposes, a sports competition of special economic relevance shall be considered one in which the majority of the participants therein receive any type of remuneration, compensation or economic income for their participation in the activity; and a sports competition of special sporting relevance, that which is classified in the annual sports calendar approved by the corresponding sports federation as an official competition of the highest category of the modality, specialty, or discipline in question.

5. For the purposes of this article, the provisions of article 297 shall apply.

###### Article 286 ter.

1. Those who, through the offer, promise or granting of any undue benefit or advantage, pecuniary or of another kind, corrupt or attempt to corrupt, directly or through an intermediary, a public authority or official for the benefit of the latter or a third party, or attend to their requests in this regard, with the purpose of having them act or refrain from acting in relation to the exercise of public functions to obtain or maintain a contract, business or any other competitive advantage in carrying out international economic activities, shall be punished, unless they were already punished with a more severe penalty in another provision of this Code, with imprisonment from three to six years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months, unless the benefit obtained is greater than the resulting amount, in which case the fine shall be from the equivalent to three times the amount of said benefit.

In addition to the penalties indicated, the perpetrator shall in any case be imposed the penalty of prohibition from contracting with the public sector, as well as the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and the right to enjoy tax and Social Security benefits or incentives, and the prohibition from participating in commercial transactions of public significance for a period of seven to twelve years.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

2. For the purposes of this article, public official shall be understood as those determined by articles 24 and 427.

###### Article 286 quater.

If the acts referred to in the articles of this Section are of particular gravity, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half, and may reach the degree above.

The acts shall be considered, in any case, of particular gravity when:

- a) the benefit or advantage has an especially high value,
- b) the action of the perpetrator is not merely occasional,
- c) they are acts committed within a criminal organization or group, or
- d) the object of the transaction concerns humanitarian goods or services or any others of basic necessity.

In the case of paragraph 4 of Article 286 bis, the acts shall also be considered of particular gravity when:

- a) they have the purpose of influencing the development of games of chance or betting; or
- b) they are committed in an official sporting competition of state scope classified as professional or in an international sporting competition.

###### Section 5.ª Common provisions to the preceding sections

###### Article 287.

1. To proceed for the offences provided for in Section 3ª of this Chapter, except those provided for in Articles 284 and 285, a complaint by the aggrieved person or their legal representatives shall be necessary. When the latter is a minor, a person with a disability in need of special protection or a helpless person, the Public Prosecutor's Office may also file a complaint.

2. The complaint required in the preceding paragraph shall not be necessary when the commission of the offence affects general interests or a plurality of persons.

###### Article 288.

In the cases provided for in the preceding articles, the publication of the judgment in the official gazettes shall be ordered and, if requested by the injured party, the judge or court may order its total or partial reproduction in any other media outlet, at the expense of the convicted person.

When in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the offences contained in this Chapter, the following penalties shall be imposed: 1.º In the case of the offences provided for in Articles 270, 271, 273, 274, 275, 276, 283 and 286:

- a) Fine of double to quadruple the benefit obtained, or that could have been obtained, if the offence committed by the natural person carries a penalty of imprisonment of more than two years.
- b) Fine of double to triple the benefit obtained, favoured or that could have been obtained, in the remaining cases.

2.º In the case of the offences provided for in Articles 277, 278, 279, 280, 281, 282, 282 bis, 284, 285, 285 bis, 285 quater and 286 bis to 286 quater:

- a) Fine of two to five years, or triple to quintuple the benefit obtained or that could have been obtained if the resulting amount were higher, when the offence committed by the natural person carries a penalty of more than two years of deprivation of liberty.
- b) Fine of six months to two years, or from the same amount to double the benefit obtained or that could have been obtained if the resulting amount were higher, in the remaining cases.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

3.º Having regard to the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

###### Article 288 bis.

In the cases provided for in Articles 281 and 284 of this Code, current and former directors, administrators de facto or de jure, managers and other staff members of any company, incorporated or in formation, who in that capacity have committed any of the acts provided for therein, shall be exempt from criminal liability when they put an end to their participation therein and cooperate with the competent authorities in a full, continuous and diligent manner, providing information and evidence which the latter lacked, which are useful for the investigation, detection and punishment of the other persons involved, provided that the following conditions are met:

- a) They actively cooperate in this regard with the competition authority handling the case,
- b) these companies or natural persons have submitted an application for exemption from payment of the fine in accordance with the provisions of the Competition Defence Act,
- c) such application has been submitted at a time prior to that at which the current and former directors, administrators de facto or de jure, managers and other staff members of any company, incorporated or in formation, who in that capacity have been informed that they are being investigated in relation to these acts,
- d) it is a matter of active collaboration also with the judicial authority or the Public Prosecutor's Office providing useful and concrete evidence to secure proof of the offence and identify other perpetrators.

##### CHAPTER XII

On the removal of one's own property from its social or cultural utility

###### Article 289.

Whoever by any means destroys, renders useless or damages one's own property of social or cultural utility, or in any way removes it from compliance with legal duties imposed in the interest of the community, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of three to five months or a fine of six to 10 months.

##### CHAPTER XIII

On corporate offences

###### Article 290.

Administrators, de facto or de jure, of a company incorporated or in formation, who falsify the annual accounts or other documents that must reflect the legal or economic situation of the entity, in a manner capable of causing economic damage to it, to any of its partners, or to a third party, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of one to three years and a fine of six to twelve months.

If the economic damage is actually caused, the penalties shall be imposed in their upper half.

###### Article 291.

Those who, taking advantage of their majority position in the General Meeting of shareholders or the administrative body of any company incorporated or in formation, impose abusive resolutions, with intent to profit themselves or others, to the detriment of the other partners, and without

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

bringing benefits to it, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of six months to three years or a fine from the same amount to triple the benefit obtained.

###### Article 292.

The same penalty as in the preceding article shall be imposed on those who impose or take advantage for themselves or for a third party, to the detriment of the company or any of its partners, of a harmful resolution adopted by a fictitious majority, obtained by abuse of signature in blank, by improper attribution of the right to vote to those who legally lack it, by unlawful denial of the exercise of this right to those who have it recognised by Law, or by any other similar means or procedure, and without prejudice to punishing the act accordingly if it constitutes another offence.

###### Article 293.

Administrators de facto or de jure of any company incorporated or in formation, who without legal cause deny or prevent a partner from exercising the rights of information, participation in the management or control of corporate activity, or preferential subscription of shares recognised by the Laws, shall be punished with a fine of six to twelve months.

###### Article 294.

Those who, as administrators de facto or de jure of any company incorporated or in formation, subject to or acting in markets subject to administrative supervision, deny or prevent the action of inspecting or supervising persons, bodies or entities, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of six months to three years or a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

In addition to the penalties provided for in the preceding paragraph, the judicial authority may order some of the measures provided for in Article 129 of this Code.

###### Article 295.

(Deleted)

###### Artículo 296.

1. The acts described in this chapter may only be prosecuted by complaint of the aggrieved person or their legal representative. When the latter is a minor, a person with a disability in need of special protection or a helpless person, the Public Prosecutor may also file a complaint.

2. The complaint required in the preceding paragraph shall not be necessary when the commission of the offence affects general interests or a plurality of persons.

###### Artículo 297.

For the purposes of this chapter, "company" means any cooperative, savings bank, mutual society, financial or credit institution, foundation, commercial company or any other entity of analogous nature that participates permanently in the market for the fulfilment of its purposes.

##### CAPÍTULO XIV

Receiving stolen goods and money laundering

###### Artículo 298.

1. A person who, with intent to profit and with knowledge of the commission of an offence against property or the socio-economic order, in which he has not intervened either as a principal or as an accomplice, assists those responsible in taking advantage of the proceeds thereof, or receives, acquires or conceals such proceeds, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to two years.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

A sentence of one to three years' imprisonment shall be imposed in the following cases:

- a) Where the items are of artistic, historical, cultural or scientific value.
- b) Where the items are essential goods, pipelines, cabling, equipment or components of electricity supply infrastructures or telecommunications services, or other items intended for the provision of services of general interest, agricultural or livestock products or the instruments or means used to obtain them.
- c) Where the acts are particularly serious, having regard to the value of the goods received or the damage that their removal would foreseeably have caused.
  - 2. These penalties shall be imposed in their upper half on anyone who receives, acquires or conceals the proceeds of the offence in order to traffic in them. If the trafficking is carried out using a commercial or industrial establishment or premises, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months shall also be imposed. In these cases, the judges or courts, having regard to the seriousness of the act and the personal circumstances of the offender, may also impose on him the penalty of special disqualification for the exercise of his profession or industry for a period of two to five years and order the temporary or permanent closure of the establishment or premises. If the closure is temporary, its duration may not exceed five years.
  - 3. In no case may a custodial sentence exceeding that assigned to the concealed offence be imposed. If the latter is punishable by a penalty of a different nature, the custodial sentence shall be replaced by a fine of 12 to 24 months, unless the concealed offence carries a penalty equal to or less than this; in that case, the guilty party shall be sentenced to the penalty for that offence in its lower half.

###### Artículo 299.

(Deleted)

###### Artículo 300.

The provisions of this chapter shall apply even where the principal or accomplice of the act from which the proceeds used originated was not criminally responsible or was personally exempt from punishment.

###### Artículo 301.

1. A person who acquires, possesses, uses, converts or transmits property, knowing that it has its origin in criminal activity, committed by him or by any third party, or performs any other act to conceal or disguise its illicit origin, or to assist the person who has participated in the offence or offences to evade the legal consequences of his acts, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to six years and a fine of the equivalent to three times the value of the property. In these cases, the judges or courts, having regard to the seriousness of the act and the personal circumstances of the offender, may also impose on him the penalty of special disqualification for the exercise of his profession or industry for a period of one to three years, and order the temporary or permanent closure of the establishment or premises. If the closure is temporary, its duration may not exceed five years.

The penalty shall be imposed in its upper half when the property has its origin in any of the offences related to the trafficking of toxic drugs, narcotics or psychotropic substances described in articles 368 to 372 of this Code. In these cases, the provisions contained in article 374 of this Code shall apply.

The penalty shall also be imposed in its upper half when the property has its origin in any of the offences included in Title VII bis, Chapter V of Title VIII, 

###### sección 4.ª

of Chapter XI of Title XIII, Title XV bis, Chapter I of Title XVI or Chapters V, VI, VII, VIII, IX and X of Title XIX.

2. The same penalties shall apply, as appropriate, to the concealment or disguising of the true nature, origin, location, destination, movement or rights over the property or ownership thereof, knowing that it proceeds from any of the offences set out in the preceding paragraph or from an act of participation in them.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

3. If the acts are committed through gross negligence, the penalty shall be imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine of the equivalent to three times the value.

4. The guilty party shall also be punished even if the offence from which the property originated, or the acts punished in the preceding paragraphs, were committed, in whole or in part, abroad.

5. If the guilty party has obtained profits, they shall be confiscated in accordance with the rules of article 127 of this Code.

###### Artículo 302.

1. In the cases provided for in the preceding article, the custodial sentences shall be imposed in their upper half on persons who belong to an organisation engaged in the purposes specified therein, and the penalty one degree higher on the leaders, managers or those in charge of such organisations.

The penalty shall also be imposed in its upper half on those who, being obliged persons under the regulations on the prevention of money laundering and the financing of terrorism, commit any of the conducts described in article 301 in the exercise of their professional activity.

2. In such cases, where in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is liable, the following penalties shall be imposed:

- a) A fine of two to five years, if the offence committed by the natural person carries a prison sentence of more than five years.
- b) A fine of six months to two years, in all other cases.

Having regard to the rules laid down in article 66 bis, the judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33.

###### Artículo 303.

If the acts provided for in the preceding articles are committed by a business person, intermediary in the financial sector, professional, public official, social worker, teacher or educator, in the exercise of his office, profession or trade, he shall be sentenced, in addition to the corresponding penalty, to special disqualification from public employment or office, profession or trade, industry or commerce, from three to ten years. The penalty of absolute disqualification from ten to twenty years shall be imposed when the aforementioned acts are committed by an authority or agent thereof.

For this purpose, professionals are understood to be doctors, psychologists, holders of health qualifications, veterinarians, pharmacists and their employees.

###### Artículo 304.

Incitement, conspiracy and proposal to commit the offences provided for in articles 301 to 303 shall be punished, respectively, with a penalty one or two degrees lower.

#### TÍTULO XIII BIS

Offences relating to illegal financing of political parties.

###### Artículo 304 bis.

1. Whoever receives donations or contributions intended for a political party, federation, coalition or grouping of voters in violation of the provisions of Article 5.One of Organic Law 8/2007, of 4 July, on the financing of political parties, shall be punished with a fine of three to five times its value.

2. The foregoing acts shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to four years and a fine of three to five times its value or the excess when:

- a) They concern donations covered by Article 5.One, letters a) or c) of Organic Law 8/2007, of 4 July, on the financing of political parties, of an amount CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION exceeding 500,000 euros, or exceeding by this amount the limit set in letter b) of that provision, when this is the one infringed.
- b) They concern donations covered by Article 7.Two of Organic Law 8/2007, of 4 July, on the financing of political parties, exceeding the amount of 100,000 euros.
  - 3. If the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph are of particular gravity, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half, and may reach the higher degree.
  - 4. The same penalties shall be imposed, in their respective cases, on whoever delivers donations or contributions intended for a political party, federation, coalition or grouping of voters, by himself or through an intermediary, in any of the cases referred to in the preceding numbers.
  - 5. The same penalties shall be imposed when, in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis of this Code, a legal person is responsible for the acts. Taking into account the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

###### Artículo 304 ter.

1. Whoever participates in structures or organizations, whatever their nature, whose purpose is the financing of political parties, federations, coalitions or groupings of voters, outside the provisions of the law, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from one to five years.

2. The penalty shall be imposed in its upper half on persons who direct such structures or organizations.

3. If the acts referred to in the preceding paragraphs are of particular gravity, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half, and may reach the higher degree.

#### TÍTULO XIV

Offences against the Public Treasury and against Social Security

###### Article 305.

1. Whoever, by action or omission, defrauds the State, Autonomous Community, Regional or Local Public Treasury, evading the payment of taxes, amounts withheld or that should have been withheld or payments on account, unduly obtaining refunds or enjoying tax benefits in the same manner, provided that the amount of the defrauded quota, the amount not paid of withholdings or payments on account or of refunds or tax benefits unduly obtained or enjoyed exceeds one hundred and twenty thousand euros shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to five years and a fine of one to six times the aforementioned amount, unless they have regularized their tax situation under the terms of paragraph 4 of this article.

The mere filing of returns or self-assessments does not exclude fraud, when such fraud is proven by other facts.

In addition to the penalties indicated, the liable person shall be subject to the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of three to six years.

2. For the purposes of determining the amount mentioned in the preceding paragraph:

- a) If it concerns taxes, withholdings, payments on account or refunds, that are periodic or periodically declared, reference shall be made to the amount defrauded in each tax period or declaration period, and if these are less than twelve months, the amount defrauded shall refer to the calendar year. Notwithstanding the foregoing, in cases where the fraud is carried out within a criminal organization or group, or by persons or entities acting under the appearance of a real economic activity without effectively developing it, the offense shall be prosecutable from the moment the amount set forth in paragraph 1 is reached.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

b) In all other cases, the amount shall be understood to refer to each of the different concepts for which a taxable event is susceptible to assessment.

3. The same penalties shall be imposed on whoever commits the conduct described in paragraph 1 and on whoever evades the payment of any amount that must be paid or unduly enjoys a benefit legally obtained, when the acts are committed against the Treasury of the European Union, provided that the defrauded amount exceeds one hundred thousand euros within a calendar year. Notwithstanding the foregoing, in cases where the fraud is carried out within a criminal organization or group, or by persons or entities acting under the appearance of a real economic activity without effectively developing it, the offense shall be prosecutable from the moment the amount set forth in this paragraph is reached.

If the defrauded amount does not exceed one hundred thousand euros but exceeds ten thousand, a prison sentence of three months to one year or a fine of one to three times the aforementioned amount shall be imposed and the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of six months to two years.

4. The tax situation shall be considered regularized when the taxpayer has proceeded to the complete acknowledgment and payment of the tax debt, before the Tax Administration has notified them of the commencement of verification or investigation actions aimed at determining the tax debts subject to regularization or, in the event that such actions have not occurred, before the Public Prosecutor, the State Attorney or the procedural representative of the Autonomous Community, Regional or Local Administration concerned, files a complaint or accusation against them, or before the Public Prosecutor or the Investigating Judge carry out actions that allow them to have formal knowledge of the initiation of proceedings. Likewise, the effects of the regularization provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be applicable when tax debts are satisfied after the Administration's right to determine them in administrative proceedings has prescribed.

The regularization by the taxpayer of their tax situation shall prevent prosecution for possible accounting irregularities or other instrumental falsehoods that, exclusively in relation to the tax debt subject to regularization, they may have committed prior to the regularization of their tax situation.

5. When the Tax Administration finds indications that an offense against the Public Treasury has been committed, it may assess separately, on the one hand the concepts and amounts that are not linked to the possible offense against the Public Treasury, and on the other hand, those that are linked to the possible offense against the Public Treasury. The assessment indicated first in the preceding paragraph shall follow ordinary processing and shall be subject to the appeal regime applicable to any tax assessment. And the assessment that in due course derives from those concepts and amounts that are linked to the possible offense against the Public Treasury shall follow the processing established for that purpose by tax regulations, without prejudice to its final adjustment to what is decided in the criminal proceedings.

The existence of criminal proceedings for an offense against the Public Treasury shall not suspend the collection action of the tax debt. The Tax Administration may initiate actions aimed at collection, unless the Judge, ex officio or at the request of a party, has agreed to suspend enforcement actions, upon provision of security. If security cannot be provided in whole or in part, exceptionally the Judge may agree to suspension with total or partial waiver of security if they find that enforcement could cause irreparable damage or damage that is very difficult to repair.

6. Judges and Courts may impose on the taxpayer or the perpetrator of the offense a penalty one or two degrees lower, provided that, before two months elapse from the judicial summons as accused they satisfy the tax debt and judicially acknowledge the facts. The foregoing shall be equally applicable with respect to other participants in the offense other than the taxpayer or the perpetrator of the offense, when they actively collaborate to obtain decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other liable persons, for the complete clarification of the criminal facts or for the discovery of the assets of the taxpayer or other persons liable for the offense.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

7. In proceedings for the offense contemplated in this article, for the enforcement of the fine penalty and civil liability, which shall comprise the amount of the tax debt that the Tax Administration has not assessed due to prescription or other legal cause under the terms provided for in Law 58/2003, General Tax Law, of 17 December, including interest on arrears, Judges and Courts shall request the assistance of the services of the Tax Administration which shall demand it through the administrative enforcement procedure under the terms established in the aforementioned Law.

###### Article 305 bis.

1. The offense against the Public Treasury shall be punished with imprisonment from two to six years and a fine from twice to six times the defrauded amount when the fraud is committed with any of the following circumstances:

- a) That the amount of the defrauded quota exceeds six hundred thousand euros.
- b) That the fraud has been committed within a criminal organization or group.
- c) That the use of interposed natural or legal persons or entities without legal personality, fiduciary businesses or instruments, or tax havens or territories with no taxation conceals or hinders the determination of the identity of the taxpayer or the person responsible for the offense, the determination of the defrauded amount or the assets of the taxpayer or the person responsible for the offense.
  - 2. To the cases described in this article, all remaining provisions contained in Article 305 shall apply.

In these cases, in addition to the aforementioned penalties, the responsible person shall be imposed with the loss of the possibility to obtain subsidies or public aid and the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of four to eight years.

###### Article 306.

Whoever by action or omission defrauds the general budgets of the European Union or others administered by it, in an amount exceeding fifty thousand euros, evading, outside the cases contemplated in paragraph 3 of Article 305, the payment of amounts due to be paid or, giving, outside the cases contemplated in Article 308, to the funds obtained an application different from that for which they were intended or improperly obtaining funds by falsifying the conditions required for their granting or concealing those that would have prevented it, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from the equivalent to six times the aforementioned amount and the loss of the possibility to obtain subsidies or public aid and the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of three to six years.

If the defrauded or improperly applied amount does not exceed fifty thousand euros, but exceeds four thousand, a penalty of imprisonment from three months to one year or a fine from the equivalent to three times the aforementioned amount and the loss of the possibility to obtain subsidies or public aid and the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of six months to two years shall be imposed.

###### Article 307.

1. Whoever, by action or omission, defrauds the Social Security by evading the payment of its contributions and jointly collected items, improperly obtaining refunds thereof or enjoying deductions for any item also improperly, provided that the amount of the defrauded contributions or improper refunds or deductions exceeds fifty thousand euros, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from the equivalent to six times the aforementioned amount unless they have regularized their situation with the Social Security in the terms of paragraph 3 of this article.

The mere submission of contribution documents does not exclude fraud when it is proven by other facts.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

In addition to the aforementioned penalties, the responsible person shall be imposed with the loss of the possibility to obtain subsidies or public aid and the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of three to six years.

2. For the purposes of determining the amount mentioned in the previous paragraph, the total amount defrauded over four calendar years shall be considered.

3. The situation with the Social Security shall be considered regularized when the person obliged to the Social Security has proceeded to the complete recognition and payment of the debt before being notified of the initiation of inspection proceedings aimed at determining such debts or, in the event that such proceedings have not occurred, before the Public Prosecutor or the Social Security Attorney files a criminal complaint or report against them or before the Public Prosecutor or the Investigating Judge carries out actions that allow them to have formal knowledge of the initiation of proceedings.

Likewise, the effects of the regularization provided in the previous paragraph shall apply when debts with the Social Security are satisfied once the Administration's right to their determination through administrative channels has prescribed.

The regularization of the situation with the Social Security shall prevent such person from being prosecuted for possible accounting irregularities or other instrumental falsifications that, exclusively in relation to the debt subject to regularization, they may have committed prior to the regularization of their situation.

4. The existence of criminal proceedings for an offense against the Social Security shall not paralyze the administrative procedure for the settlement and collection of the debt contracted with the Social Security, unless the Judge so orders upon provision of guarantee. In the event that a guarantee cannot be provided in whole or in part, the Judge, exceptionally, may order the suspension with total or partial waiver of the guarantees, in the event that they consider that the execution could cause irreparable or very difficult to repair damage. The administrative settlement shall ultimately be adjusted to what is decided in the criminal proceedings.

5. Judges and Courts may impose on the person obliged to the Social Security or the perpetrator of the offense a penalty one or two degrees lower, provided that, before two months elapse from the judicial summons as an accused, they satisfy the debt with the Social Security and judicially acknowledge the facts. The foregoing shall also apply to other participants in the offense other than the debtor to the Social Security or the perpetrator of the offense, when they actively collaborate to obtain decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other responsible parties, for the complete clarification of the criminal facts or for the determination of the assets of the person obliged to the Social Security or other persons responsible for the offense.

6. In proceedings for the offense contemplated in this article, for the execution of the fine penalty and civil liability, which shall include the amount of the debt to the Social Security that the Administration has not settled due to prescription or other legal cause, including late payment interest, Judges and Courts shall request the assistance of the Social Security Administration services, which shall demand them through the administrative enforcement procedure.

###### Article 307 bis.

1. The offense against Social Security shall be punished with a prison sentence of two to six years and a fine of double to six times the amount when any of the following circumstances concur in the commission of the offense:

- a) That the amount of defrauded contributions or of undue refunds or deductions exceeds one hundred twenty thousand euros.
- b) That the fraud has been committed within an organization or a criminal group.
- c) That the use of interposed natural or legal persons or entities without legal personality, fiduciary businesses or instruments, or tax havens or territories with zero taxation conceals or hinders the determination of the identity of the party obligated to Social Security or of the person responsible for the offense, the determination of the defrauded amount, or of the assets of the party obligated to Social Security or of the person responsible for the offense.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

2. All remaining provisions contained in Article 307 shall apply to the cases described in this article.

3. In these cases, in addition to the penalties indicated, the person responsible shall be imposed the loss of the possibility to obtain subsidies or public aid and of the right to enjoy tax benefits or incentives or those of Social Security for a period of four to eight years.

###### Article 307 ter.

1. Whoever obtains, for themselves or for another, the enjoyment of benefits from the Social Security System, the undue prolongation thereof, or facilitates their obtainment by others, by means of error provoked through simulation or misrepresentation of facts, or the conscious concealment of facts that they had a duty to report, thereby causing harm to the Public Administration, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years. When the facts, in view of the defrauded amount, the means employed, and the personal circumstances of the perpetrator, do not have special gravity, they shall be punished with a fine of equal to six times the amount.

In addition to the penalties indicated, the person responsible shall be imposed the loss of the possibility to obtain subsidies and of the right to enjoy tax benefits or incentives or those of Social Security for a period of three to six years.
