2. When the value of the benefits exceeds fifty thousand euros or any of the circumstances referred to in letters b) or c) of section 1 of Article 307 bis has occurred, a prison sentence of two to six years and a fine of equal to six times the amount shall be imposed.

In these cases, in addition to the penalties indicated, the person responsible shall be imposed the loss of the possibility to obtain subsidies and of the right to enjoy tax benefits or incentives or those of Social Security for a period of four to eight years.

3. The person who reimburses an amount equivalent to the value of the benefit received increased by an annual interest equivalent to the legal interest rate on money increased by two percentage points, from the moment they received it, before having been notified of the initiation of inspection and control proceedings in relation thereto or, in the event that such proceedings have not taken place, before the Public Prosecutor, the State Attorney, the Social Security Attorney, or the representative of the relevant autonomous or local Administration files a complaint or report directed against them, or before the Public Prosecutor or the Investigating Judge carry out proceedings that allow them to have formal knowledge of the initiation of proceedings, shall be exempt from criminal liability in relation to the conduct described in the preceding sections.

The exemption from criminal liability contemplated in the preceding paragraph shall also extend to said person for any instrumental falsifications that, exclusively in relation to the defrauded benefits subject to reimbursement, they may have committed prior to the regularization of their situation.

4. The existence of criminal proceedings for any of the offenses in sections 1 and 2 of this article shall not prevent the competent Administration from requiring reimbursement through administrative means of the benefits unduly obtained. The amount to be reimbursed shall be understood as provisionally set by the Administration, and shall be adjusted later to what is finally resolved in the criminal proceedings.

The criminal proceedings shall not paralyze the collection action of the competent Administration either, which may initiate proceedings directed at collection unless the Judge, ex officio or at the request of a party, has agreed to suspend the enforcement proceedings upon provision of a guarantee. If a guarantee cannot be provided in whole or in part, exceptionally the Judge may agree to the suspension with total or partial waiver of guarantees if they deem that the enforcement could cause irreparable damage or damage that is very difficult to repair.

5. In proceedings for the offense contemplated in this article, for the execution of the fine penalty and civil liability, the Judges and Courts shall request the assistance of the Social Security Administration services, which shall demand them through the administrative enforcement procedure.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

6. What is provided in section 5 of Article 307 of the Criminal Code shall be applicable to the cases regulated in this article.

###### Artículo 308.

1. Any person who obtains subsidies or aid from Public Administrations, including the European Union, in an amount or value exceeding one hundred thousand euros by falsifying the conditions required for their granting or by concealing those that would have prevented it shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to five years and a fine of one to six times the amount, unless they carry out the reimbursement referred to in paragraph 6.

2. The same penalties shall be imposed on any person who, in the development of an activity funded wholly or partially with funds from Public Administrations, including the European Union, applies them in an amount exceeding one hundred thousand euros to purposes other than those for which the subsidy or aid was granted, unless they carry out the reimbursement referred to in paragraph 6.

3. In addition to the penalties indicated, the person responsible shall be subject to the loss of the possibility to obtain subsidies or public aid and the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of three to six years.

4. If the amount obtained, defrauded or improperly applied does not exceed one hundred thousand euros but exceeds ten thousand, a prison sentence of three months to one year or a fine of one to three times the said amount shall be imposed, and the loss of the possibility to obtain subsidies or public aid and the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of six months to two years, unless they carry out the reimbursement referred to in paragraph 6.

5. For the purposes of determining the amount referred to in this article, reference shall be made to the total obtained, defrauded or improperly applied, regardless of whether it comes from one or several Public Administrations jointly.

6. The reimbursement referred to in paragraphs 1, 2 and 4 shall be deemed to have been carried out when the recipient of the subsidy or aid proceeds to return the subsidies or aid improperly received or applied, increased by the applicable default interest rate in matters of subsidies from the moment they were received, and it is carried out before notification has been given of the initiation of verification or control proceedings in relation to such subsidies or aid or, in the event that such proceedings have not taken place, before the Public Prosecutor, the State Attorney or the representative of the autonomous or local Administration concerned files a complaint or report against them or before the Public Prosecutor or the investigating judge carries out actions that allow them to have formal knowledge of the initiation of proceedings. The reimbursement shall prevent such person from being prosecuted for possible instrumental falsities that, exclusively in relation to the debt subject to regularization, they may have committed prior to the regularization of their situation.

7. The existence of criminal proceedings for any of the offences in paragraphs 1, 2 and 4 of this article shall not prevent the competent Administration from requiring reimbursement by administrative means of subsidies or aid improperly applied. The amount to be reimbursed shall be deemed to be provisionally fixed by the Administration, and shall then be adjusted to what is finally decided in the criminal proceedings.

The criminal proceedings shall not paralyze the Administration's collection action either, which may initiate collection proceedings unless the judge, ex officio or at the request of a party, has ordered the suspension of enforcement proceedings upon provision of security. If security cannot be provided in whole or in part, exceptionally the judge may order the suspension with total or partial exemption from security if they consider that enforcement could cause irreparable damage or damage that is very difficult to repair.

8. Judges and courts may impose on the person responsible for this offence a penalty reduced by one or two degrees, provided that, before two months have elapsed from the judicial summons as the person under investigation, they carry out the reimbursement referred to in paragraph 6 and judicially acknowledge the facts. The foregoing shall also apply with respect to other participants in the offence other than the person obliged to reimburse or the perpetrator of the offence, when they actively collaborate to obtain decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

responsible parties, for the complete clarification of the criminal facts or for the discovery of the assets of the person obliged or the person responsible for the offence.

###### Artículo 308 bis.

1. The suspension of the execution of penalties imposed for any of the offences regulated in this Title shall be governed by the provisions contained in Chapter III of

#### Título III del Libro I of this Code, supplemented by the following rules:

1.ª The suspension of the execution of the prison sentence imposed shall require, in addition to compliance with the requirements regulated in Article 80, that the convicted person has paid the tax debt or Social Security debt, or has proceeded to reimburse the subsidies or aid improperly received or used.

This requirement shall be deemed to be fulfilled when the convicted person assumes the commitment to satisfy the tax debt, the debt to Social Security or to proceed to reimburse the subsidies or aid improperly received or used and the civil liabilities according to their economic capacity and to facilitate the agreed confiscation, and it is reasonable to expect that it will be fulfilled. The suspension shall not be granted when it is established that the convicted person has provided inaccurate or insufficient information about their assets. The decision by which the judge or court grants the suspension of the execution of the penalty shall be communicated to the procedural representation of the State, autonomous, local or regional Public Treasury, Social Security or the Administration that granted the subsidy or aid.

2.ª The judge or court shall revoke the suspension and order the execution of the penalty, in addition to the cases in Article 86, when the convicted person does not fulfill the commitment to pay the tax or Social Security debt, to reimburse the subsidies and aid improperly received or used, or to pay civil liabilities, provided they have the economic capacity to do so, or provides inaccurate or insufficient information about their assets. In these cases, the prison supervision judge may refuse to grant conditional release.

2. In the case of Article 125, the judge or court shall hear previously the procedural representation of the State, autonomous, local or regional Public Treasury, Social Security or the Administration that granted the subsidy or aid, for the purpose of providing an asset report on those responsible for the offence in which the real economic and asset capacity of those responsible shall be analyzed and may include a proposal for installment payments in accordance with such capacity and with tax, Social Security or subsidy regulations.

###### Artículo 309.

(Derogado)

###### Artículo 310.

Anyone who, being obliged by tax law to keep commercial accounts, books or tax records, shall be punished with imprisonment from five to seven months:

- a) Completely fails to comply with said obligation under the direct assessment regime for tax bases.
- b) Keeps different accounts that, referring to the same activity and fiscal year, conceal or simulate the true situation of the company.
- c) Has not recorded in the mandatory books businesses, acts, operations or, in general, economic transactions, or has recorded them with figures different from the true ones.
- d) Has made fictitious accounting entries in the mandatory books.

The consideration as a crime of the factual situations to which paragraphs

c) and d) above refer, shall require that tax returns have been omitted or that those filed were a reflection of their false accounting and that the amount, more or less, of the charges or credits omitted or falsified exceeds, without arithmetic compensation between them, 240,000 euros for each fiscal year.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Artículo 310 bis.

When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the offenses contained in this Title, the following penalties shall be imposed:

- a) Fine from the same amount to double the amount defrauded or unduly obtained, if the offense committed by the natural person provides for a prison sentence of more than two years.
- b) Fine from double to quadruple the amount defrauded or unduly obtained, if the offense committed by the natural person provides for a prison sentence of more than five years.
- c) Fine from six months to one year, in the cases provided for in article 310.

In addition to those indicated, the responsible legal person shall be imposed the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of three to six years. The prohibition to contract with Public Administrations may be imposed. According to the rules established in article 66 bis, Judges and Courts may also impose the penalties contained in letters b), c), d), e) and g) of section 7 of

###### artículo 33.

#### TÍTULO XV

Offenses against the rights of workers

###### Artículo 311.

The following shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to six years and a fine from six to twelve months:

1.º Those who, by means of deceit or abuse of a situation of need, impose on workers in their service working or Social Security conditions that harm, suppress or restrict the rights they have recognized by legal provisions, collective agreements or individual contracts.

2.º Those who impose illegal conditions on their workers by hiring them under formulas outside the employment contract, or maintain them against administrative requirement or sanction.

3.º Those who simultaneously employ a plurality of workers without notifying their registration in the corresponding Social Security regime or, where applicable, without having obtained the corresponding work authorization, provided that the number of workers affected is at least:

- a) twenty-five percent, in companies or workplaces that employ more than one hundred workers,
- b) fifty percent, in companies or workplaces that employ more than ten workers and no more than one hundred, or
- c) all of them, in companies or workplaces that employ more than five and no more than ten workers.

4.º Those who, in the event of transfer of companies, with knowledge of the procedures described in the preceding sections, maintain the aforementioned conditions imposed by another.

5.º If the conduct described in the preceding sections is carried out with violence or intimidation, the penalties shall be imposed in the higher degree.

###### Artículo 311 bis.

Anyone who shall be punished with imprisonment from three to eighteen months or a fine from twelve to thirty months, unless the acts are punished with a more severe penalty in another provision of this Code:

- a) Repeatedly employs or gives occupation to foreign citizens who lack a work permit, or CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION
- b) employs or gives occupation to a minor who lacks a work permit.

###### Artículo 312.

1. Those who illegally traffic in labor shall be punished with imprisonment from two to five years and a fine from six to twelve months.

2. The same penalty shall be incurred by those who recruit persons or induce them to leave their job by offering employment or deceptive or false working conditions, and those who employ foreign subjects without a work permit under conditions that harm, suppress or restrict the rights they have recognized by legal provisions, collective agreements or individual contracts.

###### Artículo 313.

Anyone who induces or facilitates the emigration of any person to another country by simulating a contract or placement, or using other similar deceit, shall be punished with the penalty provided for in the preceding article.

###### Artículo 314.

Those who produce serious discrimination in employment, public or private, against any person on grounds of their ideology, religion or beliefs, their family situation, their belonging to an ethnic group, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, age, sexual or gender orientation or identity, gender reasons, aporophobia or social exclusion, the illness they suffer from or their disability, for holding legal or trade union representation of workers, for kinship with other workers of the company or for the use of any of the official languages within the Spanish State, and do not restore the situation of equality before the law after administrative requirement or sanction, repairing the economic damages that have resulted, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years or a fine from twelve to twenty-four months.

###### Artículo 315.

1. Those who, by means of deceit or abuse of a situation of need, prevent or limit the exercise of freedom of association or the right to strike shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years or a fine from six to twelve months.

2. If the conduct described in the preceding section is carried out with coercion, it shall be punished with imprisonment from one year and nine months to three years or with a fine from eighteen months to twenty-four months.

###### Artículo 316.

Those who, in breach of occupational risk prevention regulations and being legally obliged, do not provide the necessary means for workers to carry out their activity with adequate safety and hygiene measures, thereby seriously endangering their life, health or physical integrity, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from six to twelve months.

###### Artículo 317.

When the offense referred to in the preceding article is committed through gross negligence, it shall be punished with the penalty one degree lower.

###### Artículo 318.

When the acts provided for in the articles of this title are attributed to legal persons, the penalty indicated shall be imposed on the administrators or persons in charge of the service who have been responsible for them and on those who, knowing of them and being able to remedy them, have not adopted measures for it. In these cases, the judicial authority may also decree one or more of the measures provided for in article 129 of this Code.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

#### TÍTULO XV BIS

Offenses against the rights of foreign citizens

###### Article 318 bis.

1. Whoever intentionally assists a person who is not a national of a Member State of the European Union to enter Spanish territory or to transit through it in a manner that violates the legislation on entry or transit of foreigners, shall be punished with a fine of three to twelve months or imprisonment of three months to one year.

The acts shall not be punishable when the objective pursued by the perpetrator was solely to provide humanitarian aid to the person concerned.

If the acts were committed for profit, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half.

2. Whoever intentionally assists, for profit, a person who is not a national of a Member State of the European Union to remain in Spain, violating the legislation on residence of foreigners, shall be punished with a fine of three to twelve months or imprisonment of three months to one year.

3. The acts referred to in paragraph 1 of this article shall be punished with imprisonment of four to eight years when any of the following circumstances are present:

- a) When the acts were committed within an organization that engages in such activities. When dealing with the leaders, administrators or managers of such organizations or associations, the penalty shall be applied in its upper half, which may be raised to the immediately higher degree.
- b) When the lives of the persons subject to the offense were endangered, or the danger of causing serious injuries was created.
  - 4. The same penalties as in the preceding paragraph and also absolute disqualification from six to twelve years shall be incurred by those who commit the acts taking advantage of their status as an authority, agent thereof or public official.
  - 5. When in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the crimes included in this Title, it shall be imposed the penalty of a fine of two to five years, or that of three to five times the profit obtained if the resulting amount were higher.

Taking into account the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

6. The courts, taking into account the gravity of the act and its circumstances, the conditions of the guilty party and the purpose pursued by the latter, may impose the penalty one degree lower than that respectively indicated.

#### TITLE XVI

Of crimes relating to land use planning and urbanism, the protection of historical heritage and the environment

##### CHAPTER I

Of crimes relating to land use planning and urbanism

###### Article 319.

1. The penalties of imprisonment of one year and six months to four years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months, unless the benefit obtained from the crime exceeds the resulting amount in which case the fine shall be from one to three times the amount of said benefit, and special disqualification for profession or trade for a period of one to four years, shall be imposed on developers, builders or technical directors who carry out urbanization, construction or building works not authorizable on land designated for roads, green areas, public domain property or places that have legally or administratively recognized their

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

landscape, ecological, artistic, historical or cultural value, or for the same reasons have been considered specially protected.

2. The penalty of imprisonment of one to three years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months, unless the benefit obtained from the crime exceeds the resulting amount in which case the fine shall be from one to three times the amount of said benefit, and special disqualification for profession or trade for a period of one to four years, shall be imposed on developers, builders or technical directors who carry out urbanization, construction or building works not authorizable on non-developable land.

3. In any case, the judges or courts, with reasoning, may order, at the expense of the perpetrator of the act, the demolition of the work and the restoration to its original state of the altered physical reality, without prejudice to compensation owed to third parties in good faith, and assessing the circumstances, and after hearing the competent Administration, shall make demolition temporarily conditional upon the provision of guarantees ensuring payment of such compensation. In all cases, confiscation of the profits from the crime shall be ordered whatever transformations they may have undergone.

4. In the cases provided for in this article, when a legal person is responsible in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis of this Code, it shall be imposed the penalty of a fine of one to three years, unless the benefit obtained from the crime exceeds the resulting amount in which case the fine shall be from double to quadruple the amount of said benefit.

Taking into account the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

###### Article 320.

1. The authority or public official who, knowing of its injustice, has favorably reported on planning instruments, urbanization projects, subdivision, reparcelation, construction or building projects or the granting of licenses contrary to the rules of land use planning or urban planning in force, or who on the occasion of inspections has concealed the violation of said rules or who has omitted the carrying out of mandatory inspections, shall be punished with the penalty established in Article 404 of this Code and, in addition, with imprisonment of one year and six months to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

2. The same penalties shall be imposed on the authority or public official who by himself or as a member of a collegiate body has decided or voted in favor of the approval of the planning instruments, urbanization projects, subdivision, reparcelation, construction or building projects or the granting of the licenses referred to in the preceding paragraph, knowing of their injustice.

##### CHAPTER II

Of crimes relating to historical heritage

###### Article 321.

Those who demolish or seriously alter buildings specially protected for their historical, artistic, cultural or monumental interest shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to three years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and, in any case, special disqualification for profession or trade for a period of one to five years.

In any case, the Judges or Courts, with reasoning, may order, at the expense of the perpetrator of the act, the reconstruction or restoration of the work, without prejudice to compensation owed to third parties in good faith.

###### Article 322.

1. The authority or public official who, knowing of its injustice, has favorably reported on projects for demolition or alteration of specially protected buildings shall be punished in addition to the penalty established in Article 404 of this Code with imprisonment of six months to two years or a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

2. The same penalties shall be imposed on the authority or public official who by himself or as a member of a collegiate body has decided or voted in favor of its granting knowing of its injustice.

###### Article 323.

1. Whoever causes damage to property of historical, artistic, scientific, cultural or monumental value, or to archaeological sites, terrestrial or underwater, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years or a fine of twelve to twenty-four months. Acts of plunder in the latter shall be punished with the same penalty.

2. If damage of particular gravity has been caused or affecting property whose historical, artistic, scientific, cultural or monumental value was especially significant, the penalty higher in degree than that indicated in the preceding subsection may be imposed.

3. In all these cases, judges or courts may order, at the expense of the perpetrator of the damage, the adoption of measures aimed at restoring, as far as possible, the damaged property.

###### Article 324.

Whoever by gross negligence causes damage, in an amount exceeding 400 euros, to an archive, registry, museum, library, educational center, scientific office, analogous institution or to property of artistic, historical, cultural, scientific or monumental value, as well as to archaeological sites, shall be punished with a fine of three to 18 months, having regard to their importance.

##### CHAPTER III

Offenses against natural resources and the environment

###### Article 325.

1. Whoever, in contravention of laws or other provisions of a general nature protecting the environment, causes or carries out directly or indirectly emissions, discharges, radiation, extractions or excavations, landfills, noise, vibrations, injections or deposits, in the atmosphere, soil, subsoil or terrestrial, underground or maritime waters, including the high seas, with effects even in cross-border areas, as well as water abstractions that, by themselves or together with others, cause or may cause substantial damage to the quality of air, soil or water, or to animals or plants, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years, a fine of ten to fourteen months and special disqualification from profession or trade for a period of one to two years.

2. If the aforementioned conduct, by itself or together with others, could seriously harm the balance of natural systems, a penalty of imprisonment from two to five years, a fine of eight to twenty-four months and special disqualification from profession or trade for a period of one to three years shall be imposed.

If a risk of serious harm to the health of persons has been created, the penalty of imprisonment shall be imposed in its upper half, it being possible to reach the higher degree.

###### Article 326.

1. Those who, in contravention of laws or other provisions of a general nature, collect, transport, recover, transform, dispose of or utilize waste, or do not adequately control or supervise such activities, in such a way as to cause or be capable of causing substantial damage to the quality of air, soil or water, or to animals or plants, death or serious injury to persons, or may seriously harm the balance of natural systems, shall be punished with the penalties provided for in the preceding article, in their respective cases.

2. Whoever, outside the case referred to in the preceding subsection, transfers a non-negligible quantity of waste, whether in the case of one or several transfers that appear to be linked, in any of the cases referred to in the law of the Union

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

European relating to waste transfers, shall be punished with a penalty of three months to one year of imprisonment, or a fine of six to eighteen months and special disqualification from profession or trade for a period of three months to one year.

###### Article 326 bis.

Those who, in contravention of laws or other provisions of a general nature, carry out the operation of facilities in which a dangerous activity is carried out or in which hazardous substances or preparations are stored or used in such a way as to cause or be capable of causing substantial damage to the quality of air, soil or water, to animals or plants, death or serious injury to persons, or may seriously harm the balance of natural systems, shall be punished with the penalties provided for in Article 325, in their respective cases.

###### Article 327.

The acts referred to in the three preceding articles shall be punished with the penalty higher in degree, without prejudice to those that may apply in accordance with other provisions of this Code, when in the commission of any of the acts described in the preceding article any of the following circumstances concur:

- a) That the industry or activity operates clandestinely, without having obtained the required administrative authorization or approval of its facilities.
- b) That the express orders of the administrative authority for correction or suspension of the activities classified in the preceding article have been disobeyed.
- c) That information about the environmental aspects thereof has been falsified or concealed.
- d) That the inspection activity of the Administration has been obstructed.
- e) That a risk of irreversible or catastrophic deterioration has occurred.
- f) That an illegal extraction of water occurs during a period of restrictions.

###### Article 328.

When in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the offenses included in this Chapter, the following penalties shall be imposed on it:

- a) Fine of one to three years, or double to quadruple the damage caused when the resulting amount is higher, if the offense committed by the natural person carries a penalty of more than two years of deprivation of liberty.
- b) Fine of six months to two years, or double to triple the damage caused if the resulting amount is higher, in all other cases.

Having regard to the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in paragraphs b) to g) of subsection 7 of Article 33.

###### Article 329.

1. The authority or public official who, knowingly, has reported favorably on the granting of manifestly illegal licenses authorizing the operation of the polluting industries or activities referred to in the preceding articles, or who by reason of their inspections has concealed the infringement of laws or regulatory provisions of a general nature governing them, or who has omitted carrying out mandatory inspections, shall be punished with the penalty established in Article 404 of this Code and, in addition, with imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine of eight to twenty-four months.

2. The authority or public official who by themselves or as a member of a collegiate body has decided or voted in favor of its granting knowing of its unlawfulness shall be punished with the same penalties.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Article 330.

Whoever, in a protected natural area, seriously damages any of the elements that have served to classify it, shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from one to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

###### Article 331.

The acts provided for in this chapter shall be punished, where applicable, with the penalty lower in degree, in their respective cases, when they have been committed by gross negligence.

##### CHAPTER IV

Offenses against flora and fauna.

###### Article 332.

1. Anyone who, in violation of laws or other general provisions, cuts, fells, uproots, collects, acquires, possesses or destroys protected species of wild flora, or traffics in them, their parts, derivatives thereof or their propagules, except where the conduct affects an insignificant quantity of specimens and has no significant consequences for the conservation status of the species, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to two years or a fine of eight to twenty-four months, and special disqualification from profession or trade for a period of six months to two years.

The same penalty shall be imposed on anyone who, in violation of laws or other general provisions, destroys or seriously alters their habitat.

2. The penalty shall be imposed in its upper half if the species or subspecies are listed as endangered.

3. If the acts were committed through serious negligence, a prison sentence of three months to one year or a fine of four to eight months shall be imposed, and special disqualification from profession or trade for a period of three months to two years.

###### Article 333.

Anyone who introduces or releases non-native species of flora or fauna, in a manner that damages the biological balance, in violation of laws or general provisions protecting species of flora or fauna, shall be punished with a prison sentence of four months to two years or a fine of eight to twenty-four months and, in any case, special disqualification from profession or trade for a period of one to three years.

###### Article 334.

1. Anyone who, in violation of laws or other general provisions, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to two years or a fine of eight to twenty-four months and, in any case, special disqualification from profession or trade and special disqualification from the exercise of the right to hunt or fish for a period of two to four years:

- a) hunts, fishes, acquires, possesses or destroys protected species of wild fauna;
- b) traffics in them, their parts or derivatives thereof; or,
- c) carries out activities that prevent or hinder their reproduction or migration.

The same penalty shall be imposed on anyone who, in violation of laws or other general provisions, destroys or seriously alters their habitat.

2. The penalty shall be imposed in its upper half if the species or subspecies are listed as endangered.

3. If the acts were committed through serious negligence, a prison sentence of three months to one year or a fine of four to eight months and, in any case, special disqualification from profession or trade and special disqualification from the exercise of the right to hunt or fish for a period of three months to two years shall be imposed.

4. The penalty of deprivation of the right to possession and carrying of weapons for a period of between two to four years shall be imposed, when the acts relating to subsections a) and c) of

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

subsection 1 were committed using weapons, in activities related or not to hunting.

###### Article 335.

1. Anyone who hunts or fishes species other than those indicated in the previous article, when expressly prohibited by specific regulations on their hunting or fishing, shall be punished with a fine of eight to twelve months, special disqualification from the exercise of the right to hunt or fish for a period of two to five years and deprivation of the right to possession and carrying of weapons for the same period.

2. Anyone who hunts or fishes or carries out significant shellfish gathering activities on species other than those indicated in the previous article on public or private land belonging to others, subject to special hunting regulations, without the proper permission of its owner or subject to shellfish gathering or aquaculture concession or authorization without the proper enabling administrative title, shall be punished with a fine of four to eight months and special disqualification from the exercise of the right to hunt, fish or carry out shellfish gathering activities for a period of one to three years and deprivation of the right to possession and carrying of weapons for the same period, in addition to the penalties that may correspond, as applicable, for the commission of the offense provided for in subsection 1 of this article.

3. If the foregoing conduct causes serious damage to the hunting heritage of land subject to special hunting regulations or to the sustainability of resources in areas of shellfish gathering or aquaculture concession or authorization, a prison sentence of six months to two years and special disqualification from the exercise of the rights to hunt, fish, and carry out shellfish gathering activities for a period of two to five years and deprivation of the right to possession and carrying of weapons for the same period shall be imposed.

###### Article 336.

Anyone who, without being legally authorized, uses poison, explosive means or other instruments or methods of similar destructive or non-selective efficacy for fauna for hunting or fishing, shall be punished with a prison sentence of four months to two years or a fine of eight to twenty-four months and, in any case, special disqualification from profession or trade and special disqualification from the exercise of the right to hunt or fish for a period of one to three years, with deprivation of the right to possession and carrying of weapons for the same period. If the damage caused is of notorious importance, the aforementioned prison sentence shall be imposed in its upper half.

###### Article 337.

(Repealed)

###### Article 337 bis.

(Repealed)

##### CHAPTER V

General provisions

###### Article 338.

When the conduct defined in this Title affects any protected natural area, penalties higher in degree than those respectively provided shall be imposed.

###### Article 339.

The judges or courts shall order the adoption, at the expense of the perpetrator, of the necessary measures aimed at restoring the disturbed ecological balance, as well as any other precautionary measure necessary for the protection of the assets protected in this Title.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Article 340.

If the person guilty of any of the acts typified in this Title has voluntarily proceeded to repair the damage caused, the Judges and Courts shall impose a penalty lower in degree than those respectively provided.

#### TITLE XVI BIS

Offenses against animals

###### Article 340 bis.

1. Whoever, outside legally regulated activities and by any means or procedure, including acts of a sexual nature, causes to a domestic, tamed, domesticated animal or one living temporarily or permanently under human control an injury requiring veterinary treatment for the restoration of its health, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from three to eighteen months or a fine from six to twelve months and with the penalty of special disqualification from one to three years for the exercise of a profession, trade or business related to animals and for the keeping of animals. If the injuries in the preceding paragraph are caused to a vertebrate animal not included in the preceding paragraph, a penalty of imprisonment from three to twelve months or a fine from three to six months shall be imposed, in addition to the penalty of special disqualification from one to three years for the exercise of a profession, trade or business related to animals and for the keeping of animals.

If the offense had been committed using firearms, the judge or court may impose with reasoning the penalty of deprivation of the right to possess and carry weapons for a period from one to four years.

2. The penalties provided in the preceding paragraph shall be imposed in their upper half when any of the following aggravating circumstances is present:

- a) Using weapons, instruments, objects, means, methods or forms that could be dangerous for the life or health of the animal.
- b) Executing the act with cruelty.
- c) Causing the animal the loss or disablement of a sense, organ or principal limb.
- d) Carrying out the act by its owner or by whoever has been entrusted with the care of the animal.
- e) Executing the act in the presence of a minor or a particularly vulnerable person.
- f) Executing the act with intent to profit.
- g) Committing the act to coerce, intimidate, harass or produce psychological harm to whoever is or has been a spouse or to a person who is or has been linked to the perpetrator by an analogous relationship of affection, even without cohabitation.
- h) Executing the act at a public event or disseminating it through information or communication technologies.
- i) Using poison, explosive means or other instruments or devices of similar destructive or non-selective effectiveness.
  - 3. When, on the occasion of the acts provided in the first paragraph of this article, the death of a domestic, tamed, domesticated animal or one living temporarily or permanently under human control is caused, a penalty of imprisonment from twelve to twenty-four months shall be imposed, in addition to the penalty of special disqualification from two to four years for the exercise of a profession, trade or business related to animals and for the keeping of animals.

When, on the occasion of the acts provided in the first paragraph of this article, the death of a vertebrate animal not included in the preceding paragraph is caused, a penalty of imprisonment from six to eighteen months or a fine from eighteen to twenty-four months shall be imposed, in addition to the penalty of special disqualification from two to four years for the exercise of a profession, trade or business related to animals and for the keeping of animals.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

If the offense had been committed using firearms, the judge or court may impose with reasoning the penalty of deprivation of the right to possess and carry weapons for a period from two to five years.

When any of the circumstances provided in the preceding paragraph is present, the judge or court shall impose the penalties in their upper half.

4. If the injuries produced do not require veterinary treatment or the animal has been seriously mistreated without causing injuries, a penalty of a fine from one to two months or community service from one to thirty days shall be imposed. Likewise, the penalty of special disqualification from three months to one year for the exercise of a profession, trade or business related to animals and for the keeping of animals shall be imposed.

###### Article 340 ter.

Whoever abandons a vertebrate animal that is under their responsibility in conditions in which its life or integrity may be endangered shall be punished with a penalty of a fine from one to six months or community service from thirty-one to ninety days. Likewise, the penalty of special disqualification from one to three years for the exercise of a profession, trade or business related to animals and for the keeping of animals shall be imposed.

###### Article 340 quater.

1. When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the offenses contained in this title, the following penalties shall be imposed:

- a) Fine from one to three years, if the offense committed by the natural person has a penalty of imprisonment exceeding two years provided by law.
- b) Fine from six months to two years, in other cases.
  - 2. Taking into account the rules established in article 66 bis, in cases of liability of legal persons the judges and courts may also impose the penalties contained in article 33.7, paragraphs b) to g).

###### Article 340 quinquies.

The judges or courts may adopt with reasoning any precautionary measure necessary for the protection of the legal interests protected in this Title, including provisional changes regarding the ownership and care of the animal.

When the penalty of special disqualification for the exercise of a profession, trade or business related to animals and for the keeping of animals falls upon the person who had been assigned the ownership or care of the mistreated animal, the judge or court, ex officio or at the request of a party, shall adopt the pertinent measures regarding the ownership and care of the animal.

#### TITLE XVII

Of offenses against collective safety

##### CHAPTER I

Of catastrophic risk offenses

###### Section 1.ª Of offenses relating to nuclear energy and ionizing radiation

###### Article 341.

Whoever releases nuclear energy or radioactive elements that endanger the life or health of persons or their property, even if no explosion occurs, shall be punished with

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

a penalty of imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years, and special disqualification for public employment or office, profession or trade for a period from ten to twenty years.

###### Article 342.

Whoever, without being included in the preceding article, disrupts the operation of a nuclear or radioactive installation, or alters the development of activities in which materials or equipment producing ionizing radiation are involved, creating a situation of serious danger for the life or health of persons, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from four to ten years, and special disqualification for public employment or office, profession or trade for a period from six to ten years.

###### Artículo 343.

1. Whoever, by means of dumping, emission or introduction into the air, soil or water of a quantity of materials or ionizing radiation, or exposure by any other means to such radiation, endangers the life, integrity, health or property of one or more persons, shall be punished with imprisonment of six to twelve years and special disqualification for public employment or office, profession or trade for a period of six to ten years. The same penalty shall be imposed when this conduct endangers the quality of air, soil or water or animals or plants.

2. When, on the occasion of the conduct described in the previous paragraph, in addition to the prevented risk, a harmful result constituting a crime is produced, whatever its severity, the judges or courts shall only assess the most severely punished offense, applying the penalty in its upper half.

3. When in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the crimes set forth in this article, it shall be imposed a fine of two to five years.

Taking into account the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set forth in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

###### Artículo 344.

The acts provided for in the previous articles shall be punished with the penalty lower in degree, in their respective cases, when they have been committed by gross negligence.

###### Artículo 345.

1. Whoever, in violation of laws or other provisions of a general nature, acquires, possesses, traffics, facilitates, treats, transforms, uses, stores, transports or disposes of nuclear materials or other dangerous radioactive substances that cause or may cause death or serious injury to persons, or substantial damage to the quality of air, soil quality or water quality or to animals or plants, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years, a fine of six to eighteen months, and special disqualification for profession or trade for a period of one to three years.

2. Whoever produces such materials or substances without proper authorization shall be punished with the penalty higher in degree.

3. If the acts referred to in the previous paragraphs had been committed by gross negligence, the penalty lower in degree than that indicated therein shall be imposed.

###### Sección 2.ª De los estragos

###### Artículo 346.

1. Those who, by provoking explosions or using any other means of similar destructive power, cause the destruction of airports, ports, stations, buildings, public premises, deposits containing flammable or explosive materials, communication routes, means of collective transport, or the immersion or stranding of a vessel, flooding, explosion of a mine or industrial installation, lifting of the rails of a railway line, malicious change of the signals used in its service for the safety of means of transport, blowing up of a bridge, destruction of public roadway,

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

damage to oil pipelines, serious disturbance of any kind or means of communication, disturbance or interruption of the supply of water, electricity, hydrocarbons or other fundamental natural resource shall incur the penalty of imprisonment of ten to twenty years, when the havoc necessarily entailed a danger to the life or integrity of persons.

2. When such danger does not exist, they shall be punished with a penalty of four to eight years of imprisonment.

3. If, in addition to the danger, injury to the life, physical integrity or health of persons has been produced, the acts shall be punished separately with the penalty corresponding to the crime committed.

###### Artículo 347.

Whoever by gross negligence provokes a crime of havoc shall be punished with imprisonment of one to four years.

###### Sección 3.ª De otros delitos de riesgo provocados por explosivos y otros

agentes

###### Artículo 348.

1. Those who in the manufacture, handling, transport, possession or marketing of explosives, flammable or corrosive substances, toxic and asphyxiating substances, or any other materials, devices or artifacts that may cause havoc, violate the established safety standards, putting in concrete danger the life, physical integrity or health of persons, or the environment, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to three years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification for public employment or office, profession or trade for a period of six to twelve years. The same penalties shall be imposed on whoever, illegally, produces, imports, exports, markets or uses ozone-depleting substances.

2. Those responsible for the surveillance, control and use of explosives that may cause havoc who, in violation of the regulations on explosives, have facilitated their actual loss or theft shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment of six months to three years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification for public employment or office, profession or trade of six to twelve years.

3. In the cases set forth in the previous paragraphs, when a legal person is responsible for the acts in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis of this Code, it shall be imposed a fine of one to three years, unless, once the damage produced has been proven, its amount is greater, in which case the fine shall be double to quadruple the amount of said damage.

Taking into account the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set forth in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33. The penalties established in the previous paragraphs shall be imposed in their upper half when they are the directors, administrators or managers of the company, firm, organization or operation.

4. Those responsible for factories, workshops, means of transport, deposits and other establishments related to explosives that may cause havoc shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to one year, a fine of six to twelve months and special disqualification for public employment or office, profession or trade for a period of three to six years when they incur in one or more of the following conducts:

- a) Obstructing the inspection activity of the Administration in matters of explosives safety.
- b) Falsifying or concealing from the Administration relevant information on compliance with mandatory safety measures relating to explosives.
- c) Disobeying the express orders of the Administration aimed at correcting serious anomalies detected in matters of explosives safety.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Artículo 349.

Those who in the handling, transport or possession of organisms violate the established standards or safety measures, putting in concrete danger the life, physical integrity or health of persons, or the environment, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to two years, a fine of six to twelve months, and special disqualification for public employment or office, profession or trade for a period of three to six years.

###### Artículo 350.

Without prejudice to the provisions of Article 316, those who in the opening of wells or excavations, in the construction or demolition of buildings, dams, pipelines or similar works or, in their conservation, conditioning or maintenance violate the established safety standards whose non-observance may cause catastrophic results, and put in concrete danger the life, physical integrity of persons or the environment, shall incur the penalties provided for in the previous article.

##### CAPÍTULO II

De los incendios

###### Sección 1.ª Offences of Arson

###### Artículo 351.

Those who cause a fire that involves a danger to the life or physical integrity of persons shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from ten to twenty years. Judges or Courts may impose the penalty one degree lower taking into account the lesser extent of the danger caused and the other circumstances of the act.

When such danger to the life or physical integrity of persons does not exist, the acts shall be punished as damage provided for in article 266 of this Code.

###### Sección 2.ª Forest Fires

###### Artículo 352.

Those who set fire to mountains or forest masses shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from twelve to eighteen months.

If there has been danger to the life or physical integrity of persons, the act shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of article 351, imposing, in any case, the penalty of a fine from twelve to twenty-four months.

###### Artículo 353.

1. The acts referred to in the preceding article shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from three to six years and a fine from eighteen to twenty-four months when the fire reaches special gravity, considering the concurrence of any of the following circumstances:

1.ª That it affects a surface of considerable importance.

2.ª That large or serious erosive effects on the soil result.

3.ª That it significantly alters the conditions of animal or plant life, or affects any protected natural area.

4.ª That the fire affects areas close to population centers or inhabited places.

5.ª That the fire is caused at a time when the climatological or terrain conditions significantly increase the risk of its propagation.

6.ª In any case, when serious deterioration or destruction of the affected resources is caused.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

2. The same penalty shall be imposed when the perpetrator acts to obtain economic benefit from the effects derived from the fire.

###### Artículo 354.

1. Whoever sets fire to mountains or forest masses without the fire spreading through them shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine from six to twelve months.

2. The conduct provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be exempt from penalty if the fire does not spread due to the voluntary and positive action of its perpetrator.

###### Artículo 355.

In all cases provided for in this section, Judges or Courts may order that the classification of the land in areas affected by a forest fire may not be modified for a period of up to thirty years. They may likewise order that the uses that had been carried out in the areas affected by the fire be limited or eliminated, as well as the administrative intervention of the burned wood from the fire.

###### Sección 3.ª Fires in Non-Forest Areas

###### Artículo 356.

Whoever sets fire to non-forest vegetation areas seriously damaging the natural environment shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine from six to twenty-four months.

###### Sección 4.ª Fires on Own Property

###### Artículo 357.

The arsonist of own property shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from one to four years if he had the purpose of defrauding or harming third parties, has caused fraud or harm, there exists danger of propagation to another's building, trees or plantation, or has seriously damaged the conditions of wildlife, forests or natural areas.

###### Sección 5.ª Common Provisions

###### Artículo 358.

Whoever through gross negligence causes any of the arson offences punished in the preceding sections shall be punished with the penalty one degree lower than those respectively provided for each case.

###### Artículo 358 bis.

The provisions of articles 338 to 340 shall also be applicable to the offences regulated in this Chapter.

##### CAPÍTULO III

Offences Against Public Health

###### Artículo 359.

Whoever, without being duly authorized, produces substances harmful to health or chemical products that may cause havoc, or dispenses or supplies them, or trades in them, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine from six to twelve months, and special disqualification for profession or industry for a period from six months to two years.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Artículo 360.

Whoever, being authorized for the traffic of the substances or products referred to in the preceding article, dispenses or supplies them without complying with the formalities provided for in the respective Laws and Regulations, shall be punished with the penalty of a fine from six to twelve months and disqualification for the profession or occupation from six months to two years.

###### Artículo 361.

Whoever manufactures, imports, exports, supplies, intermediates, markets, offers or places on the market, or stores for these purposes, medicines, including those for human and veterinary use, as well as medicines under investigation, that lack the necessary authorization required by law, or health products that do not have the conformity documents required by general provisions, or that were deteriorated, expired or failed to meet the technical requirements relating to their composition, stability and efficacy, and thereby generates a risk to the life or health of persons, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to three years, a fine from six to twelve months and special disqualification for profession or occupation from six months to three years.

###### Artículo 361 bis.

The distribution or public dissemination through the Internet, telephone or any other information or communication technology of content specifically intended to promote or facilitate, among minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection, the consumption of products, preparations or substances or the use of techniques for ingestion or elimination of food products whose use is likely to generate risk to the health of persons shall be punished with the penalty of a fine from six to twelve months or penalty of imprisonment from one to three years.

The judicial authorities shall order the adoption of the necessary measures for the removal of the contents referred to in the preceding paragraph, for the interruption of services that predominantly offer such contents or for the blocking of both when they are located abroad.

###### Article 362.

1. A penalty of imprisonment from six months to four years, a fine of six to eighteen months and special disqualification from profession or trade from one to three years shall be imposed on anyone who prepares or produces,

a) a medicinal product, including those for human and veterinary use, as well as investigational medicinal products; or an active substance or an excipient of such medicinal product;

b) a medical device, as well as the accessories, elements or materials that are essential for its integrity; in such a way that there is a deceptive presentation of: its identity, including, where applicable, the packaging and labelling, the expiry date, the name or composition of any of its components, or, where applicable, the dosage thereof; its origin, including the manufacturer, the country of manufacture, the country of origin and the holder of the marketing authorization or conformity documents; data relating to compliance with legal requirements or demands, licenses, conformity documents or authorizations; or its history, including records and documents relating to the distribution channels used, provided that they were intended for public consumption or use by third parties, and generate a risk to the life or health of persons.

2. The same penalties shall be imposed on anyone who alters, during its manufacture or preparation or at a later time, the quantity, the dose, the expiry date or the genuine composition, as authorized or declared, of any of the medicinal products, substances, excipients, medical devices, accessories, elements or materials mentioned in the preceding paragraph, in a manner that reduces its safety, efficacy or quality, generating a risk to the life or health of persons.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Article 362 bis.

A penalty of imprisonment from six months to four years, a fine of six to eighteen months and special disqualification from profession or trade from one to three years shall be imposed on anyone who, with knowledge of their falsification or alteration, imports, exports, announces or advertises, offers, exhibits, sells, facilitates, dispenses, dispatches, packages, supplies, including intermediation, traffics, distributes or places on the market, any of the medicinal products, active substances, excipients, medical devices, accessories, elements or materials referred to in the preceding article, and thereby generates a risk to the life or health of persons.

The same penalties shall be imposed on anyone who acquires or holds them in storage with the purpose of destining them for public consumption, use by third parties or any other use that may affect public health.

###### Article 362 ter.

Anyone who prepares any false or mendacious document relating to any of the medicinal products, active substances, excipients, medical devices, accessories, elements or materials referred to in paragraph 1 of Article 362, including their packaging, labelling and directions for use, in order to commit or facilitate the commission of one of the offences of

###### Article 362, shall be punished with a penalty of six months to two years of imprisonment, a fine of six

to twelve months and special disqualification from profession or trade from six months to two years.

###### Article 362 quater.

Penalties one degree higher than those specified in Articles 361, 362, 362 bis or 362 ter shall be imposed when the offence is perpetrated with any of the following circumstances:

1.ª That the offender is an authority, public official, physician, healthcare professional, teacher, educator, physical or sports trainer, and acts in the exercise of their position, profession or trade.

2.ª That the medicinal products, active substances, excipients, medical devices, accessories, elements or materials referred to in Article 362:

- a) have been offered through large-scale media; or
- b) have been offered or facilitated to minors, persons with disabilities in need of special protection, or persons especially vulnerable in relation to the product facilitated.

3.ª That the offender belonged to a criminal organization or group whose purpose is the commission of this type of offence.

4.ª That the acts were carried out in establishments open to the public by those responsible for them or their employees.

###### Article 362 quinquies.

1. Those who, without therapeutic justification, prescribe, provide, dispense, supply, administer, offer or facilitate to non-competitive federated athletes, non-federated athletes who practice sport for recreation, or athletes who participate in competitions organized in Spain by sports entities, prohibited substances or pharmacological groups, as well as non-regulatory methods, intended to increase their physical capacities or to modify the results of competitions, which by their content, repeated intake or other concurrent circumstances, endanger their life or health, shall be punished with penalties of imprisonment from six months to two years, a fine of six to eighteen months and special disqualification from employment or public office, profession or trade, from two to five years.

2. The penalties provided for in the preceding paragraph in their upper half shall be imposed when the offence is perpetrated with any of the following circumstances:

1.ª That the victim is a minor.

2.ª That deception or intimidation has been employed.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

3.ª That the person responsible has taken advantage of a relationship of labour or professional superiority.

###### Article 362 sexies.

In the offences provided for in the preceding articles of this Chapter, the substances and products referred to in Articles 359 et seq. shall be subject to confiscation, as well as the assets, means, instruments and proceeds in accordance with the provisions of Articles 127 to 128.

###### Article 363.

Producers, distributors or traders who endanger the health of consumers shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from one to four years, a fine of six to twelve months and special disqualification from profession, trade, industry or commerce for a period of three to six years:

- 1. By offering food products on the market with omission or alteration of the requirements established in laws or regulations on expiry or composition.
- 2. By manufacturing or selling beverages or foodstuffs intended for public consumption and harmful to health.
- 3. By trafficking in spoiled goods.
- 4. By preparing products whose use is not authorized and is harmful to health, or by trading in them.
- 5. By concealing or removing items intended to be rendered unusable or disinfected, in order to trade in them.

###### Article 364.

1. Whoever adulterates with additives or other unauthorised agents capable of causing harm to people's health the foodstuffs, substances or beverages intended for the food trade, shall be punished with the penalties of the previous article. If the offender were the owner or the person responsible for production of a foodstuffs factory, the penalty of special disqualification for profession, trade, industry or commerce from six to ten years shall additionally be imposed.

2. The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever carries out any of the following conducts:

1.º Administering to animals whose meat or products are intended for human consumption substances not permitted that generate risk for people's health, or in higher doses or for purposes other than those authorised.

2.º Slaughtering animals for consumption or destining their products for human consumption, knowing that the substances mentioned in the previous number have been administered to them.

3.º Slaughtering animals for consumption to which therapeutic treatments have been applied by means of substances of those referred to in paragraph 1.º

4.º Dispatching for public consumption the meat or products of animals for consumption without respecting the waiting periods in their case provided for by regulation.

###### Article 365.

Whoever poisons or adulterates with infectious substances, or others that may be seriously harmful to health, the drinking water or the food substances intended for public use or for consumption by a community of persons shall be punished with a prison sentence of two to six years.

###### Article 366.

When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the offences contained in the previous articles of this Chapter, a fine of one to three years shall be imposed, or double to five times the value of the substances and products referred to in articles 359 and following, or the benefit that has been or could have been obtained, applying the highest amount.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

Having regard to the rules established in article 66 bis, the judges and courts may also impose the penalties contained in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33.

###### Article 367.

If the acts provided for in all the previous articles were carried out through gross negligence, the penalties lower in degree shall be imposed, respectively.

###### Article 368.

Those who execute acts of cultivation, production or trafficking, or otherwise promote, favour or facilitate the illegal consumption of toxic drugs, narcotics or psychotropic substances, or possess them for those purposes, shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from three to six years and a fine of the equivalent to triple the value of the drug object of the offence if it concerns substances or products that cause serious harm to health, and imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of the equivalent to double in other cases.

Notwithstanding the provisions of the previous paragraph, the courts may impose the penalty lower in degree to those indicated having regard to the minor nature of the act and the personal circumstances of the offender. This power may not be exercised if any of the circumstances referred to in articles 369 bis and

###### 370 concur. Article 369.

1. The penalties higher in degree to those indicated in the previous article and a fine of the equivalent to quadruple shall be imposed when any of the following circumstances concur: 1.ª The offender were an authority, public official, medical practitioner, social worker, teacher or educator and acts in the exercise of his position, profession or trade.

2.ª The offender participates in other organised activities or whose execution is facilitated by the commission of the offence.

3.ª The acts were carried out in establishments open to the public by those responsible for or employees of the same.

4.ª The substances referred to in the previous article are supplied to persons under 18 years of age, to persons with mental disabilities or to persons undergoing treatment for addiction or rehabilitation.

5.ª The quantity of the said substances object of the conducts referred to in the previous article were of notorious importance.

6.ª The said substances are adulterated, manipulated or mixed with each other or with others, increasing the possible harm to health.

7.ª The conducts described in the previous article take place in educational centres, in centres, establishments or military units, in penitentiary establishments or in addiction or rehabilitation centres, or in their proximities.

8.ª The offender employs violence or exhibits or makes use of weapons to commit the act.

###### Article 369 bis.

When the acts described in article 368 have been carried out by persons who belong to a criminal organisation, the penalties of imprisonment from nine to twelve years and a fine of the equivalent to quadruple the value of the drug shall be imposed if it concerns substances and products that cause serious harm to health and imprisonment from four years and six months to ten years and the same fine in other cases.

To the leaders, managers or administrators of the organisation the penalties higher in degree to those indicated in the first paragraph shall be imposed.

When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the offences contained in the two previous articles, the following penalties shall be imposed:

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

a) Fine of two to five years, or triple to five times the value of the drug when the resulting amount were higher, if the offence committed by the natural person has a prison sentence of more than five years provided.

b) Fine of one to three years, or double to quadruple the value of the drug when the resulting amount were higher, if the offence committed by the natural person has a prison sentence of more than two years not included in the previous subparagraph provided. Having regard to the rules established in article 66 bis, the judges and courts may also impose the penalties contained in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33.

###### Article 370.

The penalty higher in one or two degrees to that indicated in article 368 shall be imposed when:

1.º Persons under 18 years of age or persons with mental disabilities are used to commit these offences.

2.º It concerns the leaders, administrators or managers of the organisations referred to in circumstance 2.ª of paragraph 1 of article 369.

3.º The conducts described in article 368 were of extreme gravity.

Cases in which the quantity of the substances referred to in article 368 considerably exceeds that considered to be of notorious importance, or vessels, boats or aircraft have been used as a specific means of transport, or the indicated conducts have been carried out simulating international trade operations between companies, or it concerns international networks dedicated to this type of activities, or when three or more of the circumstances provided for in article 369.1 concur, are considered of extreme gravity.

In the cases of the previous numbers 2.º and 3.º a fine of the equivalent to triple the value of the drug object of the offence shall additionally be imposed on the offenders.

###### Article 371.

1. Whoever manufactures, transports, distributes, trades in or has in their possession equipment, materials or substances listed in Table I and Table II of the United Nations Convention, done at Vienna on 20 December 1988, on illicit traffic in narcotic drugs and psychotropic substances, and any other products added to the same Convention or included in other future Conventions of the same nature, ratified by Spain, knowing that they are to be used in the illicit cultivation, production or manufacture of toxic drugs, narcotic drugs or psychotropic substances, or for these purposes, shall be punished with a prison sentence of three to six years and a fine of from one to three times the value of the goods or effects.

2. The penalty indicated in its upper half shall be imposed when the persons who commit the acts described in the preceding paragraph belong to an organisation dedicated to the purposes indicated therein, and the penalty superior in degree when it concerns the leaders, administrators or managers of the said organisations or associations.

In such cases, the judges or courts shall impose, in addition to the corresponding penalties, that of special disqualification of the offender from the exercise of their profession or industry for a period of three to six years, and the other measures provided for in Article 369.2.

###### Article 372.

If the acts provided for in this chapter were committed by a businessperson, intermediary in the financial sector, professional, public official, social worker, teacher or educator, in the exercise of their position, profession or occupation, they shall be imposed, in addition to the corresponding penalty, that of special disqualification from public employment or office, profession or occupation, industry or trade, for three to ten years. The penalty of absolute disqualification for ten to twenty years shall be imposed when the said acts were committed by an authority or agent thereof, in the exercise of their office.

For this purpose, professionals are understood to be doctors, psychologists, persons in possession of a health qualification, veterinarians, pharmacists and their employees.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Article 373.

The provocation, conspiracy and proposal to commit the offences provided for in Articles 368 to 372 shall be punished with a penalty one to two degrees lower than that which corresponds, respectively, to the acts provided for in the preceding provisions.

###### Article 374.

In the offences provided for in the second paragraph of paragraph 1 of Article 301 and in Articles 368 to 372, in addition to the penalties to be imposed for the offence committed, toxic drugs, narcotic drugs or psychotropic substances, the equipment, materials and substances referred to in Article 371, as well as the property, means, instruments and profits shall be subject to confiscation in accordance with the provisions of Articles 127 to 128 and the following special rules:

1.ª Once the sentence is final, the samples that have been set aside shall be destroyed, or the totality of what has been seized shall be destroyed, in the event that the competent judicial body has ordered its preservation.

2.ª The property, means, instruments and profits definitively confiscated by sentence, which may not be applied to the satisfaction of civil liabilities arising from the offence nor to legal costs, shall be awarded entirely to the State.

###### Article 375.

Convictions by foreign judges or courts for offences of the same nature as those provided for in Articles 361 to 372 of this Chapter shall produce the effects of recidivism, unless the criminal record has been cancelled or may be cancelled in accordance with Spanish law.

###### Article 376.

In the cases provided for in Articles 361 to 372, the judges or courts, providing reasoning in the sentence, may impose a penalty one or two degrees lower than that indicated by law for the offence in question, provided that the subject has voluntarily abandoned their criminal activities and has actively collaborated with the authorities or their agents either to prevent the commission of the offence, or to obtain decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other persons responsible or to prevent the action or development of the organisations or associations to which they have belonged or with which they have collaborated.

Likewise, in the cases provided for in Articles 368 to 372, the judges or courts may impose a penalty one or two degrees lower on the offender who, being drug-dependent at the time of commission of the acts, sufficiently proves that they have successfully completed a rehabilitation treatment, provided that the quantity of toxic drugs, narcotic drugs or psychotropic substances was not of notorious importance or of extreme gravity.

###### Article 377.

For the determination of the amount of fines to be imposed in application of Articles 368 to 372, the value of the drug that is the object of the offence or of the goods or effects seized shall be the final price of the product or, as the case may be, the reward or profit obtained by the offender, or that they could have obtained.

###### Article 378.

The payments made by the convicted person for one or more of the offences referred to in Articles 361 to 372 shall be allocated in the following order:

1.º To the reparation of the damage caused and compensation for losses.

2.º To the compensation of the State for the amount of expenses incurred on its account in the case.

3.º To the fine.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

4.º To the costs of the private or particular prosecutor when their payment is imposed in the sentence. preference among the interested parties.

##### CHAPTER IV

Of offences against Road Safety

###### Article 379.

1.
