Whoever drives a motor vehicle or a moped at a speed exceeding by sixty kilometres per hour in urban roads or by eighty kilometres per hour in interurban roads that permitted by regulation, shall be punished with a prison sentence of three to six months or with a fine of six to twelve months or with community service of thirty-one to ninety days, and, in any case, with deprivation of the right to drive motor vehicles and mopeds for a period of more than one and up to four years.

2. With the same penalties shall be punished whoever drives a motor vehicle or moped under the influence of toxic drugs, narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or alcoholic beverages. In any case, whoever drives with a blood alcohol level in exhaled air exceeding 0.60 milligrams per litre or with a blood alcohol level exceeding 1.2 grams per litre shall be convicted with said penalties.

###### Article 380.

1. Whoever drives a motor vehicle or a moped with manifest recklessness and places in concrete danger the life or physical integrity of persons shall be punished with prison sentences of six months to two years and deprivation of the right to drive motor vehicles and mopeds for a period of more than one and up to six years.

2. For the purposes of this provision, the driving in which the circumstances provided for in the first paragraph and in the second clause of the second paragraph of the preceding article concur shall be deemed manifestly reckless.

###### Article 381.

1. Whoever, with manifest disregard for the lives of others, carries out the conduct described in the preceding article shall be punished with prison sentences of two to five years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and deprivation of the right to drive motor vehicles and mopeds for a period of six to ten years.

2. When the life or physical integrity of persons has not been placed in concrete danger, the penalties shall be imprisonment of one to two years, a fine of six to twelve months and deprivation of the right to drive motor vehicles and mopeds for the period provided for in the preceding paragraph.

###### Artículo 382.

When the acts sanctioned in articles 379, 380 and 381 cause, in addition to the foreseen risk, a harmful result constituting a crime, whatever its gravity, the Judges or Courts shall assess only the most seriously punished offense, applying the penalty in its upper half and sentencing, in all cases, to compensation for the civil liability that may have arisen.

When the harmful result concurs with a crime under article 381, the penalty of deprivation of the right to drive motor vehicles and mopeds provided in this provision in its upper half shall be imposed in all cases.

###### Artículo 382 bis.

1. The driver of a motor vehicle or moped who, outside the cases contemplated in article 195, voluntarily and without his own or third parties' risk concurring, abandons the scene of the events after causing an accident in which one

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

or more persons died or in which they were caused any of the injuries referred to in articles 147.1, 149 and 150, shall be punished as author of a crime of abandonment of the accident scene.

2. The facts contemplated in this article that originated in a negligent action by the driver shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to four years and deprivation of the right to drive motor vehicles and mopeds from one to four years.

3. If the origin of the facts giving rise to the abandonment were fortuitous, a penalty of three to six months of imprisonment and deprivation of the right to drive motor vehicles and mopeds from six months to two years shall correspond.

###### Artículo 383.

The driver who, required by an agent of the authority, refuses to submit to the legally established tests for verification of blood alcohol levels and the presence of toxic drugs, narcotics and psychotropic substances referred to in the preceding articles, shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from six months to one year and deprivation of the right to drive motor vehicles and mopeds for a period exceeding one and up to four years.

###### Artículo 384.

Whoever drives a motor vehicle or moped in cases of loss of validity of the permit or license due to total loss of the legally assigned points shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from three to six months or with a fine of twelve to twenty-four months or with community service work from thirty-one to ninety days.

The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever carries out the driving after having been deprived provisionally or definitively of the permit or license by judicial decision and on whoever drives a motor vehicle or moped without ever having obtained a driving permit or license.

###### Artículo 385.

Whoever causes a serious risk for traffic in any of the following ways shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years or with fines of twelve to twenty-four months and community service work from ten to forty days: 1.ª By placing unforeseeable obstacles on the road, spilling slippery or flammable substances or changing, removing or canceling the signage or by any other means. 2.ª By not restoring the safety of the road when there is an obligation to do so.

###### Artículo 385 bis.

The motor vehicle or moped used in the facts provided in this Chapter shall be considered an instrument of the crime for the purposes of articles 127 and 128.

###### Artículo 385 ter.

In the crimes provided in articles 379, 383, 384 and 385, the Judge or Court, reasoning it in the judgment, may reduce by one degree the penalty of imprisonment in consideration of the lesser entity of the risk caused and the other circumstances of the fact.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

#### TÍTULO XVIII

Of falsities

##### CAPÍTULO I

Of the counterfeiting of currency and stamped effects

###### Artículo 386.

1. Shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from eight to twelve years and a fine from the equivalent to ten times the apparent value of the currency:

1.º Whoever alters currency or manufactures false currency.

2.º Whoever exports false or altered currency or imports it to Spain or to any other Member State of the European Union.

3.º Whoever transports, utters or distributes false or altered currency with knowledge of its falsity.

2. If the false currency were put into circulation, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half.

The possession, receipt or obtaining of false currency for its uttering or distribution or putting into circulation shall be punished with the penalty lower by one or two degrees, considering the value thereof and the degree of connivance with the counterfeiter, alterer, importer or exporter.

3. Whoever, having received false currency in good faith, utters or distributes it after its falsity has been established to him shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from three to six months or a fine of six to twenty-four months. However, if the apparent value of the currency does not exceed 400 euros, the penalty of a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

4. If the guilty party belonged to a society, organization or association, even of a transitory nature, that is dedicated to the carrying out of these activities, the judge or court may impose one or some of the consequences provided in article 129 of this Code.

5. When, in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis, a legal person is responsible for the preceding crimes, it shall be imposed the penalty of a fine of three times to ten times the apparent value of the currency. Considering the rules established in article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties included in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33.

###### Artículo 387.

For the purposes of the preceding article, currency means metallic currency and paper money of legal tender and that which has not yet been issued or put into circulation officially but which is intended for its circulation as legal tender currency. Currencies of other European Union countries and foreign currencies shall be equated to national currency.

False currency shall also be understood as that which, despite being made in the legal facilities and with the legal materials, is made in breach, knowingly, of the issuance conditions that the competent authority may have established or when it is issued when there is no issuance order whatsoever.

###### Artículo 388.

The conviction of a foreign Court, imposed for a crime of the same nature as those included in this chapter, shall be equated to the judgments of Spanish Judges or Courts for the purposes of recidivism, unless the criminal record has been canceled or could be canceled in accordance with Spanish law.

###### Artículo 389.

Whoever counterfeits or utters, in connivance with the counterfeiter, postage stamps or stamped effects, or introduces them into Spain knowing of their falsity, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to three years.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

The good faith acquirer of postage stamps or stamped effects who, knowing of their falsity, distributes or uses them shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from three to six months or a fine of six to twenty-four months. However, if the apparent value of the stamps or stamped effects does not exceed 400 euros, the penalty of a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

##### CAPÍTULO II

Of documentary falsities

###### Sección 1.ª Of the falsification of public, official and commercial documents

and of the dispatches transmitted by telecommunication services

###### Article 390.

1. The authority or public official who, in the exercise of their functions, commits forgery shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from three to six years, a fine of six to twenty-four months, and special disqualification for a period of two to six years:

1.º By altering a document in any of its essential elements or requirements.

2.º By simulating a document in whole or in part, in such a way as to mislead as to its authenticity.

3.º By assuming in an act the intervention of persons who have not participated, or attributing to those who have participated in it statements or declarations different from those they made.

4.º By failing to tell the truth in the narration of the facts.

2. The person in charge of any religious denomination who engages in any of the conducts described in the preceding numbers, with respect to acts and documents that may have effect on the status of persons or on the civil order, shall be punished with the same penalties as those indicated in the preceding paragraph.

###### Article 391.

The authority or public official who, through gross negligence, commits any of the falsifications provided for in the preceding article or gives rise to another person committing them, shall be punished with a fine of six to twelve months and suspension from employment or public office for a period of six months to one year.

###### Article 392.

1. The private individual who commits, in a public, official or commercial document, any of the falsifications described in the first three numbers of paragraph 1 of Article 390, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine of six to twelve months.

2. The same penalties shall be imposed on anyone who, without having participated in the falsification, traffics in any way with a false identity document. The penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine of three to six months shall be imposed on anyone who knowingly uses a false identity document.

This provision is applicable even when the false identity document appears as belonging to another European Union Member State or to a third State or has been falsified or acquired in another European Union Member State or in a third State if it is used or trafficked with in Spain.

###### Article 393.

Anyone who, knowing of its falsity, presents in court or, to harm another, uses a false document of those covered in the preceding articles, shall be punished with the penalty one degree lower than that indicated for the forgers.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Article 394.

1. The authority or public official in charge of telecommunication services who fabricates or falsifies a telegraphic dispatch or another of said services, shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from six months to three years and special disqualification for a period of two to six years.

2. Anyone who, knowing of its falsity, uses the false dispatch to harm another, shall be punished with the penalty one degree lower than that indicated for the forgers.

###### Sección 2.ª On the falsification of private documents

###### Article 395.

Anyone who, to harm another, commits in a private document any of the falsifications provided for in the first three numbers of paragraph 1 of Article 390, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years.

###### Article 396.

Anyone who, knowing of its falsity, presents in court or, to harm another, uses a false document of those covered in the preceding article, shall incur the penalty one degree lower than that indicated for the forgers.

###### Sección 3.ª On the falsification of certificates

###### Article 397.

The professional who issues a false certificate shall be punished with a fine of three to twelve months.

###### Article 398.

The authority or public official who issues a false certification with little significance in legal transactions shall be punished with suspension for six months to two years.

This provision shall not be applicable to certificates relating to Social Security and the Public Treasury.

###### Article 399.

1. The private individual who falsifies a certificate of those designated in the preceding articles shall be punished with a fine of three to six months.

2. The same penalty shall be imposed on anyone who knowingly uses the certificate, as well as on anyone who, without having participated in its falsification, traffics with it in any way.

3. This provision is applicable even when the certificate appears as belonging to another European Union Member State or to a third State or has been falsified or acquired in another European Union Member State or in a third State if it is used in Spain.

###### Sección 4.ª On the falsification of credit and debit cards, traveller's cheques and

other means of payment other than cash.

###### Article 399 bis.

1. Anyone who alters, copies, reproduces or in any other way falsifies credit or debit cards, traveller's cheques or any other means of payment other than cash, shall be punished with imprisonment from four to eight years.

The penalty in its upper half shall be imposed when the falsified effects affect a generality of persons or when the facts are committed within the framework of a criminal organization dedicated to these activities.

When, in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis, a legal person is responsible for the aforementioned crimes, the penalty of a fine of two to five years shall be imposed.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

In accordance with the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

2. The possession of falsified credit or debit cards, traveller's cheques or any other means of payment other than cash, intended for distribution or trafficking shall be punished with the penalty indicated for falsification.

3. Anyone who, without having participated in the falsification, uses, to the detriment of another and knowing of the falsity, falsified credit or debit cards, traveller's cheques or any other means of payment other than cash, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to five years.

4. Anyone who, for fraudulent use and knowing of its falsity, possesses or obtains, for themselves or for a third party, credit or debit cards, traveller's cheques or any other means of payment other than cash shall be punished with imprisonment from one to two years.

###### Article 399 ter.

For the purposes of this Code, a means of payment other than cash is understood to be any protected device, object or record, material or immaterial, or a combination of these, except for legal tender, which, by itself or in combination with a procedure or set of procedures, allows the holder or user to transfer money or monetary value even through digital means of exchange.

##### CHAPTER III

General provisions

###### Article 400.

The manufacture, receipt, obtaining, possession, distribution, making available or marketing of tools, materials, instruments, substances, data and computer programs, devices, security elements or any other means designed or specifically adapted for the commission of the crimes described in the preceding chapters, shall be punished with the penalty indicated in each case for the perpetrators.

###### Article 400 bis.

In the cases described in Articles 392, 393, 394, 396 and 399 of this Code, the use of false document, dispatch, certificate or identity document shall also be understood to mean the use of the corresponding authentic documents, dispatches, certificates or identity documents by anyone who is not authorized to do so.

##### CHAPTER IV

On the usurpation of civil status

###### Artículo 401.

Whoever usurps the civil status of another shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years.

##### CAPÍTULO V

Of the usurpation of public functions and professional intrusion

###### Artículo 402.

Whoever illegitimately exercises acts proper to an authority or public official attributing official character to themselves, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to three years.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Artículo 402 bis.

Whoever without authorization uses publicly and improperly a uniform, attire or insignia that attributes official character to them shall be punished with a fine of one to three months.

###### Artículo 403.

1. Whoever exercises acts proper to a profession without possessing the corresponding academic degree issued or recognized in Spain in accordance with current legislation, shall incur a fine of twelve to twenty-four months. If the professional activity carried out requires an official degree that accredits the necessary qualification and legally enables its exercise, and one is not in possession of said degree, a fine of six to twelve months shall be imposed.

2. A prison sentence of six months to two years shall be imposed if any of the following circumstances concur:

- a) If the guilty party, moreover, publicly attributes to themselves the status of professional protected by the aforementioned degree.
- b) If the guilty party exercises the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph in premises or an establishment open to the public in which the provision of services proper to that profession is advertised.

#### TÍTULO XIX

Offenses against Public Administration

##### CAPÍTULO I

Of the malfeasance of public officials and other unjust conduct

###### Artículo 404.

An authority or public official who, knowingly of its injustice, issues an arbitrary resolution in an administrative matter shall be punished with the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office and from the exercise of the right of passive suffrage for a period of nine to fifteen years.

###### Artículo 405.

An authority or public official who, in the exercise of their competence and knowingly of its illegality, proposes, appoints or gives possession for the exercise of a specific public office to any person without the legally established requirements being met, shall be punished with a fine of three to eight months and suspension from public employment or office for a period of one to three years.

###### Artículo 406.

The same fine shall be imposed on the person who accepts the proposal, appointment or taking of possession mentioned in the preceding article, knowing that they lack the legally required qualifications.

##### CAPÍTULO II

Of the abandonment of post and the omission of the duty to prosecute offenses

###### Artículo 407.

1. An authority or public official who abandons their post with the purpose of not preventing or not prosecuting any of the offenses included in Titles XXI, XXII, XXIII and XXIV shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to four years and absolute disqualification from public employment or office for a period of six to ten years. If they had carried out the

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

abandonment in order not to prevent or not prosecute any other offense, the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of one to three years shall be imposed.

2. The same penalties shall be imposed, respectively, when the abandonment has as its purpose not to execute the penalties corresponding to these offenses imposed by the competent judicial authority.

###### Artículo 408.

The authority or official who, failing in the obligation of their office, intentionally ceases to promote the prosecution of offenses of which they have knowledge or of those responsible for them, shall incur the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of six months to two years.

###### Artículo 409.

Authorities or public officials who promote, direct or organize the collective and manifestly illegal abandonment of a public service shall be punished with a fine of eight to twelve months and suspension from public employment or office for a period of six months to two years.

Authorities or public officials who merely take part in the collective or manifestly illegal abandonment of an essential public service and with serious harm to it or to the community, shall be punished with a fine of eight to twelve months.

##### CAPÍTULO III

Of disobedience and denial of assistance

###### Artículo 410.

1. Authorities or public officials who openly refuse to give due compliance to judicial resolutions, decisions or orders of superior authority, issued within the scope of their respective competence and clothed with legal formalities, shall incur a fine of three to twelve months and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of six months to two years.

2. Notwithstanding the provisions of the preceding paragraph, authorities or officials shall not incur criminal liability for not complying with an order that constitutes a manifest, clear and categorical infringement of a provision of Law or of any other general regulation.

###### Artículo 411.

The authority or public official who, having suspended, for any reason other than that expressed in the second paragraph of the preceding article, the execution of the orders of their superiors, disobeys them after those superiors have disapproved the suspension, shall incur the penalties of a fine of twelve to twenty-four months, and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of one to three years.

###### Artículo 412.

1. The public official who, when required by competent authority, does not provide the assistance due to the Administration of Justice or other public service, shall incur the penalties of a fine of three to twelve months, and suspension from public employment or office for a period of six months to two years.

2. If the person required is an authority, chief or person in charge of a public force or an agent of authority, the penalties of a fine of twelve to eighteen months and suspension from public employment or office for a period of two to three years shall be imposed.

3. The authority or public official who, when required by a private individual to provide some assistance to which they are obligated by reason of their office to prevent an offense against the life of persons, abstains from providing it, shall be punished with a fine of eighteen to twenty-four months and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of three to six years.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

If it concerns an offense against the integrity, sexual freedom, health or liberty of persons, they shall be punished with a fine of twelve to eighteen months and suspension from public employment or office for one to three years.

In the case that such requirement is to prevent any other offense or other harm, they shall be punished with a fine of three to twelve months and suspension from public employment or office for a period of six months to two years.

##### CAPÍTULO IV

Of the breach of trust in the custody of documents and the violation of secrets

###### Artículo 413.

The authority or public official who, knowingly, removes, destroys, renders useless or conceals, totally or partially, documents whose custody is entrusted to them by reason of their office, shall incur the penalties of imprisonment of one to four years, a fine of seven to twenty-four months, and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of three to six years.

###### Artículo 414.

1. The authority or public official who, by reason of their office, has been entrusted with the custody of documents to which the competent authority has restricted access, and who knowingly destroys or renders useless the means put in place to prevent such access or consents to their destruction or rendering useless, shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year or a fine from six to twenty-four months and, in any case, special disqualification from employment or public office for a period of one to three years.

2. The private individual who destroys or renders useless the means referred to in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with a fine from six to eighteen months.

###### Artículo 415.

The authority or public official not covered by the preceding article who, knowingly and without due authorization, accesses or permits access to secret documents whose custody has been entrusted to them by reason of their office, shall incur a penalty of a fine from six to twelve months, and special disqualification from employment or public office for a period of one to three years.

###### Artículo 416.

Private individuals who are accidentally entrusted with the dispatch or custody of documents, by commission of the Government or of the authorities or public officials to whom they have been entrusted by reason of their office, and who engage in the conduct described therein, shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment or fine immediately lower than those respectively indicated in the three preceding articles.

###### Artículo 417.

1. The authority or public official who reveals secrets or information of which they have knowledge by reason of their office or position and which should not be disclosed, shall incur a penalty of a fine from twelve to eighteen months and special disqualification from employment or public office for a period of one to three years.

If the revelation referred to in the preceding paragraph results in serious harm to the public cause or to a third party, the penalty shall be imprisonment from one to three years, and special disqualification from employment or public office for a period of three to five years.

2. If it concerns secrets of a private individual, the penalties shall be imprisonment from two to four years, a fine from twelve to eighteen months, and suspension from employment or public office for a period of one to three years.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Artículo 418.

The private individual who exploits for themselves or for a third party the secret or privileged information obtained from a public official or authority shall be punished with a fine from the amount to three times the benefit obtained or facilitated and the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and of the right to enjoy tax benefits or incentives or of Social Security for a period of one to three years. If serious harm results to the public cause or to a third party, the penalty shall be imprisonment from one to six years and the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and of the right to enjoy tax benefits or incentives or of Social Security for a period of six to ten years.

##### CAPÍTULO V

Of bribery

###### Artículo 419.

The authority or public official who, for their own benefit or that of a third party, receives or solicits, by themselves or through an intermediary, a gift, favor or compensation of any kind, or accepts an offer or promise to perform in the exercise of their office an act contrary to the duties inherent thereto or to not perform or unjustifiably delay that which they should perform, shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from three to six years, a fine from twelve to twenty-four months, and special disqualification from employment or public office and from the exercise of the right of passive suffrage for a period of nine to twelve years, without prejudice to the penalty corresponding to the act performed, omitted or delayed by reason of the compensation or promise, if it constitutes an offense.

###### Artículo 420.

The authority or public official who, for their own benefit or that of a third party, receives or solicits, by themselves or through an intermediary, a gift, favor or compensation of any kind, or accepts an offer or promise to perform an act proper to their office, shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from two to four years, a fine from twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification from employment or public office and from the exercise of the right of passive suffrage for a period of five to nine years.

###### Artículo 421.

The penalties indicated in the preceding articles shall also be imposed when the gift, favor or compensation is received or solicited by the authority or public official, in their respective cases, as a reward for the conduct described in said articles.

###### Artículo 422.

The authority or public official who, for their own benefit or that of a third party, accepts, by themselves or through an intermediary, a gift or present offered to them in consideration of their office or function, shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year and suspension from employment and public office from one to three years.

###### Artículo 423.

The provisions of the preceding articles shall be equally applicable to jurors and arbitrators, national or international, as well as to mediators, experts, judicially appointed administrators or receivers, insolvency administrators or any persons who participate in the exercise of public function.

###### Artículo 424.

1. The private individual who offers or delivers a gift or compensation of any other kind to an authority, public official or person who participates in the exercise of public function so that they perform an act contrary to the duties inherent to their office or an act

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

proper to their office, so that they do not perform or delay that which they should perform, or in consideration of their office or function, shall be punished in their respective cases, with the same penalties of imprisonment and fine as the corrupted authority, official or person.

2. When a private individual delivers the gift or compensation in response to the solicitation of the authority, public official or person who participates in the exercise of public function, they shall be imposed with the same penalties of imprisonment and fine as correspond to them.

3. If the action obtained or intended from the authority or official is related to a procurement procedure, subsidies or auctions called by the Administrations or public entities, the private individual and, where applicable, the company, association or organization they represent shall be imposed with the penalty of disqualification from obtaining subsidies and public aid, from contracting with entities, agencies or bodies that form part of the public sector and from enjoying tax benefits or incentives and of Social Security for a period of five to ten years.

###### Artículo 425.

When bribery takes place in a criminal case in favor of the defendant by their spouse or another person to whom they are linked in a stable manner by an analogous relationship of affection, or by an ascendant, descendant or sibling by nature, by adoption or relatives by affinity in the same degrees, the briber shall be imposed with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year.

###### Artículo 426.

The private individual who, having occasionally acceded to the solicitation of a gift or other compensation made by an authority or public official, reports the fact to the authority that has the duty to proceed with its investigation before the opening of the proceedings, provided that no more than two months have elapsed from the date of the events, shall be exempt from penalty for the offense of bribery.

###### Artículo 427.

The provisions set forth in the preceding articles shall also apply when the conduct described is performed by or affects:

- a) Any person who holds a legislative, administrative or judicial office or position in a country of the European Union or any other foreign country, whether by appointment or by election.
- b) Any person who exercises a public function for a country of the European Union or any other foreign country, including a public body or a public enterprise, for the European Union or for another public international organization.
- c) Any official or agent of the European Union or of a public international organization.
- d) Any person to whom has been assigned and who is exercising a public service function consisting of the management, in Member States or in third countries, of financial interests of the European Union or in making decisions concerning those interests.

###### Artículo 427 bis.

When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is liable for the offenses set forth in this Chapter, the following penalties shall be imposed:

- a) Fine of two to five years, or three to five times the benefit obtained when the resulting amount is higher, if the offense committed by the natural person provides for a prison sentence of more than five years.
- b) Fine of one to three years, or two to four times the benefit obtained when the resulting amount is higher, if the offense committed by the natural person provides for a sentence of more than two years of deprivation of liberty not included in the preceding subsection.
- c) Fine of six months to two years, or two to three times the benefit obtained if the resulting amount is higher, in all other cases.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

Taking into account the rules established in article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set forth in letters b) to g) of section 7 of article 33.

##### CAPÍTULO VI

Influence peddling

###### Artículo 428.

The public official or authority who influences another public official or authority by taking advantage of the exercise of the powers of his office or of any other situation derived from his personal or hierarchical relationship with the latter or with another official or authority in order to obtain a decision that may generate directly or indirectly an economic benefit for himself or for a third party, shall incur the penalties of imprisonment from six months to two years, fine from the equivalent to double the benefit sought or obtained, and special disqualification for public employment or office and for the exercise of the right of passive suffrage for a period of five to nine years. If he obtains the benefit sought, these penalties shall be imposed in their upper half.

###### Artículo 429.

The private individual who influences a public official or authority by taking advantage of any situation derived from his personal relationship with the latter or with another public official or authority in order to obtain a decision that may generate directly or indirectly an economic benefit for himself or for a third party, shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from six months to two years, fine from the equivalent to double the benefit sought or obtained, and prohibition from contracting with the public sector, as well as the loss of the possibility of obtaining grants or public aid and of the right to enjoy tax benefits or incentives and Social Security benefits for a period of six to ten years. If he obtains the benefit sought, these penalties shall be imposed in their upper half.

###### Artículo 430.

Those who, offering to perform the conduct described in the two preceding articles, solicit from third parties gifts, presents or any other remuneration, or accept an offer or promise, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year. If the offense is committed by an authority or public official, the penalty of special disqualification for public office or employment and for the exercise of the right of passive suffrage for a period of one to four years shall also be imposed.

When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is liable for the offenses set forth in this Chapter, the penalty of a fine from six months to two years shall be imposed.

Taking into account the rules established in article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set forth in letters b) to g) of section 7 of article 33.

###### Artículo 431.

For the purposes of this chapter, public officials shall be understood to be those determined by articles 24 and 427.

##### CAPÍTULO VII

Embezzlement

###### Artículo 432.

1. The authority or public official who, with intent to profit, misappropriates or consents to a third party, with the same intent, misappropriating public assets in his charge by reason of his functions or on the occasion thereof, shall be punished with a penalty of

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

imprisonment from two to six years, special disqualification for public office or employment and for the exercise of the right of passive suffrage for a period of six to ten years.

2. The penalties of imprisonment from four to eight years and absolute disqualification for a period of ten to twenty years shall be imposed if in the acts referred to in the preceding section any of the following circumstances have occurred:

- a) serious damage or hindrance to the public service has been caused,
- b) the value of the damage caused or of the public assets misappropriated exceeds 50,000 euros,
- c) the embezzled items are of artistic, historical, cultural or scientific value; or if they are items intended to alleviate a public calamity.

If the value of the damage caused or of the public assets misappropriated exceeds 250,000 euros, the penalty of imprisonment shall be imposed in its upper half, and may reach the next higher degree.

3. The acts referred to in this article shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from one to two years and a fine from three months and one day to twelve months, and in any case special disqualification for public office or employment and the right of passive suffrage for a period of one to five years, when the damage caused or the value of the public assets is less than 4,000 euros.

###### Artículo 432 bis.

The authority or public official who, without intent to misappropriate it, devotes to private uses the public assets placed in his charge by reason of his functions or on the occasion thereof, shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from six months to three years, and suspension from public employment or office from one to four years.

If the guilty party does not return the same elements of the public assets diverted within ten days following the initiation of the proceedings, the penalties of the preceding article shall be imposed.

###### Artículo 433.

The authority or public official who, without being covered by the preceding articles, gives the public assets he administers a public use different from that for which they were intended, shall incur the penalties of imprisonment from one to four years and special disqualification from public employment or office from two to six years, if serious damage or hindrance results to the service to which they were allocated, and disqualification from public employment or office from one to three years and a fine from three to twelve months, if it does not result.

###### Artículo 433 bis.

1. The authority or public official who, in a manner capable of causing economic damage to the public entity on which he depends, and outside the cases provided for in

###### article 390, falsifies their accounting, the documents that must reflect their situation

economic or the information contained therein, shall be punished with the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of one to ten years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

2. The same penalties shall apply to the public authority or official who, in a manner capable of causing economic harm to the public entity on which they depend, provides to third parties false information relating to the economic situation thereof or any of the documents or information referred to in the preceding paragraph.

3. If economic harm to the entity were to be caused, the penalties of imprisonment from one to four years, special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of three to ten years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months shall be imposed.

###### Article 433 ter.

For the purposes of this Code, public patrimony shall be understood as the entire set of assets and rights, of economic-patrimonial content, belonging to public Administrations.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Article 434.

If the person guilty of any of the acts classified in this chapter had effectively and fully repaired the harm caused to the public patrimony before the commencement of the oral trial, or had actively and effectively collaborated with the authorities or their agents to obtain decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other persons responsible or for the complete clarification of the criminal facts, the judges and courts shall impose on the person responsible for this offense a penalty reduced by one or two degrees.

###### Article 435.

The provisions of this chapter are extended:

1.º To those who are in charge in any capacity of funds, revenues or effects of public Administrations.

2.º To private individuals legally designated as depositaries of public funds or effects.

3.º To administrators or depositaries of money or property seized, sequestered or deposited by public authority, even if they belong to private individuals.

4.º To insolvency administrators, in relation to the insolvency estate or the economic interests of creditors. In particular, the interests of creditors shall be considered affected when the order of payment of credits established by law is fraudulently altered

5.º To legal persons that in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis are responsible for the offenses contained in this Chapter. In these cases the following penalties shall be imposed:

- a) Fine of two to five years, or from triple to quintuple the value of the harm caused or of the property or effects misappropriated when the resulting amount is higher, if the offense committed by the natural person carries a penalty of imprisonment of more than five years.
- b) Fine of one to three years, or from double to quadruple the value of the harm caused or of the property or effects misappropriated when the resulting amount is higher, if the offense committed by the natural person carries a penalty of more than two years of deprivation of liberty not included in the preceding subparagraph.
- c) Fine of six months to two years, or from double to triple the value of the harm caused or of the property or effects misappropriated if the resulting amount is higher, in all other cases.

Having regard to the rules established in article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set forth in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33.

###### Article 435 bis.

For the purposes of this chapter, public official shall be understood as those determined by articles 24 and 427.

##### CHAPTER VIII

Of frauds and illegal exactions

###### Article 436.

The public authority or official who, intervening by reason of their office in any of the acts of the modalities of public contracting or in settlements of public effects or assets, colludes with interested parties or uses any other artifice to defraud any public entity, shall incur the penalties of imprisonment from two to six years and special disqualification from public employment or office and from exercising the right of passive suffrage for a period of six to ten years. The private individual who has colluded with the public authority or official shall be subject to the same penalty of imprisonment as these, as well as disqualification from obtaining public subsidies and aid, from contracting with entities, organizations or bodies that form part of the public sector and from

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

enjoying tax and Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of two to seven years.

###### Article 437.

The public authority or official who demands, directly or indirectly, fees, tariffs for duties or bills that are not due or in an amount greater than that legally established, shall be punished, without prejudice to the reimbursements to which they may be obliged, with the penalties of a fine of six to twenty-four months and suspension from public employment or office for a period of six months to four years.

###### Article 438.

The public authority or official who, abusing their office, commits any offense of fraud or fraud of benefits of the Social Security System under article 307 ter, shall incur the penalties respectively assigned to these, in their upper half, and may reach the upper degree, and special disqualification from public employment or office and from exercising the right of passive suffrage for a period of three to nine years, unless the acts are punished with a more severe penalty in some other provision of this Code.

###### Article 438 bis.

The authority who, during the performance of their function or office and up to five years after having ceased in them, has obtained an increase in assets or a cancellation of obligations or debts for a value exceeding 250,000 euros with respect to their proven income, and openly refuses to duly comply with the requirements of the competent bodies intended to verify its justification, shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from six months to three years, a fine from the amount to triple the benefit obtained, and special disqualification from public employment or office and from exercising the right of passive suffrage for a period of two to seven years.

##### CHAPTER IX

Of negotiations and activities prohibited to public officials and of abuses in the exercise of their function

###### Article 439.

The public authority or official who, being required to intervene by reason of their office in any type of contract, matter, transaction or activity, takes advantage of such circumstance to force or facilitate for themselves any form of participation, directly or through an intermediary, in such business or proceedings, shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification from public employment or office and from exercising the right of passive suffrage for a period of two to seven years.

###### Article 440.

Expert witnesses, arbitrators and partition accountants who conduct themselves in the manner provided in the preceding article, with respect to the property or things in whose appraisal, partition or award they have intervened, and guardians, curators or executors with respect to those belonging to their wards or estates, and insolvency administrators with respect to the assets and rights integrated in the insolvency estate, shall be punished with the penalty of a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification from public employment or office, profession or trade, guardianship, tutorship or curatorship, as the case may be, for a period of three to six years, unless this conduct is sanctioned with a greater penalty in another provision of this Code.

###### Article 441.

The authority or public official who, outside the cases admitted in laws or regulations, carries out, by himself or through an intermediary, a professional or

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

advisory activity, permanent or occasional, under the dependence or in the service of private entities or individuals, in a matter in which he must intervene or has intervened by reason of his office, or in those that are processed, reported on or resolved in the office or management center where he is assigned or on which he depends, shall incur the penalties of a fine of six to twelve months and suspension from employment or public office for a period of two to five years.

###### Article 442.

The authority or public official who makes use of a secret of which he has knowledge by reason of his office or position, or of privileged information, with intent to obtain an economic benefit for himself or for a third party, shall incur the penalties of a fine of one to three times the benefit pursued, obtained or facilitated and special disqualification from employment or public office and from the exercise of the right of passive suffrage for a period of two to four years. If he obtains the benefit pursued, the penalties of imprisonment of one to three years, a fine of one to six times the benefit pursued, obtained or facilitated and special disqualification from employment or public office and from the exercise of the right of passive suffrage for a period of four to six years shall be imposed.

If serious harm results to the public cause or to a third party, the penalty shall be imprisonment of one to six years, and special disqualification from employment or public office and from the exercise of the right of passive suffrage for a period of nine to twelve years. For the purposes of this article, privileged information is understood to mean any information of a specific nature that is held exclusively by reason of the office or public position and that has not been notified, published or disclosed.

###### Article 443.

1. The authority or public official who sexually solicits a person who, for herself or for her spouse or another person to whom she is linked in a stable manner by an analogous relationship of affection, ascendant, descendant, sibling, by nature, by adoption, or related by affinity in the same degrees, has claims pending resolution by that official or about which he must issue a report or submit a query to his superior, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of one to two years and absolute disqualification for a period of six to 12 years.

2. The official of Penitentiary Institutions, of protection or reform centers for minors, detention centers for foreign persons, or any other detention, or custody center, even for temporary stay, who sexually solicits a person subject to his custody, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of one to four years and absolute disqualification for a period of six to twelve years.

3. The same penalties shall be incurred when the person solicited is an ascendant, descendant, sibling, by nature, by adoption, or related by affinity in the same degrees of a person in his custody. These penalties shall also be incurred when the person solicited is the spouse of a person in his custody or is linked to such person in a stable manner by an analogous relationship of affection.

###### Article 444.

The penalties provided for in the preceding article shall be imposed without prejudice to those corresponding to offenses against sexual freedom actually committed.

##### CHAPTER X

Common provision to the preceding Chapters

###### Article 445.

Incitement, conspiracy and proposal to commit the offenses provided for in this Title shall be punished, respectively, with the penalty lower by one or two degrees.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

#### TITLE XX

Offenses against the Administration of Justice

##### CHAPTER I

On perversion of justice

###### Article 446.

The judge or magistrate who, knowingly, issues an unjust judgment or decision shall be punished:

1.º With the penalty of imprisonment of one to four years if it is an unjust sentence against the defendant in criminal proceedings for a serious or less serious offense and the sentence has not been executed, and with the same penalty in its upper half and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months if it has been executed. In both cases, the penalty of absolute disqualification for a period of ten to twenty years shall also be imposed.

2.º With the penalty of a fine of six to twelve months and special disqualification from employment or public office for a period of six to ten years, if it is an unjust sentence against the defendant issued in proceedings for a minor offense.

3.º With the penalty of a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification from employment or public office for a period of ten to twenty years, when issuing any other unjust judgment or decision.

###### Article 447.

The Judge or Magistrate who through gross negligence or inexcusable ignorance issues a manifestly unjust judgment or decision shall incur the penalty of special disqualification from employment or public office for a period of two to six years.

###### Article 448.

The Judge or Magistrate who refuses to adjudicate, without alleging legal cause, or under pretext of obscurity, insufficiency or silence of the Law, shall be punished with the penalty of special disqualification from employment or public office for a period of six months to four years.

###### Article 449.

1. The same penalty indicated in the preceding article shall be incurred by the Judge, Magistrate or Judicial Secretary guilty of malicious delay in the Administration of Justice. Delay provoked to achieve any illegitimate purpose shall be understood as malicious.

2. When the delay is attributable to an official other than those mentioned in the preceding paragraph, the indicated penalty, in its lower half, shall be imposed on him.

##### CHAPTER II

On the omission of duties to prevent offenses or to promote their prosecution

###### Article 450.

1. Whoever, being able to do so with his immediate intervention and without risk to himself or others, does not prevent the commission of an offense affecting persons in their life, integrity or health, liberty or sexual freedom, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment of six months to two years if the offense is against life, and that of a fine of six to twenty-four months in other cases, unless the offense not prevented carries an equal or lesser penalty, in which case the penalty lower in degree to that one shall be imposed.

2. The same penalties shall be incurred by whoever, being able to do so, does not go to the authority or its agents so that they prevent an offense of those provided for in the preceding paragraph and of whose imminent or ongoing commission he has notice.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

##### CHAPTER III

On concealment

###### Article 451.

Whoever, with knowledge of the commission of a crime and without having intervened in it as author or accomplice, intervenes after its execution in any of the following ways, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to three years: 1.º Assisting the authors or accomplices so that they may benefit from the gain, product or price of the crime, without intent of personal profit.

2.º Concealing, altering or rendering useless the body, effects or instruments of a crime, in order to prevent its discovery.

3.º Helping the alleged perpetrators of a crime to evade investigation by the authority or its agents, or to escape their search or capture, provided that any of the following circumstances is present:

- a) That the concealed act constitutes treason, homicide of the King or the Queen or any of their ascendants or descendants, of the Queen consort or the consort of the Queen, of the Regent or any member of the Regency, or of the Prince or Princess of Asturias, genocide, crime against humanity, crime against persons and property protected in case of armed conflict, rebellion, terrorism, homicide, piracy, human trafficking or illegal organ trafficking.
- b) That the abettor has acted with abuse of public functions. In this case, in addition to the penalty of deprivation of liberty, special disqualification for public employment or office for a period of two to four years shall be imposed if the concealed crime is less serious, and absolute disqualification for a period of six to twelve years if it is serious.

###### Article 452.

In no case may a custodial penalty exceeding that assigned to the concealed crime be imposed. If the latter is punishable by a penalty of another nature, the custodial penalty shall be replaced by a fine of six to twenty-four months, unless the concealed crime has an equal or lesser penalty assigned to it, in which case the offender shall be imposed the penalty for that crime in its lower half.

###### Article 453.

The provisions of this chapter shall apply even when the author of the concealed act is not responsible or is personally exempt from penalty.

###### Article 454.

Those who conceal their spouse or a person to whom they are stably linked by an analogous relationship of affection, their ascendants, descendants, siblings, by nature, by adoption, or relatives by affinity in the same degrees, are exempt from the penalties imposed on concealers, with the sole exception of concealers who fall under the assumption of number 1.º of Article 451.

##### CHAPTER IV

Of the arbitrary exercise of one's own right

###### Article 455.

1. Whoever, in order to exercise their own right, acting outside legal channels, uses violence, intimidation or force against things, shall be punished with a fine of six to twelve months.

2. The penalty one degree higher shall be imposed if weapons or dangerous objects are used for intimidation or violence.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

##### CHAPTER V

Of false accusation and denunciation and the simulation of crimes

###### Article 456.

1. Those who, with knowledge of their falsehood or reckless disregard for the truth, impute to any person acts that, if true, would constitute a criminal offense, if this imputation is made before a judicial or administrative official who has the duty to investigate it, shall be sanctioned:

1.º With imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months, if a serious crime is imputed.

2.º With a fine of twelve to twenty-four months, if a less serious crime is imputed.

3.º With a fine of three to six months, if a minor crime is imputed.

2. No proceedings may be brought against the complainant or accuser except after a final judgment or also final order, of dismissal or closure by the Judge or Court that has heard the imputed offense. These shall order ex officio proceedings against the complainant or accuser whenever sufficient indications of the falsehood of the imputation result from the main case, without prejudice to the fact that the act may also be prosecuted upon complaint by the offended party.

###### Article 457.

Whoever, before any of the officials referred to in the preceding article, simulates being responsible for or a victim of a criminal offense or reports a non-existent one, provoking procedural actions, shall be punished with a fine of six to twelve months.

##### CHAPTER VI

Of false testimony

###### Article 458.

1. The witness who fails to tell the truth in their testimony in a judicial case shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine of three to six months.

2. If the false testimony is given against the accused in a criminal case for a crime, the penalties shall be imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of six to twelve months. If a conviction has been handed down as a result of the testimony, penalties one degree higher shall be imposed.

3. The same penalties shall be imposed if the false testimony takes place before International Tribunals that, by virtue of Treaties duly ratified in accordance with the Spanish Constitution, exercise powers derived from it, or is carried out in Spain when testifying pursuant to a letter rogatory sent by a foreign Court.

###### Article 459.

The penalties of the preceding articles shall be imposed in their upper half on experts or interpreters who maliciously fail to tell the truth in their opinion or translation, who shall also be punished with special disqualification for profession or trade, employment or public office, for a period of six to twelve years.

###### Article 460.

When the witness, expert or interpreter, without substantially failing to tell the truth, alters it with reticence, inaccuracies or by concealing relevant facts or data known to them, they shall be punished with a fine of six to twelve months and, where applicable, suspension from employment or public office, profession or trade, from six months to three years.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Article 461.

1. Whoever knowingly presents false witnesses or mendacious experts or interpreters shall be punished with the same penalties established for them in the preceding articles.

2. If the person responsible for this crime is a lawyer, attorney, social graduate or representative of the Public Prosecutor's Office, in professional action or exercise of their function, the penalty in its upper half and special disqualification for employment or public office, profession or trade, for a period of two to four years shall be imposed in each case.

###### Article 462.

Whoever, having given false testimony in a criminal case, retracts in due time and form, stating the truth so that it takes effect before a judgment is issued in the case in question, shall be exempt from penalty. If, as a consequence of the false testimony, deprivation of liberty has occurred, the corresponding penalties one degree lower shall be imposed.

##### CHAPTER VII

Of obstruction of Justice and professional disloyalty

###### Artículo 463.

1. Whoever, having been legally summoned, voluntarily fails to appear, without just cause, before a court or tribunal in criminal proceedings with a defendant in provisional detention, causing the suspension of the oral trial, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from three to six months or a fine from six to 24 months. A penalty of a fine from six to 10 months shall be incurred by whoever, having been warned, does so for a second time in criminal proceedings without a defendant in detention, whether or not the suspension has been caused.

2. If the person responsible for this offense is a lawyer, procurator or representative of the Public Prosecutor's Office, in professional performance or exercise of their function, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half and that of special disqualification for employment or public office, profession or trade, for a period of two to four years.

3. If the suspension takes place, in the case of paragraph 1 of this article, as a consequence of the non-appearance of the judge or member of the tribunal or of whoever exercises the functions of court clerk, the penalty of imprisonment from three to six months or a fine from six to 24 months shall be imposed and, in any case, special disqualification for a period of two to four years.

###### Artículo 464.

1. Whoever with violence or intimidation attempts to influence directly or indirectly a complainant, party or accused, lawyer, procurator, expert, interpreter or witness in a proceeding to modify their procedural action, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from one to four years and a fine from six to twenty-four months.

If the perpetrator of the act achieves their objective, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half.

2. The same penalties shall be imposed on whoever carries out any act against life, integrity, liberty, sexual freedom or property, as retaliation against the persons mentioned in the previous paragraph, for their action in judicial proceedings, without prejudice to the penalty corresponding to the offense of which such acts are constitutive.

###### Artículo 465.

1. Whoever, intervening in a proceeding as a lawyer or procurator, with abuse of their function, destroys, renders useless or conceals documents or proceedings of which they have received a copy in that capacity, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years, a fine from seven to twelve months and special disqualification for their profession, employment or public office from three to six years.

2. If the acts described in the first paragraph of this article were carried out by a private individual, the penalty shall be a fine from three to six months.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Artículo 466.

1. The lawyer or procurator who discloses procedural actions declared secret by the judicial authority, shall be punished with penalties of a fine from twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification for employment, public office, profession or trade from one to four years.

2. If the disclosure of the actions declared secret is carried out by the Judge or member of the Court, representative of the Public Prosecutor's Office, Court Clerk or any official in the service of the Administration of Justice, the penalties provided for in article 417 shall be imposed in their upper half.

3. If the conduct described in the first paragraph is carried out by any other private individual who intervenes in the proceeding, the penalty shall be imposed in its lower half.

###### Artículo 467.

1. The lawyer or procurator who, having advised or taken the defense or representation of a person, without their consent defends or represents in the same matter someone who has contrary interests, shall be punished with a penalty of a fine from six to twelve months and special disqualification for their profession from two to four years.

2. The lawyer or procurator who, by action or omission, manifestly harms the interests entrusted to them shall be punished with penalties of a fine from twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification for employment, public office, profession or trade from one to four years.

If the acts were carried out through gross negligence, the penalties of a fine from six to twelve months and special disqualification for their profession from six months to two years shall be imposed.

##### CAPÍTULO VIII

On the violation of sentence

###### Artículo 468.

1. Those who violate their sentence, security measure, imprisonment, precautionary measure, conveyance or custody shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year if they were deprived of liberty, and with a penalty of a fine from twelve to twenty-four months in other cases.

2. In any case, the penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year shall be imposed on those who violate a penalty of those contemplated in article 48 of this Code or a precautionary or security measure of the same nature imposed in criminal proceedings in which the victim is one of the persons referred to in article 173.2, as well as on those who violate the measure of supervised release.

3. Those who render useless or disturb the normal functioning of the technical devices that have been arranged to monitor compliance with penalties, security measures or precautionary measures, do not carry them or omit the measures required to maintain their correct functioning state, shall be punished with a penalty of a fine from six to twelve months.

###### Artículo 469.

Convicted persons or prisoners who escape from the place where they are confined, using violence or intimidation against persons or force against things or taking part in a mutiny, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to four years.

###### Artículo 470.

1. The private individual who provides escape to a convicted person, prisoner or detainee, either from the place where they are confined, or during their conveyance, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine from twelve to twenty-four months.

2. If violence or intimidation against persons, force against things or bribery is employed for this purpose, the penalty shall be imprisonment from six months to four years.

3. If it concerns any of the persons mentioned in article 454, they shall be punished with a penalty of a fine from three to six months, being able in this case the Judge or Court to impose only

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

the penalties corresponding to the damages caused or to the threats or violence exerted.

###### Artículo 471.

The penalty one degree higher shall be imposed, in their respective cases, if the guilty party is a public official in charge of the conveyance or custody of a convicted person, prisoner or detainee. The official shall additionally be punished with the penalty of special disqualification for employment or public office from six to ten years if the fugitive was convicted by enforceable judgment, and with special disqualification for employment or public office from three to six years in other cases.

##### CAPÍTULO IX

On offenses against the Administration of Justice of the International Criminal Court

###### Article 471 bis.

1. A witness who intentionally departs from the truth in his or her testimony before the International Criminal Court, being obliged to tell the truth in accordance with the statutory norms and rules of procedure and evidence of said Court, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to two years. If the false testimony is given against the accused, the penalty shall be imprisonment from two to four years. If as a consequence of the testimony a conviction is handed down, a penalty of imprisonment from four to five years shall be imposed.

2. Anyone who presents evidence before the International Criminal Court knowing it to be false or to have been falsified shall be punished with the penalties set out in the preceding paragraph of this article.

3. Anyone who intentionally destroys or alters evidence, or interferes with evidentiary proceedings before the International Criminal Court shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine of seven to 12 months.

4. Anyone who corrupts a witness, obstructs his or her appearance or testimony before the International Criminal Court or interferes with them shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from one to four years and a fine of six to 24 months.

5. Anyone who impedes an official of the Court, corrupts or intimidates him or her, in order to compel or induce him or her not to perform his or her functions or to do so improperly, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to four years and a fine of six to 24 months.

6. Anyone who takes reprisals against an official of the International Criminal Court by reason of functions that he or she or another official has performed shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from one to four years and a fine of six to 24 months.

Anyone who takes reprisals against a witness for his or her statement before the Court shall incur the same penalty.

7. Anyone who solicits or accepts a bribe in the capacity of an official of the Court and in relation to his or her official functions shall incur a penalty of imprisonment from two to five years and a fine of one to three times the value of the gift solicited or accepted.

#### TITLE XXI

Crimes against the Constitution

##### CHAPTER I

Rebellion

###### Article 472.

Those who rise up violently and publicly for any of the following purposes are guilty of the crime of rebellion:

1.º To repeal, suspend or modify in whole or in part the Constitution.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

2.º To remove or deprive in whole or in part the King or Queen, the Regent or members of the Regency of their prerogatives and powers, or to compel them to perform an act contrary to their will.

3.º To prevent the free holding of elections for public office.

4.º To dissolve the Cortes Generales, the Congress of Deputies, the Senate or any Legislative Assembly of an Autonomous Community, to prevent them from meeting, deliberating or resolving, to extract some resolution from them or to remove some of their powers or competences.

5.º To declare the independence of a part of the national territory.

6.º To replace the Government of the Nation or the Governing Council of an Autonomous Community with another, or to use or exercise for oneself or to deprive the Government or Governing Council of an Autonomous Community, or any of its members, of their powers, or to impede or restrict their free exercise, or to compel any of them to perform acts contrary to their will.

7.º To withdraw any kind of armed force from obedience to the Government.

###### Article 473.

1. Those who, by inducing the rebels, have promoted or sustain the rebellion, and the principal leaders thereof, shall be punished with imprisonment from fifteen to twenty-five years and absolute disqualification for the same period; those who exercise subordinate command, with imprisonment from ten to fifteen years and absolute disqualification from ten to fifteen years, and mere participants, with imprisonment from five to ten years and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of six to ten years.

2. If weapons have been brandished, or if there has been combat between the force under their command and sectors loyal to the legitimate authority, or the rebellion has caused devastation to publicly or privately owned property, cut telegraph, telephone, radio wave, railway or other communications, exercised serious violence against persons, exacted contributions or diverted public funds from their lawful use, the penalties of imprisonment shall be, respectively, twenty-five to thirty years for the first, fifteen to twenty-five years for the second and ten to fifteen years for the last.

###### Article 474.

When the rebellion has not come to be organized with known leaders, those who in fact direct the others, or speak for them, or sign documents issued in their name, or perform other similar acts of direction or representation shall be deemed as such.

###### Article 475.

Those who seduce or recruit troops or any other kind of armed force to commit the crime of rebellion shall be punished as rebels with imprisonment from five to ten years and absolute disqualification for a period of six to twelve years.

If the rebellion actually takes place, they shall be deemed promoters and shall suffer the penalty set out in article 473.

###### Article 476.

1. A military officer who does not employ the means at his or her disposal to contain the rebellion in the forces under his or her command shall be punished with imprisonment from two to five years and absolute disqualification from six to ten years.

2. A military officer who, having knowledge that a crime of rebellion is about to be committed, does not immediately report it to his or her superiors or to the authorities or officials who, by reason of their office, have the obligation to prosecute the crime, shall be punished with the same penalties provided in the preceding paragraph in their lower half.

###### Article 477.

Incitement, conspiracy and proposal to commit rebellion shall be punished, in addition to the disqualification provided for in the preceding articles, with imprisonment one or two degrees lower than that for the corresponding crime.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Article 478.

In the case that the person who commits any of the crimes provided for in this chapter is constituted as an authority, the disqualification penalty provided for in each case shall be replaced by absolute disqualification for a period of fifteen to twenty years, unless such circumstance is specifically contemplated in the criminal offense in question.

###### Article 479.

As soon as the rebellion manifests itself, the governmental authority shall call upon the insurgents to immediately disperse and withdraw.

If the insurgents do not abandon their stance immediately after the call, the authority shall make use of the force at its disposal to disperse them.

The call shall not be necessary from the moment the rebels open fire.

###### Article 480.

1. Anyone who, implicated in a crime of rebellion, reports it in time to prevent its consequences, shall be exempt from penalty.

2. Mere executors who lay down their arms before having used them, submitting themselves to the legitimate authorities, shall be given imprisonment one degree lower. The same penalty shall be imposed if the rebels disperse or submit to the legitimate authority before the call or as a consequence of it.

###### Article 481.

Particular crimes committed in a rebellion or on the occasion of it shall be punished, respectively, according to the provisions of this Code.

###### Article 482.

Authorities who have not resisted the rebellion shall be punished with absolute disqualification from twelve to twenty years.

###### Article 483.

Officials who continue to perform their duties under the command of the insurgents or who, without having had their resignation from their post accepted, abandon it when there is danger of rebellion, shall incur the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office from six to twelve years.

###### Artículo 484.

Those who accept employment from the rebels shall be punished with the penalty of absolute disqualification from six to twelve years.

##### CAPÍTULO II

Offenses against the Crown

###### Artículo 485.

1. Whoever kills the King or the Queen or the Prince or the Princess of Asturias shall be punished with the penalty of reviewable permanent imprisonment.

2. Whoever kills any of the ascendants or descendants of the King or the Queen, the Queen consort or the consort of the Queen, the Regent or any member of the Regency, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from twenty to twenty-five years, unless the acts were punished with a more severe penalty in any other provision of this Code.

If two or more aggravating circumstances concur in the offense, the penalty of imprisonment from twenty-five to thirty years shall be imposed.

3. In the case of attempt of these offenses, the penalty lower by one degree may be imposed.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Artículo 486.

1. Whoever causes to the King, the Queen or any of their ascendants or descendants, the Queen consort or the consort of the Queen, the Regent or any member of the Regency, or the Prince or the Princess of Asturias, injuries of those provided for in article 149, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years.

If it were any of the injuries provided for in article 150, it shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from eight to fifteen years.

2. Whoever causes them any other injury shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from four to eight years.

###### Artículo 487.
