Whoever deprives the King, the Queen or any of their ascendants or descendants, the Queen consort or the consort of the Queen, the Regent or any member of the Regency, or the Prince or the Princess of Asturias, of their personal liberty shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years, unless the acts are punished with a greater penalty in other provisions of this Code.

###### Artículo 488.

Provocation, conspiracy and proposal for the offenses provided for in the preceding articles shall be punished with the penalty lower by one or two degrees than those respectively provided for.

###### Artículo 489.

Whoever by violence or serious intimidation compels the persons referred to in the preceding articles to perform an act against their will shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from eight to twelve years.

In the case provided for in the preceding paragraph, if the violence or intimidation were not serious, the penalty lower by one degree shall be imposed.

###### Artículo 490.

1. Whoever enters by violence or intimidation the dwelling of any of the persons mentioned in the preceding articles shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from three to six years. If there were no violence or intimidation, the penalty shall be from two to four years.

2. With the penalty of imprisonment from three to six years shall be punished whoever seriously threatens any of the persons mentioned in the preceding paragraph, and with the penalty of imprisonment from one to three years if the threat were minor.

3. Whoever slanders or insults the King, the Queen or any of their ascendants or descendants, the Queen consort or the consort of the Queen, the Regent or any member of the Regency, or the Prince or the Princess of Asturias, in the exercise of their functions or on the occasion thereof, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years if the slander or insult were serious, and with a fine from six to twelve months if they are not.

###### Artículo 491.

1. Slanders and insults against any of the persons mentioned in the preceding article, and outside the cases provided for therein, shall be punished with the penalty of a fine from four to twenty months.

2. The penalty of a fine from six to twenty-four months shall be imposed on whoever uses the image of the King or the Queen or any of their ascendants or descendants, or of the Queen consort or the consort of the Queen, or of the Regent or any member of the Regency, or of the Prince or the Princess of Asturias, in any way that may damage the prestige of the Crown.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

##### CAPÍTULO III

Offenses against the Institutions of the State and the separation of powers

###### Sección 1.ª Offenses against the institutions of the State

###### Artículo 492.

Those who, when the Crown is vacant or its Holder is disqualified from exercising his authority, prevent the General Courts from meeting to appoint the Regency or the guardian of the minor Holder, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from ten to fifteen years and absolute disqualification for a period of ten to fifteen years, without prejudice to the penalty that may correspond to them for the commission of other more serious offenses.

###### Artículo 493.

Those who, without rising publicly, invade by force, violence or intimidation the seats of the Congress of Deputies, the Senate or a Legislative Assembly of an Autonomous Community, if they are in session, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from three to five years.

###### Artículo 494.

Those who promote, direct or preside over demonstrations or other types of gatherings before the seats of the Congress of Deputies, the Senate or a Legislative Assembly of an Autonomous Community, when they are in session, disrupting their normal functioning, shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year or a fine from twelve to twenty-four months.

###### Artículo 495.

1. Those who, without rising publicly, carrying weapons or other dangerous instruments, attempt to enter the seats of the Congress of Deputies, the Senate or the Legislative Assembly of an Autonomous Community, to present petitions to them in person or collectively, shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from three to five years.

2. The penalty provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be applied in its upper half to those who promote, direct or preside over the group.

###### Artículo 496.

Whoever seriously insults the General Courts or a Legislative Assembly of an Autonomous Community, while in session, or any of their Committees in the public acts in which they represent them, shall be punished with the penalty of a fine from twelve to eighteen months.

The accused of the insults described in the preceding paragraph shall be exempt from penalty if the circumstances provided for in article 210 are met.

###### Artículo 497.

1. Those who, without being members of the Congress of Deputies, the Senate or a Legislative Assembly of an Autonomous Community, seriously disturb the order of their sessions shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year.

2. When the disturbance of the order of the sessions referred to in the preceding paragraph is not serious, the penalty of a fine from six to twelve months shall be imposed.

###### Artículo 498.

Those who employ force, violence, intimidation or serious threat to prevent a member of the Congress of Deputies, the Senate or a Legislative Assembly of an Autonomous Community from attending their meetings, or, by the same means, restrict the free expression of their opinions or the casting of their vote, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from three to five years.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Artículo 499.

The authority or public official who violates the inviolability of the General Courts or a Legislative Assembly of an Autonomous Community shall be punished with the penalties of special disqualification for public employment or office for a period of ten to twenty years, without prejudice to those that may correspond if the act constituted another more serious offense.

###### Artículo 500.

The authority or public official who detains a member of the General Courts or a Legislative Assembly of an Autonomous Community outside the cases or without the requirements established by the legislation in force shall incur, as the case may be, the penalties provided for in this Code, imposed in their upper half, and additionally special disqualification for public employment or office from six to twelve years.

###### Artículo 501.

The judicial authority who charges or prosecutes a member of the Cortes Generales or of a Legislative Assembly of an Autonomous Community without the requirements established by current legislation shall be punished with the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for ten to twenty years.

###### Artículo 502.

1. Those who, having been summoned in legal form and under warning, fail to appear before an Investigation Committee of the Cortes Generales or of a Legislative Assembly of an Autonomous Community, shall be punished as guilty of the offense of disobedience. If the offender is an authority or public official, the penalty of suspension from public employment or office for a period of six months to two years shall also be imposed.

2. The authority or official who obstructs the investigation of the Ombudsman, Court of Auditors or equivalent bodies of the Autonomous Communities, by refusing or unduly delaying the submission of the reports requested by them or by hindering their access to the files or administrative documentation necessary for such investigation, shall incur the same penalties.

3. Whoever summoned before a parliamentary investigation committee fails to tell the truth in their testimony shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year or a fine of 12 to 24 months.

###### Artículo 503.

The following shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from two to four years:

1.º Those who violently or with intimidation invade the premises where the Council of Ministers or a Council of Government of an Autonomous Community is constituted.

2.º Those who restrict or by any means place obstacles to the freedom of the Government meeting in Council or of the members of a Government of an Autonomous Community, meeting in Council, except when the acts constitute another more serious offense.

###### Artículo 504.

1. Those who slander, insult or seriously threaten the Government of the Nation, the General Council of the Judiciary, the Constitutional Court, the Supreme Court, or the Council of Government or the High Court of Justice of an Autonomous Community shall incur the penalty of a fine of twelve to eighteen months.

The person guilty of slander or insults in accordance with the provisions of the preceding paragraph shall be exempt from penalty if the circumstances provided for in articles 207 and 210 of this Code, respectively, are met.

The penalty of imprisonment from three to five years shall be imposed on those who use force, violence or intimidation to prevent the members of said Bodies from attending their respective meetings.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

2. Those who insult or seriously threaten the Armed Forces, Classes or Corps and Security Forces, shall be punished with the penalty of a fine of twelve to eighteen months. The person guilty of the insults provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be exempt from penalty if the circumstances described in article 210 of this Code are met.

###### Artículo 505.

1. Those who, without being members of the local corporation, seriously disturb the order of its plenary sessions by preventing access to them, the development of the scheduled agenda, the adoption of agreements or cause disorders intended to express support for terrorist organizations or groups, shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year.

2. Those who, sheltering in the existence of terrorist organizations or groups, slander, insult, coerce or threaten members of local corporations, shall be punished with the penalty one degree higher than that corresponding to the offense committed.

###### Sección 2.ª Usurpation of powers

###### Artículo 506.

The authority or public official who, lacking the powers to do so, issues a general provision or suspends its execution, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from one to three years, a fine of six to twelve months and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of six to twelve years.

###### Artículo 506 bis.

(Repealed)

###### Artículo 507.

The Judge or Magistrate who assumes administrative powers which they lack, or prevents their legitimate exercise by the person who holds them, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year, a fine of three to six months and suspension from public employment or office for a period of one to three years.

###### Artículo 508.

1. The authority or public official who assumes judicial powers or prevents the execution of a resolution issued by the competent judicial authority, shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from six months to one year, a fine of three to eight months and suspension from public employment or office for a period of one to three years.

2. The administrative or military authority or official who attacks the independence of Judges or Magistrates, guaranteed by the Constitution, by issuing them instructions, orders or demands relating to cases or proceedings they are hearing, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from one to two years, a fine of four to ten months and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of two to six years.

###### Artículo 509.

The Judge or Magistrate, the authority or the public official who, legally required to abstain, continues to proceed without waiting for the corresponding jurisdictional conflict to be decided, except in cases permitted by Law, shall be punished with the penalty of a fine of three to ten months and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of six months to one year.

##### CAPÍTULO IV

Offenses relating to the exercise of fundamental rights and public freedoms.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Sección 1.ª Offenses committed on the occasion of the exercise of rights

fundamental and public freedoms guaranteed by the Constitution

###### Article 510.

1. The following shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to four years and a fine of six to twelve months:

- a) Those who publicly foment, promote or incite directly or indirectly hatred, hostility, discrimination or violence against a group, a part thereof or against a specific person by reason of their membership in that group, on racist, anti-Semitic, anti-Romani or other grounds relating to ideology, religion or beliefs, family status, the membership of its members in an ethnicity, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, sexual orientation or identity, for reasons of gender, aporophobia, illness or disability.
- b) Those who produce, prepare, possess with the purpose of distributing, provide third parties access to, distribute, disseminate or sell writings or any other kind of material or media that by their content are suitable for fomenting, promoting, or inciting directly or indirectly hatred, hostility, discrimination or violence against a group, a part thereof, or against a specific person by reason of their membership in that group, on racist, anti-Semitic, anti-Romani or other grounds relating to ideology, religion or beliefs, family status, the membership of its members in an ethnicity, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, sexual orientation or identity, for reasons of gender, aporophobia, illness or disability.
- c) Those who publicly deny, seriously trivialize or glorify the crimes of genocide, against humanity or against persons and property protected in case of armed conflict, or glorify their perpetrators, when they have been committed against a group or a part thereof, or against a specific person by reason of their membership in it, on racist, anti-Semitic, anti-Romani, or other grounds relating to ideology, religion or beliefs, family status or the membership of its members in an ethnicity, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, sexual orientation or identity, for reasons of gender, aporophobia, illness or disability, when in this manner a climate of violence, hostility, hatred or discrimination against them is promoted or favored.
  - 2. The following shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to two years and a fine of six to twelve months:
- a) Those who injure the dignity of persons through actions that entail humiliation, contempt or discredit of any of the groups referred to in the preceding paragraph, or of a part thereof, or of any specific person by reason of their membership in them on racist, anti-Semitic, anti-Romani or other grounds relating to ideology, religion or beliefs, family status, the membership of its members in an ethnicity, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, sexual orientation or identity, for reasons of gender, aporophobia, illness or disability, or who produce, prepare, possess with the purpose of distributing, provide third parties access to, distribute, disseminate or sell writings or any other kind of material or media that by their content are suitable for injuring the dignity of persons by representing a serious humiliation, contempt or discredit of any of the mentioned groups, of a part thereof, or of any specific person by reason of their membership in them.
- b) Those who glorify or justify by any means of public expression or dissemination the crimes that have been committed against a group, a part thereof, or against a specific person by reason of their membership in that group on racist, anti-Semitic, anti-Romani or other grounds relating to ideology, religion or beliefs, family status, the membership of its members in an ethnicity, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, sexual orientation or identity, for reasons of gender, aporophobia, illness or disability, or those who have participated in their commission.

The acts shall be punished with a sentence of one to four years of imprisonment and a fine of six to twelve months when in that manner a climate of violence, hostility, hatred or discrimination against the mentioned groups is promoted or favored.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

3. The penalties provided for in the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed in their upper half when the acts have been carried out through a social media outlet, by means of the internet or through the use of information technologies, in such a way that it becomes accessible to a large number of persons.

4. When the acts, in view of their circumstances, are suitable for disturbing public peace or creating a serious feeling of insecurity or fear among the members of the group, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half, which may be raised to the next degree.

5. In all cases, the penalty of special disqualification for educational profession or occupation, in the teaching, sports and leisure sphere, shall also be imposed for a period between three and ten years greater than the duration of the custodial sentence imposed where applicable in the judgment, taking into account proportionally the seriousness of the crime, the number of those committed and the circumstances present in the offender.

6. The judge or court shall order the destruction, deletion or rendering useless of books, files, documents, articles and any kind of media that are the object of the crime referred to in the preceding paragraphs or by means of which it has been committed. When the crime has been committed through information and communication technologies, the removal of the contents shall be ordered.

In cases where, through an internet access portal or information society service, the contents referred to in the preceding paragraph are disseminated exclusively or predominantly, the blocking of access or the interruption of the provision thereof shall be ordered.

###### Article 510 bis.

When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the crimes comprised in the two preceding articles, it shall be imposed with the penalty of a fine of two to five years. Considering the rules established in article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set forth in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33.

In this case, the provisions of number 3 of article 510 of the Criminal Code shall be equally applicable.

###### Artículo 511.

1. A private individual in charge of a public service who denies a person a benefit to which they are entitled by reason of their ideology, religion or beliefs, their family situation, belonging to an ethnicity, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, age, sexual orientation or gender identity, reasons of gender, aporophobia or social exclusion, the illness they suffer from or their disability, shall be liable to a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of one to three years.

2. The same penalties shall apply when the acts are committed against an association, foundation, company or corporation or against its members by reason of their ideology, religion or beliefs, their family situation, the belonging of its members or any of them to an ethnicity, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, age, sexual orientation or gender identity, reasons of gender, aporophobia or social exclusion, the illness they suffer from or their disability.

3. Public officials who commit any of the acts provided for in this article shall be liable to the same penalties in their upper half and to special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of two to four years.

4. In all cases, the penalty of special disqualification for educational profession or occupation, in the teaching, sports and leisure fields, shall also be imposed for a period between one and three years longer than the duration of the penalty imposed if this were deprivation of liberty; when the penalty imposed were a fine, the penalty of special disqualification shall have a duration of one to three years. In any case, regard shall be had proportionately to the seriousness of the offense and to the circumstances present in the offender.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Artículo 512.

Those who, in the exercise of their professional or business activities, deny a person a benefit to which they are entitled by reason of their ideology, religion or beliefs, their family situation, their belonging to an ethnicity, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, age, sexual orientation or gender identity, reasons of gender, aporophobia or social exclusion, the illness they suffer from or their disability, shall be liable to the penalty of special disqualification from the exercise of profession, occupation, industry or trade and special disqualification for educational profession or occupation, in the teaching, sports and leisure fields for a period of one to four years.

###### Artículo 513.

Unlawful meetings or demonstrations are punishable, and the following are considered as such: 1.º Those held for the purpose of committing any offense.

2.º Those at which persons attend with weapons, explosive devices or blunt objects or in any other dangerous manner.

###### Artículo 514.

1. The promoters or leaders of any meeting or demonstration included in number 1.º of the preceding article and those who, in relation to number 2.º of the same, have not tried to prevent by all means at their disposal the circumstances mentioned therein, shall be liable to the penalties of imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months. For these purposes, those who convene or preside over them shall be deemed leaders or promoters of the meeting or demonstration.

2. Those attending a meeting or demonstration who carry weapons or other equally dangerous means shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from one to two years and a fine of six to twelve months. The Judges or Courts, taking into account the background of the subject, circumstances of the case and characteristics of the weapon or instrument carried, may reduce the prescribed penalty by one degree.

3. Persons who, on the occasion of the holding of a meeting or demonstration, commit acts of violence against the authorities, their agents, persons or public or private property, shall be punished with the penalty corresponding to their offense, in its upper half.

4. Those who prevent the lawful exercise of the freedoms of assembly or demonstration, or seriously disturb the development of a lawful meeting or demonstration shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from two to three years if the acts are carried out with violence, and with the penalty of imprisonment from three to six months or a fine of six to 12 months if committed by means of assault or any other unlawful procedure.

5. The promoters or leaders of any meeting or demonstration who convene, hold or attempt to hold again a meeting or demonstration that has been previously suspended or prohibited, and provided that thereby they intended to subvert the constitutional order or seriously disturb the public peace, shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine of six to twelve months, without prejudice to the penalty that may correspond, where applicable, in accordance with the preceding paragraphs.

###### Artículo 515.

Unlawful associations are punishable, the following being considered as such:

1.º Those whose purpose is to commit any offense or, after being established, promote its commission.

2.º Those which, even having a lawful purpose, employ violent means or means of alteration or control of the personality for its achievement.

3.º Organizations of a paramilitary nature.

4.º Those that encourage, promote or incite directly or indirectly to hatred, hostility, discrimination or violence against persons, groups or associations by reason of their ideology, religion or beliefs, the belonging of their members or any of them to an ethnicity, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, age, sexual orientation or gender identity,

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

reasons of gender, aporophobia or social exclusion, family situation, illness or disability.

###### Artículo 516.

(Deleted)

###### Artículo 517.

In the cases provided for in numbers 1.º and 3.º to 6.º of article 515 (*) the following penalties shall be imposed:

1.º For the founders, leaders and presidents of the associations, imprisonment from two to four years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of six to twelve years.

2.º For active members, imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

(*) The reference to numbers 1º and 3º to 6º of art. 515 is understood to be made to the current numbers 1º to 4º of art. 515, following the modification made by sole art. 239 of L.O. 1/2015, of 30 March. Ref. BOE-A-2015-3439.

###### Artículo 518.

Those who, with their economic cooperation or of any other kind, in any case relevant, favor the foundation, organization or activity of the associations included in numbers 1.o and 3.o to 6.o of article 515(*), shall be liable to the penalty of imprisonment from one to three years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months, and disqualification from public employment or office for a period of one to four years.

(*) The reference to numbers 1º and 3º to 6º of art. 515 is understood to be made to the current numbers 1º to 4º of art. 515, following the modification made by sole art. 239 of L.O. 1/2015, of 30 March. Ref. BOE-A-2015-3439.

###### Artículo 519.

Provocation, conspiracy and proposal to commit the offense of unlawful association shall be punished with a penalty one or two degrees lower than that which corresponds, respectively, to the acts provided for in the preceding articles.

###### Artículo 520.

The Judges or Courts, in the cases provided for in article 515, shall order the dissolution of the unlawful association and, where applicable, any other of the ancillary consequences of article 129 of this Code.

###### Artículo 521.

In the offense of unlawful association, if the offender were an authority, agent thereof or public official, the penalty of absolute disqualification from ten to fifteen years shall be imposed on him, in addition to the prescribed penalties.

###### Artículo 521 bis.

(Deleted)

###### Sección 2.ª De los delitos contra la libertad de conciencia, los sentimientos

religiosos y el respeto a los difuntos

###### Artículo 522.

Incurrirán en la pena de multa de cuatro a diez meses:

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

1.º Los que por medio de violencia, intimidación, fuerza o cualquier otro apremio ilegítimo impidan a un miembro o miembros de una confesión religiosa practicar los actos propios de las creencias que profesen, o asistir a los mismos.

2.º Los que por iguales medios fuercen a otro u otros a practicar o concurrir a actos de culto o ritos, o a realizar actos reveladores de profesar o no profesar una religión, o a mudar la que profesen.

###### Artículo 523.

El que con violencia, amenaza, tumulto o vías de hecho, impidiere, interrumpiere o perturbare los actos, funciones, ceremonias o manifestaciones de las confesiones religiosas inscritas en el correspondiente registro público del Ministerio de Justicia e Interior, será castigado con la pena de prisión de seis meses a seis años, si el hecho se ha cometido en lugar destinado al culto, y con la de multa de cuatro a diez meses si se realiza en cualquier otro lugar.

###### Artículo 524.

El que en templo, lugar destinado al culto o en ceremonias religiosas ejecutare actos de profanación en ofensa de los sentimientos religiosos legalmente tutelados será castigado con la pena de prisión de seis meses a un año o multa de 12 a 24 meses.

###### Artículo 525.

1. Incurrirán en la pena de multa de ocho a doce meses los que, para ofender los sentimientos de los miembros de una confesión religiosa, hagan públicamente, de palabra, por escrito o mediante cualquier tipo de documento, escarnio de sus dogmas, creencias, ritos o ceremonias, o vejen, también públicamente, a quienes los profesan o practican.

2. En las mismas penas incurrirán los que hagan públicamente escarnio, de palabra o por escrito, de quienes no profesan religión o creencia alguna.

###### Artículo 526.

El que, faltando al respeto debido a la memoria de los muertos, violare los sepulcros o sepulturas, profanare un cadáver o sus cenizas o, con ánimo de ultraje, destruyere, alterare o dañare las urnas funerarias, panteones, lápidas o nichos será castigado con la pena de prisión de tres a cinco meses o multa de seis a 10 meses.

###### Artículo 527.

(Sin contenido)

###### Artículo 528.

(Derogado)

##### CAPÍTULO V

De los delitos cometidos por los funcionarios públicos contra las garantías constitucionales

###### Sección 1.ª De los delitos cometidos por los funcionarios públicos contra la

libertad individual

###### Artículo 529.

1. El Juez o Magistrado que entregare una causa criminal a otra autoridad o funcionario, militar o administrativo, que ilegalmente se la reclame, será castigado con la pena de inhabilitación especial para empleo o cargo público por tiempo de seis meses a dos años.

2. Si además entregara la persona de un detenido, se le impondrá la pena superior en grado.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Artículo 530.

La autoridad o funcionario público que, mediando causa por delito, acordare, practicare o prolongare cualquier privación de libertad de un detenido, preso o sentenciado, con violación de los plazos o demás garantías constitucionales o legales, será castigado con la pena de inhabilitación especial para empleo o cargo público por tiempo de cuatro a ocho años.

###### Artículo 531.

La autoridad o funcionario público que, mediando causa por delito, decretare, practicare o prolongare la incomunicación de un detenido, preso o sentenciado, con violación de los plazos o demás garantías constitucionales o legales, será castigado con la pena de inhabilitación especial para empleo o cargo público por tiempo de dos a seis años.

###### Artículo 532.

Si los hechos descritos en los dos artículos anteriores fueran cometidos por imprudencia grave, se castigarán con la pena de suspensión de empleo o cargo público por tiempo de seis meses a dos años.

###### Artículo 533.

El funcionario penitenciario o de centros de protección o corrección de menores que impusiere a los reclusos o internos sanciones o privaciones indebidas, o usare con ellos de un rigor innecesario, será castigado con la pena de inhabilitación especial para empleo o cargo público por tiempo de dos a seis años.

###### Sección 2.ª De los delitos cometidos por los funcionarios públicos contra la

inviolabilidad domiciliaria y demás garantías de la intimidad

###### Artículo 534.

1. Será castigado con las penas de multa de seis a doce meses e inhabilitación especial para empleo o cargo público de dos a seis años la autoridad o funcionario público que, mediando causa por delito, y sin respetar las garantías constitucionales o legales: 1.º Entre en un domicilio sin el consentimiento del morador.

2.º Registre los papeles o documentos de una persona o los efectos que se hallen en su domicilio, a no ser que el dueño haya prestado libremente su consentimiento.

Si no devolviera al dueño, inmediatamente después del registro, los papeles, documentos y efectos registrados, las penas serán las de inhabilitación especial para empleo o cargo público de seis a doce años y multa de doce a veinticuatro meses, sin perjuicio de la pena que pudiera corresponderle por la apropiación.

2. La autoridad o funcionario público que, con ocasión de lícito registro de papeles, documentos o efectos de una persona, cometa cualquier vejación injusta o daño innecesario en sus bienes, será castigado con las penas previstas para estos hechos, impuestas en su mitad superior, y, además, con la pena de inhabilitación especial para empleo o cargo público por tiempo de dos a seis años.

###### Artículo 535.

La autoridad o funcionario público que, mediando causa por delito, interceptare cualquier clase de correspondencia privada, postal o telegráfica, con violación de las garantías constitucionales o legales, incurrirá en la pena de inhabilitación especial para empleo o cargo público de dos a seis años.

Si divulgara o revelara la información obtenida, se impondrá la pena de inhabilitación especial, en su mitad superior, y, además, la de multa de seis a dieciocho meses.

###### Artículo 536.

La autoridad, funcionario público o agente de éstos que, mediando causa por delito, interceptare las telecomunicaciones o utilizare artificios técnicos de escuchas, transmisión,

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

grabación o reproducción del sonido, de la imagen o de cualquier otra señal de comunicación, con violación de las garantías constitucionales o legales, incurrirá en la pena de inhabilitación especial para empleo o cargo público de dos a seis años.

Si divulgare o revelare la información obtenida, se impondrán las penas de inhabilitación especial, en su mitad superior y, además, la de multa de seis a dieciocho meses.

###### Sección 3.ª De los delitos cometidos por los funcionarios públicos contra otros

derechos individuales

###### Artículo 537.

La autoridad o funcionario público que impida u obstaculice el derecho a la asistencia de abogado al detenido o preso, procure o favorezca la renuncia del mismo a dicha asistencia o no le informe de forma inmediata y de modo que le sea comprensible de sus derechos y de las razones de su detención, será castigado con la pena de multa de cuatro a diez meses e inhabilitación especial para empleo o cargo público de dos a cuatro años.

###### Artículo 538.

La autoridad o funcionario público que establezca la censura previa o, fuera de los casos permitidos por la Constitución y las Leyes, recoja ediciones de libros o periódicos o suspenda su publicación o la difusión de cualquier emisión radiotelevisiva, incurrirá en la pena de inhabilitación absoluta de seis a diez años.

###### Artículo 539.

The authority or public official who dissolves or suspends in its activities a legally constituted association, without prior judicial resolution, or without legitimate cause prevents it from holding its sessions, shall be punished with the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for eight to twelve years and a fine of six to twelve months.

###### Artículo 540.

The authority or public official who prohibits a peaceful assembly or dissolves it outside the cases expressly permitted by the Laws, shall be punished with the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for four to eight years and a fine of six to nine months.

###### Artículo 541.

The authority or public official who expropriates a person of their property outside the permitted cases and without complying with the legal requirements, shall incur the penalties of special disqualification from public employment or office for one to four years and a fine of six to twelve months.

###### Artículo 542.

The authority or public official who, knowingly, prevents a person from exercising other civic rights recognized by the Constitution and the Laws shall incur the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of one to four years.

##### CAPÍTULO VI

Of outrages to Spain

###### Artículo 543.

The offenses or outrages by word, in writing or by deed to Spain, to its Autonomous Communities or to their symbols or emblems, carried out publicly, shall be punished with a fine of seven to twelve months.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

#### TÍTULO XXII

Offenses against public order

##### CAPÍTULO I

Sedition

###### Artículo 544.

(Repealed)

###### Artículo 545.

(Repealed)

###### Artículo 546.

(Repealed)

###### Artículo 547.

(Repealed)

###### Artículo 548.

(Repealed)

###### Artículo 549.

(Repealed)

##### CAPÍTULO II

Of attacks against authority, its agents and public officials, and of resistance and disobedience

###### Artículo 550.

1. Those who assault or, with serious intimidation or violence, offer serious resistance to the authority, its agents or public officials, or attack them, when they are in the exercise of the functions of their positions or on the occasion thereof, are guilty of an attack.

In all cases, acts committed against teaching or health officials who are in the exercise of the functions of their position, or on the occasion thereof, shall be considered acts of attack.

2. Attacks shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from one to four years and a fine of three to six months if the attack is against an authority and imprisonment from six months to three years in other cases.

3. Notwithstanding the provisions of the preceding paragraph, if the authority against whom the attack is committed is a member of the Government, of the Governing Councils of the Autonomous Communities, of the Congress of Deputies, of the Senate or of the Legislative Assemblies of the Autonomous Communities, of local Corporations, of the General Council of the Judiciary, Magistrate of the Constitutional Court, judge, magistrate or member of the Prosecution Service, the penalty of imprisonment from one to six years and a fine of six to twelve months shall be imposed.

###### Artículo 551.

Penalties one degree higher than those respectively provided in the preceding article shall be imposed whenever the attack is committed:

1.º Using weapons or other dangerous objects.

2.º When the act of violence executed is potentially dangerous to the life of persons or may cause serious injuries. In particular, the following cases are included

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

the throwing of blunt objects or flammable liquids, arson and the use of explosives.

3.º Attacking the authority, its agent or the public official using a motor vehicle.

4.º When the acts are carried out on the occasion of a riot, mutiny or collective incident inside a penitentiary center.

###### Artículo 552.

(Repealed)

###### Artículo 553.

The provocation, conspiracy and proposal for any of the offenses provided in the preceding articles shall be punished with a penalty one or two degrees lower than that of the corresponding offense.

###### Artículo 554.

1. The acts described in articles 550 and 551 shall also be punished with the penalties expressed therein when committed against a member of the Armed Forces who, wearing uniform, was providing a service that had been legally entrusted to him.

2. The same penalties shall be imposed on those who attack, use violence or intimidate persons who come to the aid of the authority, its agents or officials.

3. The penalties of articles 550 and 551 shall also be imposed on those who attack, use violence or seriously intimidate:

- a) Firefighters or members of health personnel or rescue teams who are intervening on the occasion of a disaster, public calamity or emergency situation, with the purpose of preventing them from exercising their functions.
- b) Private security personnel, duly identified, who carry out private security activities in cooperation and under the command of the Security Forces and Corps.

###### Artículo 555.

(Repealed)

###### Artículo 556.

1. Those who, without being included in article 550, seriously resist or disobey the authority or its agents in the exercise of their functions, or private security personnel, duly identified, who carry out private security activities in cooperation and under the command of the Security Forces and Corps, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from three months to one year or a fine of six to eighteen months.

2. Those who fail to show the respect and consideration due to the authority, in the exercise of its functions, shall be punished with a fine of one to three months.

##### CAPÍTULO III

Of public disorders

###### Artículo 557.

1. Those who, acting in a group and with the purpose of attacking public peace, execute acts of violence or intimidation shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to three years:

- a) Against persons or property; or
- b) obstructing public thoroughfares causing a danger to the life or health of persons; or LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA
- c) invading facilities or buildings seriously altering the effective functioning of essential services in those places.
  - 2. The acts described in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from three to five years and special disqualification from public employment or office for the same time when committed by a crowd whose number, organization and purpose are suitable to seriously affect public order. In case the perpetrators are constituted in authority, the penalty of disqualification shall be absolute for a period of six to eight years.
  - 3. The penalties of the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed in their upper half to the participants who carry dangerous instruments or to those who carry out acts of pillage.

These penalties shall be applied one degree higher when firearms are carried.

4. The provocation, conspiracy and proposal for the conducts provided in paragraphs 2 and 3 of this article shall be punished with penalties one or two degrees lower than those respectively provided.

5. Anyone who in a crowded place causes an avalanche, stampede or other similar reaction in the public that puts the life or health of persons in a situation of danger shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years.

6. The penalties indicated in this article shall be imposed without prejudice to those that may correspond to the specific acts of injuries, threats, coercion or damages that have been carried out.

###### Artículo 557 bis.

Those who, acting in a group, invade or occupy, against the will of its holder, the domicile of a public or private legal person, an office, bureau, establishment or premises, even if it is open to the public, and thereby cause a significant disturbance to public peace and its normal activity, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from three to six months or a fine of six to twelve months, unless the acts were already punished with a more severe penalty in another provision of this Code.

###### Artículo 557 ter.

(Deleted)

###### Artículo 558.

Those who seriously disturb the order in the hearing of a court or tribunal, in the public acts of any authority or corporation, in an electoral college, public office or establishment, educational center, or on the occasion of sporting or cultural events, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from three to six months or a fine of six to 12 months. In these cases, the penalty of prohibition from attending places, events or spectacles of the same nature for a period of up to three years beyond the imposed prison sentence may also be imposed.

###### Artículo 559.

(Deleted)

###### Artículo 560.

1. Those who cause damage that interrupts, obstructs or destroys telecommunications lines or installations or postal correspondence shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from one to five years.

2. The same penalty shall be incurred by those who cause damage to railway lines or cause serious damage to railway traffic in any of the forms provided for in article 382.

3. The same penalty shall be imposed on those who damage water, gas or electricity conduits or transmissions for populations, interrupting or seriously altering the supply or service.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Artículo 561.

Whoever falsely affirms or simulates a situation of danger to the community or the occurrence of a disaster as a consequence of which it is necessary to provide assistance to another, and thereby provokes the mobilization of police, assistance or rescue services, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from three months and one day to one year or a fine of three to eighteen months.

##### CAPÍTULO IV

Common provision to the preceding chapters

###### Artículo 562.

In the case that the person who commits any of the offenses stated in the preceding chapters of this Title is constituted as an authority, the penalty of disqualification that is provided in each case shall be replaced by absolute disqualification for a period of ten to fifteen years, unless such circumstance is specifically contemplated in the criminal type in question.

##### CAPÍTULO V

On the possession, trafficking and storage of weapons, ammunition or explosives

###### Artículo 563.

The possession of prohibited weapons and that of those which are the result of substantial modification of the manufacturing characteristics of regulated weapons, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from one to three years.

###### Artículo 564.

1. The possession of regulated firearms, lacking the necessary licenses or permits, shall be punished:

1.º With a penalty of imprisonment from one to two years, if it concerns short weapons.
2.º With a penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year, if it concerns long weapons.

2. The offenses provided for in the preceding number shall be punished, respectively, with penalties of imprisonment from two to three years and from one to two years, when any of the following circumstances concur:

1.ª That the weapons lack factory marks or number, or have them altered or erased.

2.ª That they have been illegally introduced into Spanish territory.

3.ª That they have been transformed, modifying their original characteristics.

###### Artículo 565.

Judges or Courts may reduce by one degree the penalties indicated in the preceding articles, provided that the circumstances of the act and of the offender evidence the lack of intention to use the weapons for illicit purposes.

###### Artículo 566.

1. Those who manufacture, trade or establish deposits of weapons or ammunition not authorized by the laws or the competent authority shall be punished:

1.º If it concerns weapons or ammunition of war or chemical, biological, nuclear or radiological weapons or anti-personnel mines or cluster munitions, with a penalty of imprisonment from five to ten years for promoters and organizers, and with imprisonment from three to five years for those who have cooperated in their formation.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

2.º If it concerns regulated firearms or ammunition for them, with a penalty of imprisonment from two to four years for promoters and organizers, and with imprisonment from six months to two years for those who have cooperated in their formation.

3.º With the same penalties shall be punished, in their respective cases, the trafficking of weapons or ammunition of war or defense, or of chemical, biological, nuclear or radiological weapons or anti-personnel mines or cluster munitions.

2. The penalties contemplated in point 1.º of the preceding paragraph shall be imposed on those who develop or employ chemical, biological, nuclear or radiological weapons or anti-personnel mines or cluster munitions, or initiate military preparations for their use or do not destroy them in violation of international treaties or conventions to which Spain is a party.

###### Artículo 567.

1. The manufacture, trading or possession of any of such weapons is considered a deposit of weapons of war, regardless of their model or class, even when they are in disassembled pieces. The manufacture, trading or possession thereof is considered a deposit of chemical, biological, nuclear or radiological weapons or anti-personnel mines or cluster munitions.

The deposit of weapons, in its trading aspect, comprises both acquisition and disposal.

2. Weapons of war are considered those determined as such in the provisions regulating national defense. Chemical, biological, nuclear or radiological weapons, anti-personnel mines or cluster munitions are considered those determined as such in international treaties or conventions to which Spain is a party.

Development of chemical, biological, nuclear or radiological weapons, anti-personnel mines or cluster munitions is understood as any activity consisting of research or study of a scientific or technical nature aimed at the creation of a new chemical, biological, nuclear or radiological weapon, or anti-personnel mine or cluster munition or the modification of a pre-existing one.

3. The manufacture, trading or collection of five or more of such weapons is considered a deposit of regulated firearms, even when they are in disassembled pieces.

4. With respect to ammunition, Judges and Courts, taking into account the quantity and class thereof, shall declare whether they constitute a deposit for the purposes of this chapter.

###### Artículo 568.

The possession or deposit of explosive, flammable, incendiary or asphyxiating substances or devices, or their components, as well as their manufacture, trafficking or transport, or supply in any form, not authorized by the Laws or the competent authority, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from four to eight years, if it concerns their promoters and organizers, and with a penalty of imprisonment from three to five years for those who have cooperated in their formation.

###### Artículo 569.

The deposits of weapons, ammunition or explosives established in the name or on behalf of an association with criminal purpose, shall determine the judicial declaration of illegality and its consequent dissolution.

###### Article 570.

1. In the cases provided for in this chapter, the penalty of deprivation of the right to possess and carry weapons may be imposed for a period three years longer than the prison sentence imposed.

2. Likewise, if the offender were authorized to manufacture or traffic in any one or more of the substances, weapons and ammunition mentioned therein, he shall suffer, in addition to the penalties indicated, that of special disqualification for the exercise of his industry or trade for a period of 12 to 20 years.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

##### CHAPTER VI

Criminal organizations and groups

###### Article 570 bis.

1. Those who promote, constitute, organize, coordinate or direct a criminal organization shall be punished with a prison sentence of four to eight years if the organization has as its purpose or object the commission of serious crimes, and with a prison sentence of three to six years in other cases; and those who actively participate in the organization, form part of it or cooperate economically or in any other way with it shall be punished with prison sentences of two to five years if it has as its purpose the commission of serious crimes, and with a prison sentence of one to three years in other cases.

For the purposes of this Code, a criminal organization is understood as the grouping formed by more than two persons on a stable basis or for an indefinite period of time, who in a concerted and coordinated manner distribute various tasks or functions with the purpose of committing crimes.

2. The penalties provided for in the preceding number shall be imposed in their upper half when the organization:

- a) is formed by a large number of persons.
- b) has weapons or dangerous instruments.
- c) has advanced technological means of communication or transportation that by their characteristics are especially suitable for facilitating the commission of crimes or the impunity of the guilty parties.

If two or more of such circumstances concur, the penalties shall be imposed in the higher degree.

3. The penalties respectively provided for in this article shall be imposed in their upper half if the crimes were against the life or physical integrity of persons, liberty, sexual freedom and safety, or trafficking in human beings.

###### Article 570 ter.

1. Those who constitute, finance or integrate a criminal group shall be punished:

- a) If the purpose of the group is to commit crimes of those mentioned in paragraph 3 of the preceding article, with a sentence of two to four years of prison if it concerns one or more serious crimes and with one to three years of prison if it concerns less serious crimes.
- b) With a sentence of six months to two years of prison if the purpose of the group is to commit any other serious crime.
- c) With a sentence of three months to one year of prison when it concerns committing one or more less serious crimes not included in paragraph a) or the repeated perpetration of minor crimes.

For the purposes of this Code, a criminal group is understood as the union of more than two persons who, without meeting one or more of the characteristics of the criminal organization defined in the preceding article, have as their purpose or object the concerted perpetration of crimes.

2. The penalties provided for in the preceding number shall be imposed in their upper half when the group:

- a) is formed by a large number of persons.
- b) has weapons or dangerous instruments.
- c) has advanced technological means of communication or transportation that by their characteristics are especially suitable for facilitating the commission of crimes or the impunity of the guilty parties.

If two or more of such circumstances concur, the penalties shall be imposed in the higher degree.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Article 570 quáter.

1. The judges or courts, in the cases provided for in this Chapter and the next, shall order the dissolution of the organization or group and, where applicable, any other of the consequences of articles 33.7 and 129 of this Code.

2. Likewise, those responsible for the conduct described in the two preceding articles shall be imposed, in addition to the penalties provided for therein, special disqualification for all those economic activities or legal transactions related to the activity of the criminal organization or group or with their actions within them, for a period of between six and twenty years in addition to the duration of the penalty of deprivation of liberty imposed where applicable, taking into account proportionally the seriousness of the crime, the number of those committed and the circumstances that concur in the offender.

In any case, when the conduct provided for in said articles is included in another provision of this Code, the provisions of rule 4 of article 8 shall apply.

3. The provisions of this Chapter shall be applicable to any criminal organization or group that carries out any criminally relevant act in Spain, even if they have been constituted, are based or carry out their activity abroad.

4. The judges or courts, reasoning it in the sentence, may impose on the person responsible for any of the crimes provided for in this Chapter the penalty one or two degrees lower, provided that the subject has voluntarily abandoned his criminal activities and has actively collaborated with the authorities or their agents, either to obtain decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other responsible parties or to prevent the action or development of the organizations or groups to which he has belonged, or to prevent the perpetration of a crime that was to be committed within or through said organizations or groups.

##### CHAPTER VII

Terrorist organizations and groups and terrorism crimes

###### Section 1. Terrorist organizations and groups

###### Article 571.

For the purposes of this Code, terrorist organizations or groups shall be considered those groupings that, meeting the characteristics respectively established in the second paragraph of paragraph 1 of article 570 bis and in the second paragraph of paragraph 1 of article 570 ter, have as their purpose or object the commission of any of the crimes defined in the following section.

###### Article 572.

1. Those who promote, constitute, organize or direct a terrorist organization or group shall be punished with prison sentences of eight to fifteen years and absolute disqualification for the duration of the sentence.

2. Those who actively participate in the organization or group, or form part of them, shall be punished with prison sentences of six to twelve years and absolute disqualification for the duration of the sentence.

###### Section 2 On terrorism offenses

###### Article 573.

1. The commission of any serious offense against life or physical integrity, freedom, moral integrity, sexual freedom and safety, property, natural resources or the environment, public health, of catastrophic risk, fire, documentary forgery, against the Crown, assault and possession, trafficking and deposit of weapons, ammunition or explosives, provided for in this Code, and the seizure of aircraft, ships or other means of collective transport or of goods, shall be considered a terrorism offense when carried out with any of the following purposes:

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

1.ª To subvert the constitutional order, or to suppress or seriously destabilize the functioning of the political institutions or the economic or social structures of the State, or to compel public authorities to perform an act or to abstain from doing so. 2.ª To seriously disturb public peace.

3.ª To seriously destabilize the functioning of an international organization. 4.ª To provoke a state of terror in the population or a part thereof.

2. The computer offenses defined in Articles 197 bis and 197 ter and 264 to 264 quater shall likewise be considered terrorism offenses when the acts are committed with any of the purposes referred to in the preceding paragraph.

3. Likewise, the remaining offenses defined in this Chapter shall be considered terrorism offenses.

###### Article 573 bis.

1. The terrorism offenses referred to in paragraph 1 of the preceding article shall be punished with the following penalties:

1.ª With imprisonment for the maximum term provided for in this Code if the death of a person is caused.

2.ª With imprisonment of twenty to twenty-five years when, in cases of kidnapping or unlawful detention, information about the whereabouts of the person is not provided.

3.ª With imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years if an abortion under Article 144 is caused, injuries of those defined in Articles 149, 150, 157 or 158 are produced, the kidnapping of a person, or destruction or fire as provided respectively in Articles 346 and 351.

4.ª With imprisonment of ten to fifteen years if any other injury is caused, or a person is unlawfully detained, threatened or coerced.

5.ª And with the penalty provided for the offense committed in its upper half, it being possible to reach the higher degree, when dealing with any other of the offenses referred to in paragraph 1 of the preceding article.

2. The penalties shall be imposed in their upper half if the acts were committed against the persons mentioned in paragraph 3 of Article 550 or against members of the Security Forces and Corps or the Armed Forces or against public employees who provide service in penitentiary institutions.

3. The terrorism offenses referred to in paragraph 2 of the preceding article shall be punished with the penalty higher in degree than that respectively provided in the corresponding articles.

4. The offense of public disorder provided for in paragraphs 2 and 3 of Article 557, as well as the offense of rebellion, when committed by a terrorist organization or group or individually but with their support, shall be punished with the penalty higher in degree than those provided for such offenses.

###### Article 574.

1. The deposit of weapons or ammunition, the possession or deposit of explosive, flammable, incendiary or asphyxiating substances or devices, or their components, as well as their manufacture, trafficking, transport or supply in any form, and the mere placement or use of such substances or of the appropriate means or devices, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight to fifteen years when the acts are committed with any of the purposes expressed in paragraph 1 of Article 573.

2. The penalty of ten to twenty years of imprisonment shall be imposed when dealing with nuclear, radiological, chemical or biological weapons, substances or devices, or any others of similar destructive power.

3. Those who, with the same purposes indicated in paragraph 1, develop chemical or biological weapons, or seize, possess, transport, provide to others or handle nuclear materials, radioactive elements or materials or equipment producing ionizing radiation shall also be punished with imprisonment of ten to twenty years.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

###### Article 575.

1. Anyone who, for the purpose of training to carry out any of the offenses defined in this Chapter, receives indoctrination or military or combat training, or in techniques for developing chemical or biological weapons, for preparing or manufacturing explosive, flammable, incendiary or asphyxiating substances or devices, or specifically intended to facilitate the commission of any such offenses, shall be punished with imprisonment of two to five years.

2. Anyone who, with the same purpose of training to commit any of the offenses defined in this Chapter, carries out on their own any of the activities provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with the same penalty.

Anyone who, for such purpose, habitually accesses one or more communication services accessible to the public online or content accessible through the internet or an electronic communications service whose contents are directed at or are suitable for inciting incorporation into a terrorist organization or group, or to collaborate with any of them or in their purposes, shall be deemed to commit this offense. The acts shall be deemed committed in Spain when the content is accessed from Spanish territory.

Likewise, anyone who, with the same purpose, acquires or has in their possession documents that are directed at or, by their content, are suitable for inciting incorporation into a terrorist organization or group or to collaborate with any of them or in their purposes shall be deemed to commit this offense.

3. The same penalty shall be imposed on anyone who, for that same purpose, or to collaborate with a terrorist organization or group, or to commit any of the offenses included in this Chapter, travels to or establishes themselves in a foreign territory.

###### Article 576.

1. Anyone who, by any means, directly or indirectly, solicits, acquires, possesses, uses, converts, transmits or carries out any other activity with assets or values of any kind with the intention that they be used, or knowing that they will be used, in whole or in part, to commit any of the offenses included in this Chapter shall be punished with imprisonment of five to ten years and a fine of three to five times their value.

2. If the assets or values were effectively placed at the disposal of the person responsible for the terrorism offense, the penalty higher in degree may be imposed. If they were actually used for the execution of specific terrorist acts, the act shall be punished as co-perpetration or complicity, as the case may be.

3. In the event that the conduct referred to in paragraph 1 has been carried out by attacking property, committing extortion, documentary forgery or through the commission of any other offense, these shall be punished with the penalty higher in degree than that which corresponds to them, without prejudice to also imposing the one that applies in accordance with the preceding paragraphs.

4. Anyone who, being specifically subject by law to collaborate with the authorities in the prevention of terrorism financing activities, gives rise, through serious negligence in the fulfillment of such obligations, to the failure to detect or prevent any of the conducts described in paragraph 1 shall be punished with the penalty lower by one or two degrees than that provided for therein.

###### Artículo 577.

1. Anyone who carries out, solicits or facilitates any act of collaboration with the activities or purposes of a terrorist organization, group or element, or to commit any of the offenses included in this Chapter, shall be punished with imprisonment of five to ten years and a fine of eighteen to twenty-four months.

In particular, acts of collaboration include the gathering of information or surveillance of persons, property or facilities, the construction, fitting out, transfer or use of accommodations or deposits, the concealment, sheltering or transportation of persons, the organization of training practices or attendance at them, the provision of technological services, and any other equivalent form of cooperation or assistance to the activities of the terrorist organizations or groups, groups or persons referred to in the preceding paragraph.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

When the information gathering or surveillance of persons mentioned in the preceding paragraph endangers the life, physical integrity, freedom or property of such persons, the penalty provided for in this section shall be imposed in its upper half. If injury to any of these legal interests occurs, the act shall be punished as joint authorship or complicity, as the case may be.

2. The penalties provided for in the preceding section shall be imposed on those who carry out any activity of recruitment, indoctrination or training, which is directed at or which, by its content, is suitable for inciting incorporation into a terrorist organization or group, or to commit any of the offenses included in this Chapter. Likewise, these penalties shall be imposed on those who provide training or instruction on the manufacture or use of explosives, firearms or other weapons or harmful or dangerous substances, or on methods or techniques especially suitable for the commission of any of the offenses of Article 573, with the intention or knowledge that they will be used for such purpose.

The penalties shall be imposed in their upper half, and may reach the higher degree, when the acts provided for in this section have been directed at minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection or at women victims of trafficking for the purpose of converting them into spouses, partners or sexual slaves of the perpetrators of the crime, without prejudice to imposing those additionally applicable for sexual freedom offenses committed.

3. If collaboration with the activities or purposes of a terrorist organization or group, or in the commission of any of the offenses included in this Chapter, has occurred through gross negligence, the penalty of imprisonment of six to eighteen months and a fine of six to twelve months shall be imposed.

###### Artículo 578.

1. The public glorification or justification of the offenses included in Articles 572 to 577 or of those who have participated in their execution, or the performance of acts that involve discredit, contempt or humiliation of the victims of terrorist offenses or their families, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to three years and a fine of twelve to eighteen months. The judge may also order in the sentence, for the period of time that he himself determines, one or more of the prohibitions provided for in Article 57.

2. The penalties provided for in the preceding section shall be imposed in their upper half when the acts have been carried out through the dissemination of services or content accessible to the public through media, the internet, or by means of electronic communications services or through the use of information technologies.

3. When the acts, in view of their circumstances, are suitable for seriously disturbing public peace or creating a serious feeling of insecurity or fear in society or part of it, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half, which may be raised to the higher degree.

4. The judge or court shall order the destruction, deletion or rendering unusable of the books, files, documents, articles or any other medium through which the offense was committed. When the offense has been committed through information and communication technologies, the removal of the contents shall be ordered.

If the acts have been committed through services or content accessible through the internet or electronic communications services, the judge or court may order the removal of the illicit content or services. Subsidiarily, it may order hosting service providers to remove the illicit content, search engines to delete links pointing to them and electronic communications service providers to prevent access to the illicit content or services whenever one of the following circumstances exists:

- a) When the measure is proportionate to the seriousness of the acts and the relevance of the information and necessary to prevent its dissemination.
- b) When the content referred to in the preceding sections is disseminated exclusively or predominantly.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

5. The measures provided for in the preceding section may also be ordered by the investigating judge as a precautionary measure during the investigation of the case.

###### Artículo 579.

1. Anyone who, by any means, publicly disseminates messages or slogans that have as their purpose or that, by their content, are suitable for inciting others to commit any of the offenses of this Chapter, shall be punished with a penalty one or two degrees lower than that provided for the offense in question.

2. The same penalty shall be imposed on anyone who, publicly or before a gathering of persons, incites others to commit any of the offenses of this Chapter, as well as on anyone who requests another person to commit them.

3. The other acts of provocation, conspiracy and proposal to commit any of the offenses regulated in this Chapter shall also be punished with a penalty one or two degrees lower than that corresponding respectively to the acts provided for in this Chapter.

4. In the cases provided for in this provision, the judges or courts may adopt the measures established in sections 4 and 5 of the preceding article.

###### Article 579 bis.

1. The person responsible for the offenses provided for in this Chapter, without prejudice to the penalties that correspond in accordance with the preceding articles, shall also be punished, taking into account proportionally the gravity of the offense, the number of those committed and the circumstances present in the offender, with the penalties of absolute disqualification, special disqualification for educational profession or trade, in the teaching, sports and leisure time spheres, for a time exceeding six to twenty years in addition to the duration of the custodial sentence imposed, if applicable, in the judgment.

2. A person sentenced to a serious custodial sentence for one or more offenses included in this Chapter shall also be imposed the measure of supervised release from five to ten years, and from one to five years if the custodial sentence were less serious. Notwithstanding the foregoing, when it concerns a single offense that is not serious, and its perpetrator had offended for the first time, the court may impose or not impose the measure of supervised release, in view of their lesser dangerousness.

3. In the offenses provided for in this Chapter, the judges and courts, reasoning it in the judgment, may impose the penalty lower by one or two degrees than that established for the offense in question, when the subject has voluntarily abandoned their criminal activities, presents themselves to the authorities confessing the facts in which they have participated and actively collaborates with them to prevent the commission of the offense, or effectively contributes to obtaining decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other responsible parties or to prevent the action or development of organizations, groups or other terrorist elements to which they have belonged or with which they have collaborated.

4. The judges and courts, with reasoning, taking into account the specific circumstances, may also impose the penalty lower by one or two degrees than that established in this Chapter for the offense in question, when the act is objectively of lesser gravity, considering the means employed or the result produced.

###### Article 580.

In all terrorism offenses, the conviction by a foreign judge or court shall be equivalent to the judgments of Spanish judges or courts for the purposes of applying the aggravating circumstance of recidivism.

###### Article 580 bis.

When in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the offenses set forth in this Chapter, the following penalties shall be imposed:

- a) Fine of two to five years, or double to quadruple the damage caused when the resulting amount is higher, if the offense committed by the natural person has a penalty of more than two years of deprivation of liberty.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

b) Fine of six months to two years, or double to triple the damage caused if the resulting amount is higher, in the remaining cases.

Taking into account the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set forth in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

#### TITLE XXIII

Of the offenses of treason and against the peace or independence of the State and relating to National Defense

##### CHAPTER I

Offenses of treason

###### Article 581.

The Spanish national who induces a foreign power to declare war on Spain or conspires with it for the same purpose, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years.

###### Article 582.

Shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from twelve to twenty years:

1.º The Spanish national who facilitates to the enemy the entry into Spain, the taking of a square, military post, ship or aircraft of the State or depots of supplies or armament.

2.º The Spanish national who seduces or gathers Spanish troops or those in the service of Spain, so that they go over to the enemy ranks or desert their flags while on campaign.

3.º The Spanish national who recruits people or supplies arms or other effective means to make war on Spain, under enemy flags.

###### Article 583.

Shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from twelve to twenty years:

1.º The Spanish national who takes up arms against the Homeland under enemy flags.

The penalty higher in degree shall be imposed on whoever acts as chief or promoter, or has some command, or is constituted in authority.

2.º The Spanish national who supplies to enemy troops funds, arms, vessels, aircraft, supplies or munitions of supplies or armament or other direct and effective means to attack Spain, or favors the progress of enemy arms in a manner not included in the preceding article.

3.º The Spanish national who supplies to the enemy plans of fortresses, buildings or terrain, documents or information that directly lead to the same purpose of attacking Spain or favoring the progress of enemy arms.

4.º The Spanish national who, in time of war, prevents national troops from receiving the aid referred to in number 2.º or the data and information indicated in number 3.º of this article.

###### Article 584.

The Spanish national who, with the purpose of favoring a foreign power, association or international organization, obtains, falsifies, renders useless or reveals information classified as reserved or secret, capable of harming national security or national defense, shall be punished, as a traitor, with the penalty of imprisonment from six to twelve years.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Article 585.

The provocation, conspiracy and proposal for any of the offenses provided for in the preceding articles of this chapter, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment lower by one or two degrees than that of the corresponding offense.

###### Article 586.

The foreigner residing in Spain who commits any of the offenses included in this chapter shall be punished with the penalty lower in degree than that established for them, except as established by Treaties or by the Law of nations concerning diplomatic, consular and international organization officials.

###### Article 587.

The penalties established in the preceding articles of this chapter are applicable to those who commit the offenses included therein against a power allied with Spain, in the event of being on campaign against the common enemy.

###### Article 588.

Members of the Government who, without complying with the provisions of the Constitution, declare war or sign peace shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years.

##### CHAPTER II

Offenses that compromise the peace or independence of the State

###### Article 589.

Whoever publishes or executes in Spain any order, provision or document of a foreign Government that threatens the independence or security of the State, opposes the observance of its Laws or provokes their breach, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from one to three years.

###### Article 590.

1. Whoever, through illegal acts or acts that are not duly authorized, provokes or gives cause for a declaration of war against Spain by another power, or exposes Spanish nationals to suffer vexations or reprisals in their persons or property, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from eight to fifteen years if they are an authority or official, and from four to eight if they are not.

2. If war does not come to be declared nor the vexations or reprisals take effect, the next lower penalty shall be imposed, respectively.

###### Article 591.

With the same penalties established in the preceding article, whoever, during a war in which Spain does not intervene, executes any act that compromises the neutrality of the State or infringes the provisions published by the Government to maintain it, shall be punished in their respective cases.

###### Artículo 592.

1. Those who, with the purpose of harming the authority of the State or compromising the dignity or vital interests of Spain, maintain intelligence or relations of any kind with foreign Governments, with their agents or with international or foreign groups, Organizations or Associations, shall be punished with a prison sentence of four to eight years.

2. Whoever carries out the acts referred to in the previous paragraph with the intention of provoking a war or rebellion shall be punished in accordance with articles 581, 473 or 475 of this Code as applicable.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Artículo 593.

A prison sentence of eight to fifteen years shall be imposed on anyone who violates a truce or armistice agreed between the Spanish Nation and another enemy nation, or between their belligerent forces.

###### Artículo 594.

1. A Spanish person who, in time of war, communicates or circulates false news or rumors aimed at harming the credit of the State or the interests of the Nation, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to two years.

2. A foreigner who carries out any of the acts included in the previous paragraph in Spanish territory shall incur the same penalties.

###### Artículo 595.
