Anyone who, without legally granted authorization, raises troops in Spain for the service of a foreign power, whatever the purpose or the Nation they intend to attack, shall be punished with a prison sentence of four to eight years.

###### Artículo 596.

1. Anyone who, in time of war and with the purpose of compromising the peace, security or independence of the State, has correspondence with an enemy country or one occupied by its troops when the Government has prohibited it, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to five years. If the correspondence contains warnings or news that could benefit the enemy, a prison sentence of eight to fifteen years shall be imposed.

2. Anyone who commits the crimes included in this article shall incur the same penalties, even if they direct the correspondence through a friendly or neutral country to evade the Law.

3. If the offender intends to serve the enemy with their warnings or news, they shall be considered as included in number 3.º or number 4.º of article 583.

###### Artículo 597.

A Spanish or foreign person who, being in national territory, passes or attempts to pass to an enemy country when the Government has prohibited it, shall be punished with a fine of six to twelve months.

##### CAPÍTULO III

Discovery and revelation of secrets and information relating to

National Defense

###### Artículo 598.

Anyone who, without the purpose of favoring a foreign power, procures, reveals, falsifies or renders useless information legally classified as reserved or secret, related to national security or national defense or relating to the technical means or systems employed by the Armed Forces or industries of military interest, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to four years.

###### Artículo 599.

The penalty established in the previous article shall be applied in its upper half when any of the following circumstances concur:

1.º That the active subject is a depositary or aware of the secret or information by reason of their position or assignment.

2.º That the revelation consists of giving publicity to the secret or information in any media outlet or in a manner that ensures its dissemination.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Artículo 600.

1. Anyone who without express authorization reproduces plans or documentation referring to zones, installations or military materials that are of restricted access and whose knowledge is protected and reserved by information legally classified as reserved or secret, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years.

2. Anyone who has in their possession objects or information legally classified as reserved or secret, relating to national security or national defense, without complying with the provisions established in current legislation, shall be punished with the same penalty.

###### Artículo 601.

Anyone who, by reason of their position, commission or service, has in their possession or officially knows of objects or information legally classified as reserved or secret or of military interest, relating to national security or national defense, and through gross negligence causes them to become known to an unauthorized person or disclosed, published or rendered useless, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to one year.

###### Artículo 602.

Anyone who discovers, violates, reveals, steals or uses information legally classified as reserved or secret related to nuclear energy, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years, unless the act has a more severe penalty established in another Law.

###### Artículo 603.

Anyone who destroys, renders useless, falsifies or opens without authorization correspondence or documentation legally classified as reserved or secret, related to national defense and which they have in their possession by reason of their position or assignment, shall be punished with a prison sentence of two to five years and special disqualification from employment or public office for a period of three to six years.

###### Artículo 604.

(Without content)

#### TÍTULO XXIV

Crimes against the International Community

##### CAPÍTULO I

Crimes against the Law of Nations

###### Artículo 605.

1. Anyone who kills the Head of a foreign State, or another person internationally protected by a Treaty, who is in Spain, shall be punished with the penalty of reviewable permanent imprisonment.

2. Anyone who causes injuries of those provided for in article 149 to the persons mentioned in the previous paragraph, shall be punished with a prison sentence of fifteen to twenty years.

If it were any of the injuries provided for in article 150, they shall be punished with a prison sentence of eight to fifteen years, and four to eight years if it were any other injury.

3. Any other crime committed against the persons mentioned in the preceding numbers, or against the official premises, private residence or means of transport of such persons, shall be punished with the penalties established in this Code for the respective crimes, in their upper half.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Artículo 606.

1. Anyone who violates the personal immunity of the Head of another State or of another person internationally protected by a Treaty, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years.

2. When the crimes included in this article and in the previous one do not have a reciprocal penalty established in the laws of the country to which the offended persons belong, the offender shall be imposed the penalty that would be appropriate for the crime, in accordance with the provisions of this Code, if the offended person did not have the official character mentioned in the previous paragraph.

##### CAPÍTULO II

Crimes of genocide

###### Artículo 607.

1. Those who, with the purpose of destroying totally or partially a national, ethnic, racial, religious group or one determined by the disability of its members, perpetrate any of the following acts, shall be punished:

1.º With the penalty of reviewable permanent imprisonment, if they kill any of its members. 2.º With the penalty of reviewable permanent imprisonment, if they sexually assault any of its members or cause any of the injuries provided for in article 149.

3.º With a prison sentence of eight to fifteen years, if they subject the group or any of its individuals to conditions of existence that endanger their life or seriously disturb their health, or when they cause them some of the injuries provided for in the

###### Article 150.

4.º With the same penalty, if they carry out forced displacements of the group or its members, adopt any measure that tends to prevent their way of life or reproduction, or forcibly transfer individuals from one group to another.

5.º With imprisonment of four to eight years, if they cause any other injury other than those indicated in numerals 2.º and 3.º of this section.

2. In all cases, the penalty of special disqualification for educational profession or occupation, in the teaching, sports and leisure fields, shall also be imposed for a period of between three and five years longer than the duration of the custodial sentence imposed, where applicable, in the judgment, proportionally considering the severity of the offense and the circumstances present in the offender.

##### CHAPTER II BIS

Of crimes against humanity

###### Article 607 bis.

1. Those who commit the acts provided for in the following section as part of a widespread or systematic attack against the civilian population or against a part thereof are guilty of crimes against humanity.

In any case, the commission of such acts shall be considered a crime against humanity:

1.º By reason of the victim's membership in a group or collective persecuted on political, racial, national, ethnic, cultural, religious, gender, disability or other grounds universally recognized as unacceptable under international law. 2.º In the context of an institutionalized regime of systematic oppression and domination by one racial group over one or more racial groups and with the intention of maintaining that regime.

2. Those guilty of crimes against humanity shall be punished:

1.º With the penalty of reviewable permanent imprisonment if they cause the death of any person.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

2.º With imprisonment of 12 to 15 years if they commit rape, and four to six years' imprisonment if the act consists of any other sexual assault.

3.º With imprisonment of 12 to 15 years if they cause any of the injuries of

###### Article 149, and with eight to 12 years' imprisonment if they subject persons to

conditions of existence that endanger their life or seriously disturb their health or when they cause any of the injuries provided for in Article 150. The penalty of imprisonment of four to eight years shall be applied if they commit any of the injuries of Article 147. 4.º With imprisonment of eight to 12 years if they deport or forcibly transfer, without grounds authorized by international law, one or more persons to another State or place, by means of expulsion or other coercive acts.

5.º With imprisonment of six to eight years if they force the pregnancy of any woman with the intention of modifying the ethnic composition of the population, without prejudice to the penalty that may apply, where applicable, for other offenses.

6.º With imprisonment of twelve to fifteen years for the enforced disappearance of persons. Enforced disappearance shall be understood as the apprehension, detention or abduction or any other form of deprivation of liberty by agents of the State or by persons or groups of persons acting with the authorization, support or acquiescence of the State, followed by a refusal to acknowledge such deprivation of liberty or the concealment of the fate or whereabouts of the disappeared person, removing them from the protection of the law. 7.º With imprisonment of eight to 12 years if they detain another person, depriving them of their liberty, in violation of international rules on detention.

The penalty one degree lower shall be imposed when the detention lasts less than fifteen days. 8.º With imprisonment of four to eight years if they commit serious torture on persons under their custody or control, and with imprisonment of two to six years if it is less serious.

For the purposes of this article, torture means subjecting a person to physical or psychological suffering.

The penalty provided for in this number shall be imposed without prejudice to the penalties that may apply, where applicable, for attacks on other rights of the victim. 9.º With imprisonment of four to eight years if they commit any of the conducts related to prostitution contained in Article 187.1, and with six to eight years in the cases provided for in Article 188.1.

The penalty of six to eight years shall be imposed on those who transfer persons from one place to another, for the purpose of their sexual exploitation, using violence, intimidation or deception, or taking advantage of a situation of superiority or necessity or vulnerability of the victim.

When the conducts provided for in the preceding paragraph and in Article 188.1 are committed against minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection, the penalties one degree higher shall be imposed.

10.º With imprisonment of four to eight years if they subject any person to slavery or maintain them in it. This penalty shall be applied without prejudice to those that may apply, where applicable, for the specific attacks committed against the rights of persons.

Slavery shall be understood as the condition of a person over whom another exercises, even de facto, all or some of the attributes of the right of ownership, such as buying, selling, lending or bartering them.

3. In all cases provided for in the preceding section, the penalty of special disqualification for educational profession or occupation, in the teaching, sports and leisure fields, shall also be imposed for a period of between three and five years longer than the duration of the custodial sentence imposed, where applicable, in the judgment, proportionally considering the severity of the offense and the circumstances present in the offender.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

##### CHAPTER III

Of crimes against persons and property protected in case of armed conflict

###### Article 608.

For the purposes of this chapter, protected persons shall be understood as:

1.º The wounded, sick or shipwrecked and medical or religious personnel, protected by the I and II Geneva Conventions of 12 August 1949 or by Additional Protocol I of 8 June 1977.

2.º Prisoners of war protected by the III Geneva Convention of 12 August 1949 or by Additional Protocol I of 8 June 1977.

3.º The civilian population and civilian persons protected by the IV Geneva Convention of 12 August 1949 or by Additional Protocol I of 8 June 1977.

4.º Persons hors de combat and personnel of the Protecting Power and its substitute protected by the Geneva Conventions of 12 August 1949 or by Additional Protocol I of 8 June 1977.

5.º Parliamentarians and the persons accompanying them, protected by the II Hague Convention of 29 July 1899.

6.º United Nations personnel and associated personnel, protected by the Convention on the Safety of United Nations Personnel and Associated Personnel, of 9 December 1994.

7.º Any other person having that status by virtue of Additional Protocol II of 8 June 1977 or any other international Treaties to which Spain is a party.

###### Article 609.

Whoever, on the occasion of an armed conflict, physically mistreats or seriously endangers the life, health or integrity of any protected person, subjects them to torture or inhuman treatment, including biological experiments, causes them great suffering or subjects them to any medical act that is not indicated by their state of health nor in accordance with generally recognized medical standards that the Party responsible for the action would apply, in analogous medical circumstances, to its own nationals not deprived of liberty, shall be punished with imprisonment of four to eight years, without prejudice to the penalty that may apply for the injurious results produced.

###### Article 610.

Anyone who, on the occasion of an armed conflict, employs or orders the employment of methods or means of combat that are prohibited or intended to cause unnecessary suffering or superfluous injury, as well as those conceived to cause, or which may clearly be expected to cause, widespread, long-term and severe damage to the natural environment, compromising the health or survival of the population, or orders that no quarter be given, shall be punished with imprisonment from 10 to 15 years, without prejudice to the penalty corresponding to the results produced.

###### Article 611.

Anyone who, on the occasion of an armed conflict, shall be punished with imprisonment from ten to fifteen years, without prejudice to the penalty corresponding to the results produced: 1.º Carries out or orders indiscriminate or excessive attacks or makes the civilian population the object of attacks, reprisals or acts or threats of violence the primary purpose of which is to terrorize it.

2.º Destroys or damages, in violation of the rules of international law applicable in armed conflicts, a non-military vessel or aircraft of an adverse or neutral Party, unnecessarily and without giving time or without adopting the necessary measures to provide for the safety of persons and the preservation of documentation on board.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

3.º Forces a prisoner of war or civilian to serve, in any form, in the Armed Forces of the adverse Party, or deprives them of their right to be tried regularly and impartially.

4.º Deports, forcibly transfers, takes as hostage or illegally detains or confines any protected person or uses them to render certain points, areas or military forces immune from attack by the adverse party.

5.º Transfers and settles, directly or indirectly, in occupied territory the population of the occupying party, so that it may reside there permanently.

6.º Carries out, orders or maintains, with respect to any protected person, practices of racial segregation and other inhuman and degrading practices based on other distinctions of an adverse character, which constitute an outrage upon personal dignity.

7.º Unjustifiably prevents or delays the release or repatriation of prisoners of war or of civilians.

8.º Declares abolished, suspended or inadmissible before a Judge or Court the rights and actions of nationals of the adverse party.

9.º Commits offences against the sexual freedom of a protected person by committing acts of rape, sexual slavery, induced or forced prostitution, forced pregnancy, forced sterilization or any other form of sexual assault.

###### Article 612.

Anyone who, on the occasion of an armed conflict, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to seven years, without prejudice to the penalty corresponding to the results produced: 1.º Knowingly violates the protection due to hospitals, installations, material, units and means of sanitary transport, prisoner camps, sanitary and safety zones and localities, neutralized zones, places of internment of the civilian population, undefended localities and demilitarized zones, made known by the appropriate distinctive signs or signals.

2.º Exercises violence against sanitary or religious personnel or members of the medical mission, or of relief societies or against personnel authorized to use the distinctive signs or signals of the Geneva Conventions, in accordance with international law.

3.º Gravely insults, deprives or fails to provide indispensable food or necessary medical assistance to any protected person or subjects them to humiliating or degrading treatment, fails to inform them, without justified delay and in an understandable manner, of their situation, imposes collective punishments for individual acts or violates the provisions on the accommodation of women and families or on special protection of women and children established in international treaties to which Spain is a party and, in particular, recruits or enlists persons under eighteen years of age or uses them to participate directly in hostilities.

4.º Improperly uses the protective or distinctive signs, emblems or signals established and recognized in international treaties to which Spain is a party, especially the distinctive signs of the Red Cross, the Red Crescent and the Red Crystal.

5.º Improperly or perfidiously uses the flag, uniform, insignia or distinctive emblem of neutral States, of the United Nations or of other States that are not parties to the conflict or of adverse Parties, during attacks or to cover, favour, protect or obstruct military operations, except in cases expressly excepted provided for in international Treaties to which Spain is a party.

6.º Improperly or perfidiously uses a flag of truce or of surrender, commits offences against the inviolability or unduly detains a parliamentarian or any of the persons accompanying him, personnel of the Protecting Power or its substitute, or a member of the International Commission of Inquiry.

7.º Despoils a corpse, wounded, sick, shipwrecked, prisoner of war or interned civilian of their effects.

8.º Intentionally causes starvation of the civilian population as a method of warfare, depriving it of goods indispensable to its survival, including the act of

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

arbitrarily obstructing relief supplies, carried out in accordance with the Geneva Conventions and their Additional Protocols.

9.º Violates suspension of arms, armistice, capitulation or other agreement concluded with the adverse party.

10.º Intentionally directs attacks against any member of United Nations personnel, associated personnel or participant in a peacekeeping or humanitarian assistance mission, in accordance with the Charter of the United Nations, provided they are entitled to the protection given to civilians or civilian property, under the international law of armed conflicts, or threatens them with such an attack to compel a natural or legal person to perform or refrain from performing some act.

###### Article 613.

1. Whoever, on the occasion of an armed conflict, carries out or orders any of the following actions shall be punished with imprisonment of four to six years:

- a) Attacks or makes the object of reprisals or acts of hostility against cultural property or places of worship that constitute the cultural or spiritual heritage of peoples, provided that such property or places are not located in the immediate vicinity of a military objective or are not used in support of the adversary's military effort and are properly marked;
- b) Improperly uses the cultural property or places of worship referred to in letter a) in support of a military action;
- c) Appropriates on a large scale, steals, plunders or commits acts of vandalism against the cultural property or places of worship referred to in letter a);
- d) Attacks or makes the object of reprisals or acts of hostility civilian property of the adverse party, causing its destruction, provided that this does not offer, in the circumstances of the case, a definite military advantage or that such property does not effectively contribute to the military action of the adversary;
- e) Attacks, destroys, removes or renders useless property indispensable to the survival of the civilian population, unless the adverse party uses such property in direct support of a military action or exclusively as a means of subsistence for the members of its armed forces;
- f) Attacks or makes the object of reprisals works or installations containing dangerous forces, when such attacks may produce the release of those forces and cause, consequently, significant losses in the civilian population, unless such works or installations are used in regular, important and direct support of military operations and such attacks are the only feasible means of ending such support;
- g) Destroys, damages or seizes, without military necessity, things that do not belong to him, compels another to hand them over or commits any other acts of pillage;
- h) Requisitions, improperly or unnecessarily, movable or immovable property in occupied territory or destroys non-military ship or aircraft, and its cargo, of an adverse or neutral party or captures them, in violation of international rules applicable to armed conflicts at sea;
- i) Attacks or commits acts of hostility against the installations, material, units, private residence or vehicles of any member of the personnel referred to in item 10 of Article 612 or threatens with such attacks or acts of hostility to compel a natural or legal person to perform or refrain from performing any act.
  - 2. When the attack, reprisal, act of hostility or improper use has as its object cultural property or places of worship under special protection or to which protection has been conferred by virtue of special agreements, or immovable cultural property or places of worship under enhanced protection or their immediate surroundings, the penalty may be increased by one degree.

In the other cases provided for in the preceding paragraph of this article, the penalty may be increased by one degree when extensive and significant destruction is caused to the property, works or installations on which they fall or in cases of extreme gravity.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Article 614.

Whoever, on the occasion of an armed conflict, commits or orders any other infringements or acts contrary to the prescriptions of international treaties to which Spain is a party and relating to the conduct of hostilities, regulation of means and methods of combat, protection of the wounded, sick and shipwrecked, treatment due to prisoners of war, protection of civilian persons and protection of cultural property in case of armed conflict, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to two years.

###### Article 614 bis.

When any of the conducts contained in this chapter form part of a plan or policy or are committed on a large scale, the respective penalties shall be applied in their upper half.

##### CHAPTER IV

Common provisions

###### Article 615.

Incitement, conspiracy and proposal for the execution of the offenses provided for in the preceding chapters of this Title shall be punished with a penalty one or two degrees lower than that which would correspond to them.

###### Article 615 bis.

1. The authority or military chief or whoever effectively acts as such who does not adopt the measures within his reach to prevent the commission, by the forces under his command or effective control, of any of the offenses included in Chapters II, II bis and III of this title, shall be punished with the same penalty as the perpetrators.

2. If the preceding conduct is committed through gross negligence, the penalty shall be one or two degrees lower.

3. The authority or military chief or whoever effectively acts as such who does not adopt the measures within his reach to ensure that the offenses included in Chapters II, II bis and III of this title committed by persons under his command or effective control are prosecuted shall be punished with a penalty two degrees lower than that of the perpetrators.

4. The superior not included in the preceding paragraphs who, within the scope of his competence, does not adopt the measures within his reach to prevent the commission by his subordinates of any of the offenses included in Chapters II, II bis and III of this title shall be punished with the same penalty as the perpetrators.

5. The superior who does not adopt the measures within his reach to ensure that the offenses included in Chapters II, II bis and III of this title committed by his subordinates are prosecuted shall be punished with a penalty two degrees lower than that of the perpetrators.

6. The official or authority who, without incurring the conducts provided for in the preceding paragraphs, and failing the duty of his office, fails to promote the prosecution of any of the offenses included in Chapters II, II bis and III of this title of which he has knowledge shall be punished with special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of two to six years.

###### Article 616.

In the case of committing any of the offenses included in the preceding Chapters of this Title, except those provided for in Article 614 and in paragraphs 2 and 6 of Article 615 bis, and in the preceding Title by an authority or public official, he shall be imposed, in addition to the penalties indicated therein, that of absolute disqualification for a period of ten to twenty years; if he is a private individual, the judges and courts may impose on him special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of one to ten years.

LEGISLACIÓN CONSOLIDADA

###### Article 616 bis.

The provisions of Article 20.7 of this Code shall in no case be applicable to those who comply with orders to commit or participate in the acts included in Chapters II and II bis of this title.

##### CHAPTER V

Offense of piracy

###### Article 616 ter.

Whoever with violence, intimidation or deceit, seizes, damages or destroys an aircraft, ship or other type of vessel or platform at sea, or attacks persons, cargo or property found on board them, shall be punished as guilty of the offense of piracy with imprisonment of ten to fifteen years.

In any case, the penalty provided for in this article shall be imposed without prejudice to those corresponding to the offenses committed.

###### Article 616 quáter.

1. Whoever, on the occasion of the prevention or prosecution of the acts provided for in the preceding article, resists or disobeys a warship or military aircraft or other vessel or aircraft bearing clear signs and identifiable as a vessel or aircraft in the service of the Spanish State and authorized for such purpose, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to three years.

2. If force or violence is used in the aforementioned conduct, the penalty of ten to fifteen years of imprisonment shall be imposed.

3. In any case, the penalties provided for in this article shall be imposed without prejudice to those corresponding to the offences committed.

#### TITLE V

Common provisions for misdemeanors

Articles 638 and 639.

(Repealed)

First additional provision.

When a person is declared exempt from criminal liability due to the occurrence of any of the causes provided for in numbers 1 and 3 of article 20, the Public Prosecutor shall assess, taking into account the circumstances of the case, the appropriateness of initiating proceedings for the judicial adoption of support measures for the person with disability or, in the event that such measures had already been previously adopted, for their review.

Second additional provision.

When the governmental authority becomes aware of the existence of a minor or a person with disability in need of special protection who is in a state of prostitution, whether or not by their own will, but with the consent of the persons exercising family or ethico-social authority over them or de facto, or who lacks such persons, or when such persons have abandoned them and do not take charge of their custody, it shall immediately notify the public entity that has been entrusted with the protection of minors in the respective territory and the Public Prosecutor, so that they may act in accordance with their respective competences. Likewise, in cases where the Judge or Court orders the special disqualification for the exercise of parental authority, foster care, guardianship, tutorship or curatorship, or the deprivation of parental authority, it shall immediately notify the public entity that has been entrusted with the protection of minors in the respective territory and the Public Prosecutor so that they may act in accordance with their respective competences.

Third additional provision.

When, following a complaint or claim by the injured party, criminal proceedings are initiated for acts constituting offenses provided for and punishable under articles 267 and 621 of this Code, all other persons involved in the same acts who consider themselves injured may appear in the criminal proceedings initiated and become parties thereto, regardless of the amount of damages they claim.

First transitional provision.

Offenses and misdemeanors committed until the date of entry into force of this Code shall be tried in accordance with the legal body and other special criminal laws that are repealed. Once this Code enters into force, if the provisions thereof are more favorable to the accused, these shall be applied.

Second transitional provision.

For the determination of which law is more favorable, the penalty that would correspond to the adjudicated act with the application of the complete provisions of one or the other Code shall be taken into account. The provisions on redemption of sentences through work shall only apply to all persons convicted under the repealed Code and those to whom the provisions of the new Code apply shall not be able to benefit from them.

In any case, the accused shall be heard.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

Third transitional provision.

The Directors of penitentiary establishments shall submit with the utmost urgency, following the publication of the new Criminal Code, to the Judges or Courts that are hearing the execution, a list of convicted inmates in the Center they direct, and a provisional liquidation of the sentence being served, indicating the days that the convict has redeemed through work and those that may be redeemed, as applicable, in the future in accordance with article 100 of the Criminal Code that is repealed and complementary provisions.

Fourth transitional provision.

The Judges or Courts mentioned in the previous provision shall proceed, once the previous sentence liquidation has been received, to refer the matter to the Public Prosecutor, so that it may report on whether it is appropriate to review the sentence and, in such case, the terms of the review. Once the Prosecutor has reported, they shall also proceed to hear the accused, notifying them of the terms of the proposed review, as well as to refer the matter to the Lawyer who assumed their defense at the oral trial, so that they may set forth what they deem most favorable for the accused.

Fifth transitional provision.

The General Council of the Judiciary, within the scope of the competences attributed to it by article 98 of the Organic Law of the Judicial Power, may assign to one or several of the Criminal Courts or Sections of the Provincial Courts dedicated exclusively to the execution of criminal sentences, the review of final sentences issued before this Code came into force.

Said Judges or Courts shall proceed to review final sentences in which the convicted person is effectively serving the sentence, applying the most favorable provision considered strictly and not through the exercise of judicial discretion. In custodial sentences, this Code shall not be considered more favorable when the duration of the previous sentence imposed on the act with its circumstances is also imposable under the new Code. The case in which this Code contains for the same act the alternative provision of a non-custodial sentence is excepted; in such case the sentence must be reviewed.

Sentences in which the serving of the sentence is suspended shall not be reviewed, without prejudice to doing so in the event that the suspension is revoked and before proceeding to the effective serving of the suspended sentence. The same rule shall apply if the convicted person is in a period of conditional release.

Nor shall sentences be reviewed in which, under the repealed Code and the new one, exclusively a fine penalty corresponds.

Sixth transitional provision.

Sentences in which the penalty has been executed or suspended shall not be reviewed, even if other pronouncements of the judgment are pending execution, as well as those already fully executed, without prejudice to the fact that the Judge or Court that in the future might take them into account for the purposes of recidivism must previously examine whether the act punished therein has ceased to be an offense or could correspond to a lesser penalty than that imposed under this Code.

In cases of partial pardon, sentences shall not be reviewed when the resulting penalty being served by the convicted person falls within a lower imposable range with respect to the new Code.

Seventh transitional provision.

For purposes of the assessment of the aggravating circumstance of recidivism, there shall be understood to be included in the same Title of this Code, those offenses provided for in the legal body that is repealed and that have an analogous name and attack in the same way the same legal interest.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

Eighth transitional provision.

In cases where the penalty that could correspond by application of this Code would be weekend arrest, it shall be considered, to assess its comparative severity, that the duration of the deprivation of liberty is equivalent to two days for each weekend that should be imposed. If the penalty were a fine, it shall be considered that each day of substitute arrest that has been imposed or could be imposed by the Judge or Court under the Code being repealed, is equivalent to two daily quotas of the fine of this legal body.

Ninth transitional provision.

In sentences issued under the legislation being repealed and that are not final because an appeal is pending, the following rules shall be observed, once the vacatio period has elapsed:

- a) If it is an appeal, the parties may invoke and the Judge or Court shall apply ex officio the provisions of the new Code, when they are more favorable to the accused.
- b) If it is an appeal in cassation, not yet formalized, the appellant may point out the legal violations based on the provisions of the new Code.
- c) If, having filed an appeal in cassation, it is being processed, the appellant shall be given again, ex officio or at the request of a party, a period of eight days, so that they may adapt, if deemed appropriate, the grounds for cassation alleged to the provisions of the new Code, and the interested parties, the Prosecutor and the reporting Magistrate shall be instructed on the appeal thus modified, continuing the proceedings according to law.

Tenth transitional provision.

Security measures that are being executed or pending execution, adopted in accordance with the Law on Dangerousness and Social Rehabilitation, or in application of numbers 1 and 3 of article 8 or number 1 of article 9 of the Criminal Code being repealed, shall be reviewed in accordance with the provisions of Title IV of Book I of this Code and the previous rules.

In those cases in which the maximum duration of the measure provided for in this Code is less than the time that those subject to it have effectively served, the Judge or Court shall declare said compliance extinguished and, in the case of an internment measure, shall order their immediate release.

Eleventh transitional provision.

1. When special criminal or procedural Laws are to be applied by the ordinary jurisdiction, the following shall be understood to be substituted:

- a) The penalty of major reclusion, by imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years, with the clause of elevation thereof to the penalty of imprisonment of twenty to twenty-five years when two or more aggravating circumstances concur in the act.
- b) The penalty of minor reclusion, by imprisonment of eight to fifteen years.
- c) The penalty of major imprisonment, by imprisonment of three to eight years.
- d) The penalty of minor imprisonment, by imprisonment of six months to three years.
- e) The penalty of major arrest, by arrest of seven to fifteen weekends.
- f) The penalty of fine imposed in an amount exceeding one hundred thousand pesetas established for acts punished as an offense, by a fine of three to ten months.
- g) The penalty of fine imposed in an amount less than one hundred thousand pesetas established for acts punished as an offense, by a fine of two to three months.
- h) The penalty of fine imposed for criminal acts in an amount proportional to the profit obtained or the damage caused shall continue to be applied proportionally.
- i) The penalty of minor arrest, by arrest of one to six weekends.
- j) The penalty of fine established for acts defined as misdemeanors, by a fine of one to sixty days.
- k) Penalties depriving of rights shall be imposed in the terms and for the periods established in this Code.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

l) Any other penalty of those eliminated in this Code, by the penalty or security measure that the Judge or Court deems most analogous and of equal or lesser severity. If none exists or all are more serious, it shall cease to be imposed.

2. In case of doubt, the accused shall be heard.

Twelfth transitional provision.

(Repealed)

Single repealing provision.

1. The following are repealed:

- a) The consolidated text of the Criminal Code published by Decree 3096/1973, of September 14, in accordance with Law 44/1971, of November 15, with its subsequent modifications, except articles 8.2, 9.3, rule 1 of article 20 insofar as it refers to number 2 of article 8, the second paragraph of article 22, 65 and the first and second additional provisions of Organic Law 3/1989, of June 21.
- b) The Law of March 17, 1908 on conditional sentencing, with its subsequent modifications and complementary provisions.
- c) Law 16/1970, of August 4, on Dangerousness and Social Rehabilitation, with its subsequent modifications and complementary provisions.
- d) The Law of July 26, 1878, on the prohibition of dangerous exercises performed by minors.
- e) The substantive criminal provisions of the following special laws:

Law of September 19, 1896, for the protection of insectivorous birds.

Law of May 16, 1902, on industrial property.

Law of July 23, 1903, on begging by minors.

Law of February 20, 1942, on river fishing.

Law of December 31, 1946, on fishing with explosives.

Law 1/1970, of April 4, on hunting. The offenses and misdemeanors provided for in said Law, not contained in this Code, shall be considered very serious administrative infractions, punishable by a fine of fifty thousand to five hundred thousand pesetas and withdrawal of the hunting license, or the ability to obtain it, for a period of two to five years.

f) The following provisions:

Article 256 of the Penitentiary Regulations, approved by Royal Decree 1201/1981, of May 8.

Articles 65 to 73 of the Regulations of prison services, approved by Decree of February 2, 1956.

Articles 84 to 90 of Law 25/1964, of April 29, on Nuclear Energy.

Article 54 of Law 33/1971, of July 21, on Emigration.

The second paragraph of article 24 of Organic Law 2/1981, of April 6, on the Ombudsman.

Article 2 of Organic Law 8/1984, of December 26, on the regime of remedies in case of conscientious objection and its criminal regime.

Article 4 of Organic Law 5/1984, of May 24, on Appearance before Investigation Committees of the Congress and the Senate or both Chambers.

Articles 29 and 49 of Law 209/1964, of December 24, Criminal and Procedural Law of Air Navigation.

The terms "active and" of article 137 of Organic Law 5/1985, of June 19, on the General Electoral Regime.

Article 6 of Law 57/1968, of July 27, on Receipt of Advanced Amounts in the Construction and Sale of Dwellings.

2. All provisions incompatible with the provisions of this Code are also repealed.

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

First final provision.

The Law of Criminal Procedure shall be modified in the following terms:

«Article 14.

Third. For the hearing and judgment of cases for less serious offenses, as well as misdemeanors, whether or not incidental, attributable to the authors of such offenses or to other persons, when the commission of the misdemeanor or its proof is related to them, the Criminal Court of the district where the offense was committed or the Central Criminal Court within its proper scope.»

«Article 779.

Without prejudice to what is established for other special proceedings, the procedure regulated in this Title shall apply to the prosecution of offenses punishable by custodial penalty not exceeding nine years, or with any other penalties of a different nature, whether single, joint or alternative, whatever their amount or duration.»

Second final provision.

Paragraph 2 of article 1 of Organic Law 5/1995, on the Jury Court, is worded in the following terms:

«2. Within the scope of prosecution provided for in the preceding paragraph, the Jury Court shall be competent for the hearing and judgment of cases for offenses typified in the following provisions of the Criminal Code:

- a) Homicide (articles 138 to 140).
- b) Threats (article 169.1).
- c) Failure to render assistance (articles 195 and 196).
- d) Trespass (articles 202 and 204).
- e) Forest fires (articles 352 to 354).
- f) Infidelity in the custody of documents (articles 413 to 415).
- g) Bribery (articles 419 to 426).
- h) Influence peddling (articles 428 to 430).
- i) Embezzlement of public funds (articles 432 to 434).
- j) Fraud and illegal exactions (articles 436 to 438)
- k) Negotiations prohibited to civil servants (articles 439 and 440).
- l) Infidelity in the custody of prisoners (article 471).»

Third final provision.

1. Chapter VI of Law 35/1988, of November 22, on Assisted Reproduction Techniques, shall be modified in the following terms:

1. Letters a), k), l) and v) of section 2.B) of article 20 are deleted.

2. The text of letter r) of said section 2.B) shall be replaced by the following: «the transfer of gametes or human pre-embryos into the uterus of another animal species or the reverse operation, as well as fertilizations between human and animal gametes that are not authorized».

2. Article 21 of chapter VII of Law 35/1988, on Assisted Reproduction Techniques, shall become article 24.

Fourth final provision.

Organic Law 1/1982, of May 5, on Protection of the Right to Honor, Personal and Family Privacy and Self-Image, shall be modified in the following terms:

CONSOLIDATED LEGISLATION

«Article 1

2. The criminal nature of the intrusion shall not prevent recourse to the judicial protection procedure provided for in article 9 of this Law. In any case, the criteria of this Law shall be applicable for the determination of civil liability arising from an offense.»

«Article 7

7. The imputation of facts or the expression of value judgments through actions or expressions that in any way injure the dignity of another person, undermining their reputation or attacking their self-esteem.»

Fifth final provision.

The second additional provision of Organic Law 6/1995, of June 29, shall be modified in the following terms:

«The exemption from criminal liability contemplated in the second paragraphs of articles 305, paragraph 4; 307, paragraph 3, and 308, paragraph 4, shall be equally applicable even when the debts subject to regularization are less than the amounts established in the aforementioned articles.»

Sixth final provision.

Title V of Book I of this Code, articles 193, 212, 233.3 and 272, as well as the first and second additional provisions, the twelfth transitional provision and the first and third final provisions have the character of ordinary Law.

Seventh final provision.

This Code shall enter into force six months after its complete publication in the "Official State Gazette" and shall apply to all punishable acts committed from its effective date.

Notwithstanding the foregoing, the entry into force of its article 19 is excepted until such time as the law regulating the criminal liability of minors referred to in said provision acquires force.

Therefore, I command all Spaniards, individuals and authorities to keep and enforce this Organic Law.

Madrid, November 23, 1995.

JUAN CARLOS R.

The President of the Government,

FELIPE GONZÁLEZ MÁRQUEZ

This consolidated text has no legal value.
